Ablations and diagnostics show complementary gains from routed injection and CAP, with the largest benefits in ambiguity-prone, partially occluded, and low-data regimes. These findings indicate that adaptive priors are most effective when image evidence underdetermines metric size, while atypical geometry or extreme visibility loss can still cause mismatch between routed priors and true instance geometry. Code, trained models, result logs, and reproducibility material are available at https://github.com/bigggs/MonoPRIO.

</details>


### [135] [Do Composed Image Retrieval Benchmarks Require Multimodal Composition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14787)
*Matteo Attimonelli,Alessandro De Bellis,Aryo Pradipta Gema,Rohit Saxena,Monica Sekoyan,Wai-Chung Kwan,Claudio Pomo,Alessandro Suglia,Dietmar Jannach,Tommaso Di Noia,Pasquale Minervini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现组合图像检索（CIR）基准中大量查询（32.2%-83.6%）可通过单模态捷径解决，而非真正的多模态组合；通过两阶段审核与人实验证，仅有1689/4741个查询是良构的，重新评估表明模型在多模态组合能力上被高估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CIR基准假设强性能需要多模态组合，但该假设未经严格检验，可能导致模型能力被高估。

Method: 两阶段审核：1) 跨模型分析识别捷径可解查询；2) 人工验证捷径无关查询的良构性。在4个CIR基准和11个通用多模态嵌入模型上分析。

Result: 大量查询可通过单模态解决（32.2%-83.6%）；4741个捷径无关查询中仅1689个良构；重新评估后模型准确率下降，但多模态信息依赖增加。

Conclusion: 当前CIR基准混淆了捷径可解、噪声和真正组合查询，导致模型多模态组合能力被系统性高估。

Abstract: Composed Image Retrieval (CIR) is a multimodal retrieval task where a query consists of a reference image and a textual modification, and the goal is to retrieve a target image satisfying both. In principle, strong performance on CIR benchmarks is assumed to require multimodal composition, i.e., combining complementary information from reference image and textual modification. In this work, we show that this assumption does not always hold. Across four widely used CIR benchmarks and eleven Generalist Multimodal Embedding models, a large fraction of queries can be solved using a single modality (from 32.2% to 83.6%), revealing pervasive unimodal shortcuts. Thus, high CIR performance can arise from unimodal signals rather than true multimodal composition. To better understand this issue, we perform a two-stage audit. First, we identify shortcut-solvable queries through cross-model analysis. Second, we conduct human validation on 4,741 shortcut-free queries, of which only 1,689 are well-formed, with common issues including ambiguous edits and mismatched targets. Re-evaluating models on this validated subset reveals qualitatively different behaviour: queries can no longer be solved with a single modality, and successful retrieval requires combining both inputs. While accuracy decreases, reliance on multimodal information increases. Overall, current CIR benchmarks conflate shortcut-solvable, noisy, and genuinely compositional queries, leading to an overestimation of model capability in multimodal composition.

</details>


### [136] [COAL: Counterfactual and Observation-Enhanced Alignment Learning for Discriminative Referring Multi-Object Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14795)
*Shukun Jia,Shiyu Hu,Yipei Wang,Ximeng Cheng,Yichao Cao,Xiaobo Lu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出COAL框架，通过知识正则化解决RMOT中的稀疏语义监督与高判别性需求之间的结构矛盾。具体包括：利用VLM进行显式语义注入以稠密化观察空间，借助LLM推理进行反事实学习以增强监督，并通过层级多流集成架构将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2上取得SOTA，后者HOTA提升7.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RMOT面临高判别性需求与稀疏语义监督之间的根本性结构矛盾，尤其在高度同质化场景中，模型在稀疏监督下容易过拟合于显著但不充分的线索，导致捷径学习和语义坍缩。

Method: 提出COAL框架：1) 显式语义注入(ESI)：通过VLM稠密化观察空间，增强实例判别性；2) 反事实学习(CFL)：利用LLM推理增强监督，实施严格的属性验证以实现鲁棒的组合识别；3) 层级多流集成(HMSI)：将外部知识蒸馏为领域特定的判别性表示。

Result: 在Refer-KITTI和Refer-KITTI-V2基准上验证，在极具挑战性的Refer-KITTI-V2上超过SOTA达7.28% HOTA。

Conclusion: 知识正则化能有效解决RMOT中的稀疏性-判别性悖论，推动RMOT超越孤立的结构优化。

Abstract: Referring Multi-Object Tracking (RMOT) faces a fundamental structural contradiction between the high-discriminability demand and the sparse semantic supervision. This mismatch is particularly acute in highly homogeneous scenarios that require fine-grained discrimination over complex compositional semantics. However, under sparse supervision, models overfit to salient yet insufficient cues, thereby encouraging shortcut learning and semantic collapse. To resolve this, we propose COAL (Counterfactual and Observation-enhanced Alignment Learning), a framework that advances RMOT beyond isolated structural optimization through knowledge regularization. First, we introduce Explicit Semantic Injection (ESI) via a VLM to densify the observation space and enhance instance discriminability. Second, leveraging LLM reasoning, we propose Counterfactual Learning (CFL) to augment supervision, enforcing strict attribute verification for robust compositional recognition. These strategies are unified within a Hierarchical Multi-Stream Integration (HMSI) architecture, which distills external knowledge into domain-specific discriminative representations. Experiments on Refer-KITTI and Refer-KITTI-V2 benchmarks validate COAL's efficacy. Notably, it surpasses the state-of-the-art by 7.28% HOTA on the highly challenging Refer-KITTI-V2. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of knowledge regularization for resolving the sparsity-discriminability paradox in RMOT.

</details>


### [137] [Probing into Camera Control of Video Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14815)
*Chen Hou,Christian Rupprecht*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将视频生成中的相机控制重新定义为位移场（displacement fields）形式的几何引导，通过去噪过程中对潜在特征进行可微重采样来实现，无需训练即可适用于大多数视频扩散模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖额外的相机模块和配对数据来学习相机运动到视频的隐式映射，但此类数据集在规模、多样性和场景动态方面受限，容易使模型产生偏差并损害基础模型已有的强先验。

Method: 将相机控制问题重新定义为位移场，在去噪过程中对潜在特征进行可微重采样，实现对帧间位移的几何引导，无需额外训练即可适用于大多数视频扩散模型。

Result: 该方法在保持多种质量指标的前提下实现了有效的相机控制，性能退化程度优于微调基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法可作为探针工具研究基础模型的相机控制能力，识别了代表性视频模型的通用偏差及其对相机控制的响应差异，并评估了多视角生成性能。

Abstract: Video is a rich and scalable source of 3D/4D visual observations, and camera control is a key capability for video generation models to produce geometrically meaningful content. Existing approaches typically learn a mapping from camera motion to video using additional camera modules and paired data. However, such datasets are often limited in scale, diversity, and scene dynamics, which can bias the model toward a narrow output distribution and compromise the strong prior learned by the base model. These limitations motivate a different perspective on camera control. In this paper, we show that camera control need not be modeled as an implicit mapping problem, but can instead be treated as a form of geometric guidance that induces displacements across frames. Specifically, we reformulate camera control into a set of displacement fields and apply them via differentiable resampling of latent features during denoising. Our simple approach achieves effective camera control with minimal degradation across diverse quality metrics compared to fine-tuned baselines. Since our method is applicable to most video diffusion models without training, it can also serve as a probe to study the camera control capabilities of base models. Using this probe, we identify universal biases shared by representative video models, as well as disparities in their responses to camera control. Finally, we benchmark their performance in multi-view generation, offering insights into their potential for 3D/4D tasks.

</details>


### [138] [The Velocity Deficit: Initial Energy Injection for Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14819)
*Linze Li,Zong-Wei Hong,Shen Zhang,Bo Lin,Jinglun Li,Yao Tang,Jiajun Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现流匹配模型在高维实践中存在“速度赤字”问题，即MSE目标系统性地低估速度幅度，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形（积分滞后）。提出初始能量注入方法：基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）和无训练的缩放调度校正器（SSC）。SSC仅需一行代码零重训即可实现显著加速，在ImageNet-1k上FID提升44.6%（13.68→7.58），实现5倍加速；50步生成器超越250步基线；在MS-COCO上FID提升约22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流匹配理论保证恒速轨迹，但高维实践中MSE目标系统低估速度幅度，导致生成样本无法到达数据流形（积分滞后），需要解决这一速度赤字问题。

Method: 提出初始能量注入策略，包含两种互补方法：基于训练的幅度感知流匹配（MAFM）和无训练的缩放调度校正器（SSC）。两者基于关键发现——速度收缩在轨迹起始导致有害动能停滞，但在末端起到有益去噪作用。

Result: SSC在ImageNet-1k（256x256）上将FID从13.68提升至7.58（提升44.6%），实现5倍加速；50步生成器（FID 7.58）超越250步基线（FID 8.65）；在MS-COCO上FID提升约22%；方法还可泛化至文生图和高分辨率生成任务。

Conclusion: 流匹配高维实践中存在速度赤字和积分滞后问题，通过初始能量注入（MAFM/SSC）可有效解决，其中SSC以零重训、一行代码的极简方式实现显著效率提升和性能改善。

Abstract: While Flow Matching theoretically guarantees constant-velocity trajectories, we identify a critical breakdown in high-dimensional practice: the Velocity Deficit. We show that the MSE objective systematically underestimates velocity magnitude, causing generated samples to fail to reach the data manifold-a phenomenon we term Integration Lag. To rectify this, we propose Initial Energy Injection, instantiated via two complementary methods: the training-based Magnitude-Aware Flow Matching (MAFM) and the training-free Scale Schedule Corrector (SSC). Both are grounded in our discovery of a crucial asymmetry: velocity contraction causes harmful kinetic stagnation at the trajectory's start, yet acts as a beneficial denoising mechanism at its end. Empirically, SSC yields significant efficiency gains with zero retraining and just one line of code. On ImageNet-1k (256x256), it improves FID by 44.6% (from 13.68 to 7.58) and achieves a 5x speedup, enabling a 50-step generator (FID 7.58) to beat a 250-step baseline (FID 8.65). Furthermore, our methods generalize to Text-to-Image tasks and high-resolution generation, improving FID on MS-COCO by ~22%.

</details>


### [139] [SEDiT: Mask-Free Video Subtitle Erasure via One-step Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14894)
*Zheng Hui,Yunlong Bai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SEDiT，一种基于一步扩散Transformer的单阶段视频字幕擦除方法，采用无掩码推理、混合训练策略和分块流式推理，能够高效处理原生1440p无限长度视频，并通过理论证明了一步采样的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频编辑方法通常依赖基于掩码输入的两阶段处理（先分割再修复），分割精度直接影响完成质量，且两阶段流程存在次优性。为解决这些问题，作者提出一种单阶段、无掩码的视频字幕擦除方法。

Method: 1) 提出单阶段无掩码推理框架，直接擦除目标字幕；2) 利用字幕擦除作为局部编辑任务（大部分像素不变、分布偏移小）的特点，采用整流流（rectified flow）下的一步生成；3) 理论证明局部编辑结构下条件最优传输映射及其诱导的整流流速度场关于潜变量是Lipschitz连续的，支持一步采样的可行性；4) 采用混合训练策略，偶尔以干净首帧潜变量为条件，增强长时间时序一致性；5) 直接输入原始视频而非裁剪后处理，避免可见接缝；6) 采用一步和分块流式推理，支持原生1440p无限长度视频处理。

Result: 经验验证了一步去噪的可靠性，并通过理论证明支持一步采样的可行性。方法能够高效处理原生1440p视频且支持无限长度。

Conclusion: SEDiT通过单阶段无掩码设计、理论支撑的一步采样、混合训练策略和分块流式推理，有效解决了视频字幕擦除中的分割依赖、两阶段次优性、时序一致性和高分辨率长视频处理等关键问题。

Abstract: Recent breakthroughs in video diffusion models have significantly accelerated the development of video editing techniques. However, existing methods often rely on inpainting video frames based on masked input, which requires extracting the target video mask in advance, and the precision of the segmentation directly affects the quality of the completion. In this paper, we present SEDiT, a novel one-stage video Subtitle Erasure approach via One-step Diffusion Transformer. We introduce a mask-free inference approach that enables direct erasure of the targeted subtitle. The proposed one-stage framework mitigates the sub-optimality inherent in the two-stage processing of prior models. Since subtitle removal is a localized editing task in which most pixels remain unchanged, the underlying distribution shift is minimal, making it well-suited to one-step generation under rectified flow. We empirically validate the reliability of one-step denoising and further provide a formal theoretical justification. Under the localized-editing structure of subtitle removal, the conditional optimal transport (OT) map and its induced rectified flow velocity field are Lipschitz continuous with respect to the latent variable, which underpins the theoretical feasibility of one-step sampling. To address the challenge of long-term temporal consistency, we adopt a hybrid training strategy by occasionally conditioning the model with a clean first-frame latent. This facilitates temporal continuity, allowing each segment during inference to leverage the output of its predecessor. To avoid visible seams caused by cropping and reinserting processed targets, particularly in scenarios involving substantial motion, we feed the original video directly into SEDiT. Thanks to one-step and chunk-wise streaming inference, our method can efficiently handle native 1440p video with infinite length.

</details>


### [140] [SteerSeg: Attention Steering for Reasoning Video Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14908)
*Ali Cheraghian,Hamidreza Dastmalchi,Abdelwahed Khamis,Morteza Saberi,Aijun An,Lars Petersson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SteerSeg提出了一种轻量级框架，通过可学习软提示和Chain-of-Thought提示来修正LVLM注意力图的错位问题，从而提升视频推理分割中的空间定位能力。该框架冻结LVLM和分割模型参数，仅学习少量软提示，在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练后能泛化到多个基准，显著改善空间定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法利用冻结LVLM的注意力图作为空间先验进行分割，但这些注意力图针对文本生成优化，而非空间定位，导致定位信号弥散模糊。本文旨在解决注意力错位这一关键瓶颈。

Method: 1) 输入级条件控制：通过可学习软提示重塑注意力分布，产生更集中的空间图；2) 推理引导的CoT提示：利用CoT推导的属性消除相似物体间的歧义，引导注意力指向正确实例；3) 将注意力图转换为关键帧的点提示，指导分割模型；4) 基于相关性评分排序和选择候选轨迹。模型冻结LVLM和分割模型参数，仅学习少量软提示。

Result: 仅在Ref-YouTube-VOS上训练，但能泛化到多个不同基准，显著提升了LVLM的空间定位能力。

Conclusion: 通过输入级条件控制从源头引导注意力，是提升LVLM空间定位能力的有效且轻量的方法，保留了模型的预训练推理能力同时改善了定位效果。

Abstract: Video reasoning segmentation requires localizing objects across video frames from natural language expressions, often involving spatial reasoning and implicit references. Recent approaches leverage frozen large vision-language models (LVLMs) by extracting attention maps and using them as spatial priors for segmentation, enabling training-free grounding. However, these attention maps are optimized for text generation rather than spatial localization, often resulting in diffuse and ambiguous grounding signals. In this work, we introduce SteerSeg, a lightweight framework that identifies attention misalignment as the key bottleneck in attention-based grounding and proposes to steer attention at its source through input-level conditioning. SteerSeg combines learnable soft prompts with reasoning-guided Chain-of-Thought (CoT) prompting. The soft prompts reshape the attention distribution to produce more spatially concentrated maps, while CoT-derived attributes resolve ambiguity among similar objects by guiding attention toward the correct instance. The resulting attention maps are converted into point prompts across keyframes to guide a segmentation model, while candidate tracklets are ranked and selected using correlation-based scoring. Our approach freezes the LVLM and segmentation model parameters and learns only a small set of soft prompts, preserving the model's pretrained reasoning capabilities while significantly improving grounding. Despite being trained only on Ref-YouTube-VOS, SteerSeg generalizes well across diverse benchmarks, significantly improving the spatial grounding capability of LVLMs. Project page: https://steerseg.github.io

</details>


### [141] [SceneParser: Hierarchical Scene Parsing for Visual Semantics Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14923)
*Pengxin Xu,Xincheng Lin,Luping Xiao,Qing Jiang,Meishan Zhang,Hao Fei,Shanghang Zhang,Xingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出层级场景解析任务（Hierarchical Scene Parsing），构建了SceneParser模型和SceneParser-Bench基准，将物理场景表示为场景→物体→部件→功能的显式层级结构，并验证了其在交互导向场景理解中的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景感知研究在开放词汇定位、部件定位和功能预测等方面取得了进展，但这些能力通常作为孤立的预测任务实现，缺乏捕获交互导向场景理解所需的结构化依赖关系（即物体、部件、交互点之间的层级绑定关系）。

Method: 提出SceneParser，一个基于VLM的解析器，采用统一层级生成、结构补全伪标签和课程学习进行训练；并构建SceneParser-Bench基准（含110K训练图、777K物体、1.14M部件、1.74M功能注解），引入Level 1-3条件指标和ParseRate评估定位、跨层级绑定和层级完整性。

Result: 实验表明现有MLLM和感知拼接流水线在SceneParser-Bench上的层级解析表现较弱，而SceneParser实现了更强的结构感知性能；消融实验、COCO和AGD20K评估以及下游规划探测进一步验证了SceneParser与传统任务兼容且能为视觉理解提供可操作的表示。

Conclusion: 层级场景解析框架能够有效建模场景中物体、部件与功能之间的结构化依赖关系，SceneParser在该任务上优于现有方法，且可与传统任务兼容，为交互导向的视觉理解提供有力支持。

Abstract: General scene perception has progressed from object recognition toward open-vocabulary grounding, part localization, and affordance prediction. Yet these capabilities are often realized as isolated predictions that localize objects, parts, or interaction points without capturing the structured dependencies needed for interaction-oriented scene understanding. To address this gap, we introduce Hierarchical Scene Parsing, an interaction-oriented parsing task that represents physical scenes as explicit scene -> object -> part -> affordance hierarchies with cross-level bindings. We instantiate this task with SceneParser, a VLM-based parser trained for unified hierarchical generation with structural-completion pseudo labels and curriculum learning. To support training and evaluation, we construct SceneParser-Bench, a large-scale benchmark built with a scalable hierarchical data engine, containing 110K training images, a 5K validation split, 777K objects, 1.14M parts, 1.74M affordance annotations, and 1.74M valid object-part-affordance chain instances. We further introduce Level-1 to Level-3 conditional metrics and ParseRate to evaluate localization, cross-level binding, and hierarchical completeness. Experiments show that existing MLLMs and perception-stitching pipelines struggle with hierarchical parsing on our SceneParser-Bench, while SceneParser achieves stronger structure-aware performance. Besides, ablations, evaluations on COCO and AGD20K, and a downstream planning probe demonstrate that our SceneParser is compatible with conventional tasks and provides an actionable representation for visual understanding.

</details>


### [142] [Road Maps as Free Geometric Priors: Weather-Invariant Drone Geo-Localization with GeoFuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14925)
*Yunsong Fang,Tingyu Wang,Zhedong Zheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoFuse提出一种跨模态融合框架，通过将天气不变的道路地图与卫星图像结合，并采用动态门控机制和类别级跨视角对比学习，显著提升了无人机视角地理定位在恶劣天气下的鲁棒性，在University-1652和DenseUAV基准上分别提升Recall@1达3.46%和23.18%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视角地理定位方法在恶劣天气（雨、雪、雾）下性能严重下降，因为它们依赖视觉外观特征而忽略了道路地图这一低成本、天气不变的结构模态。

Method: 1) 为University-1652和DenseUAV数据集补充地理对齐的道路地图；2) 设计灵活的融合模块，通过token级和通道级交互结合卫星与道路地图特征；3) 采用轻量级动态门控机制自适应调整各模态贡献；4) 使用类别级跨视角对比学习对齐天气退化后的无人机特征与融合后的卫星-道路地图表示。

Result: 在University-1652上Recall@1提升3.46%，在DenseUAV上提升23.18%，在各种天气条件下均一致优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 道路地图作为一种天气不变的结构先验，能够有效弥补恶劣天气下视觉特征的退化，跨模态融合是提升无人机定位鲁棒性的有效途径。

Abstract: Drone-view geo-localization aims to match a query drone image, often captured under adverse weather conditions (e.g., rain, snow, fog), against a gallery of geo-tagged satellite images. Weather-induced degradations in the drone view, such as noise, reduced visibility, and partial occlusions, severely exacerbate the intrinsic cross-view domain gap. While prior methods predominantly rely on weather-specific architectures or data augmentations, they have largely overlooked road map data, a readily available modality that provides strong, inherently weather-invariant geometric layout cues (e.g., road networks and building footprints) at negligible additional cost. We introduce GeoFuse, a cross-modal fusion framework that integrates precisely aligned road map tiles with satellite imagery to yield more discriminative and weather-resilient representations. We first augment the existing University-1652 and DenseUAV benchmarks with geo-aligned road maps, supplying structural priors robust to meteorological variations. Building on this, we propose a flexible fusion module that combines satellite and road map features via token-level and channel-level interactions, with a lightweight dynamic gating mechanism that adaptively weights modality contributions per instance. Finally, we employ class-level cross-view contrastive learning to promote robust alignment between weather-degraded drone features and the fused satellite-roadmap representations. Extensive experiments under diverse weather conditions show that GeoFuse consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, achieving +3.46% and +23.18% Recall@1 accuracy on the University-1652 and DenseUAV benchmarks, respectively.

</details>


### [143] [On the Cultural Anachronism and Temporal Reasoning in Vision Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15071)
*Mukul Ranjan,Prince Jha,Khushboo Kumari,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出"文化时代错位"概念，指VLMs用不合时宜的概念误解历史文物；构建了TAB-VLM基准（600题，覆盖印度1600件文物），测试10个SOTA模型，最佳模型GPT-5.2仅58.7%准确率，揭示视觉AI在非西方文化遗产理解上的显著缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLMs在文化遗产领域（数字档案、教育平台等）应用日益广泛，但现有研究未系统评估模型对历史文物的时间推理能力。作者发现模型倾向于用当下不恰当的概念框架误解历史文物，尤其在非西方视觉文化中表现更差，亟需系统性基准来量化这一问题。

Method: 1) 定义"文化时代错位"现象；2) 构建TAB-VLM基准数据集：包含600道题、6大类别、覆盖印度从史前到现代的1,600件文物；3) 系统评估10个SOTA模型（GPT-5.2等），涵盖不同架构和规模。

Result: 最佳模型GPT-5.2仅58.7%总体准确率，所有模型在该基准上表现显著不足，且性能差距在不同架构和模型规模下持续存在，说明文化时代错位是视觉AI的系统性局限。

Conclusion: 当前VLM能力与文化遗产准确解读需求之间存在巨大差距，尤其在训练数据中代表性不足的非西方视觉文化方面。TAB-VLM基准为提升多模态AI系统的时间认知能力提供了基础。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly applied to cultural heritage materials, from digital archives to educational platforms. This work identifies a fundamental issue in how these models interpret historical artifacts. We define this phenomenon as cultural anachronism, the tendency to misinterpret historical objects using temporally inappropriate concepts, materials, or cultural frameworks. To quantify this phenomenon, we introduce the Temporal Anachronism Benchmark for Vision-Language Models (TAB-VLM), a dataset of 600 questions across six categories, designed to evaluate temporal reasoning on 1,600 Indian cultural artifacts spanning prehistoric to modern periods. Systematic evaluations of ten state-of-the-art models reveal significant deficiencies on our benchmark, and even the best model (GPT-5.2) achieves only 58.7% overall accuracy. The performance gap persists across varying architectures and scales, suggesting that cultural anachronism represents a significant limitation in visual AI systems, regardless of model size. These findings highlight the disparity between current VLM capabilities and the requirements for accurately interpreting cultural heritage materials, particularly for non-Western visual cultures underrepresented in training data. Our benchmark provides a foundation for enhancing temporal cognition in multimodal AI systems that interact with historical artifacts. The dataset and code are available in our project page.

</details>


### [144] [SAGE3D: Soft-guided attention and graph excitation for 3D point cloud corner detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15088)
*Batuhan Arda Bekar,Can Sarı,Hüseyin Can Gülkan,Barış Özcan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SAGE3D是一个基于混合Transformer的模型，用于机载LiDAR点云中的角点检测。它采用分层编码器-解码器架构，通过Set Abstraction层逐步下采样点云，并通过Feature Propagation恢复逐点预测。创新包括：Soft-Guided Attention在训练时将真实角点标签作为对数先验注入注意力logits以提高精度；Excitatory GNN在分层结构的关键分辨率处进行仅正消息传递，高置信度角点通过学习增强来优化召回率。分层设计实现多尺度特征提取，引导注意力和激发模块确保角点信号在跨尺度传播时被放大而非稀释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机载LiDAR点云中的角点检测精度和召回率不足，现有方法难以在跨尺度特征提取中有效放大角点信号而不会被稀释。

Method: 提出SAGE3D混合Transformer模型，采用分层编码器-解码器架构；创新包括Soft-Guided Attention（训练时将真实角点标签作为对数先验注入注意力logits）和Excitatory GNN（在关键分辨率处进行仅正消息传递，高置信度角点通过学习增强优化召回率）。

Result: 引导注意力和激发模块能确保角点信号在跨尺度传播时被放大而非稀释，实现精度与召回率的提升。

Conclusion: SAGE3D通过分层设计与创新的注意力/GNN模块，有效解决了LiDAR点云角点检测中精度与召回率的权衡问题。

Abstract: We present SAGE3D, a hybrid Transformer-based model for corner detection in airborne LiDAR point clouds. We propose a multi-stage solution built on a hierarchical encoder-decoder architecture that progressively downsamples point clouds through Set Abstraction layers and recovers per-point predictions via Feature Propagation. We introduce two innovations: Soft-Guided Attention, which injects ground-truth corner labels as a log-prior into attention logits during training to improve precision; then an Excitatory Graph Neural Network positioned at strategic resolutions in the hierarchy, employing positive-only message passing where high-confidence corners reinforce predictions through learned boosting, optimizing for recall. The hierarchical design enables multi-scale feature extraction while our guided attention and excitatory modules ensure corner signals are amplified rather than diluted across scales.

</details>


### [145] [CoralLite: μCT Reconstruction of Coral Colonies from Individual Corallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15093)
*Jess Jones,Leonardo Bertini,Kenneth Johnson,Erica Hendy,Tilo Burghardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoralLite，一个珊瑚骨骼μCT扫描数据集及首个基于深度学习的珊瑚个体（corallite）三维重建基准方法。方法采用混合V-Trans-UNet架构进行分割，在弱标注数据上预训练并通过拓扑感知微调优化，在相同菌落上达到0.94拓扑准确率和0.77 Dice分数，在无关标本上达到0.63 Dice分数，首次证明视觉机器学习可有效支持基于μCT扫描的个体珊瑚三维建模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学家需要追踪每个珊瑚虫（polyp）沉积的骨骼结构（corallite），以理解珊瑚虫分裂速率、时机及其对菌落骨骼生长的影响。现有方法缺乏对整个钙化骨骼中每个珊瑚虫进行三维重建的能力。

Method: 构建CoralLite数据集（697张μCT切片、37张部分或全标注切片）；提出混合V-Trans-UNet架构用于珊瑚骨骼μCT虚拟切片分割；先在弱标注数据上预训练，再通过完全标注的切片进行拓扑感知微调；实现跨切片链接以生成每个珊瑚虫的三维模型可视化。

Result: 在相同菌落未见切片上达到0.94拓扑准确率和0.77平均Dice分数；在不同生物学无关标本上达到0.63平均Dice分数。

Conclusion: 首次证明视觉机器学习可有效支持基于μCT扫描的单个珊瑚虫三维建模；尽管实验规模和背景有限，结果为未来珊瑚骨骼生长研究提供了可复现的基准和数据集。

Abstract: The life history of an individual coral is archived within the accreting skeleton of the colony. While reef-forming coral colonies (e.g. massive \emph{Porites} sp.) may live for hundreds of years and deposit calcareous structures many metres in height and width, their living tissue is a thin outer surface layer comprised of asexually-dividing polyps that only survive a few years. To understand the rate and timing of polyp division and the consequences for colony skeletal growth, scientists need to track the skeletal corallite deposited around each polyp. Here we propose CoralLite, an annotated μCT scan dataset of entire calcareous skeletons and an associated, first corallite deep learning reconstruction baseline. CoralLite combines fully quantified volumetric segmentations with cross-slice linking for visualisations of 3D models for each corallite up to colony scale. For segmentation, we propose and evaluate in detail a hybrid V-Trans-UNet architecture applicable to segmenting tiled μCT virtual slabs of \emph{Porites} sp. colonies. The model is pre-trained on weakly annotated data and topology-aware fine-tuned using fully annotated slice sections with 8k+ manual corallite region annotations. On unseen slices of the same colony, the resulting model reaches 0.94 topological accuracy at mean Dice scores of 0.77 on the same colony and projection axis, and 0.63 mean Dice scores on a different, biologically unrelated specimen. Whilst our experiments are limited in scale and context, our results show for the first time that visual machine learning can effectively support full 3D individual corallite modelling from μCT scans of coral skeletons alone. For reproducibility and as a baseline for future research we publish our full dataset of 697 μCT slices, 37 partial or full slice annotations, and all network weights and source code with this paper.

</details>


### [146] [DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15116)
*Haonan Zhao,Yiting Wang,Jingkun Chen,Valentina Donzella,Thomas Bashford-Rogers,Kurt Debattista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DriveCtrl是一个基于深度条件控制的仿真到真实视频生成框架，通过结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成，保持场景布局和运动模式，并引入可扩展数据生成管道和评估指标DVRS，有效缩小了驾驶视频的仿真到真实域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模标注驾驶视频数据对自动驾驶系统训练至关重要，但仿真数据与真实世界之间存在显著域差距，限制了其实用性；现有视频生成方法无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时间一致性和视觉真实性。

Method: 提出DriveCtrl框架，包含：(1) 基于预训练视频基础模型的结构感知适配器，实现深度条件控制的仿真到真实视频生成；(2) 可扩展数据生成管道，支持结构深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示三种条件信号；(3) 驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标DVRS。

Result: DriveCtrl在真实性、时间质量和感知任务性能上持续优于基线和竞争方法，显著缩小了仿真到真实的域差距。

Conclusion: DriveCtrl有效实现了仿真到真实驾驶视频的生成，为自动驾驶系统训练提供了高质量、全标注的生成数据。

Abstract: Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.

</details>


### [147] [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Kaiwen Zheng,Zihan Zhou,Bokai Yan,Xinyuan Li,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Causal Forcing++ 框架，通过因果一致性蒸馏（causal CD）实现帧级 1-2 步自回归视频生成，相比现有 4 步块级方法在 VBench、VisionReward 等指标上提升显著，同时降低首帧延迟 50% 和训练成本约 4 倍，并扩展至动作条件世界模型生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自回归扩散蒸馏方法在块级 4 步生成上取得不错效果，但存在响应粒度粗、采样延迟高的问题。更激进的帧级 1-2 步自回归设置下，少步自回归学生的初始化策略成为关键瓶颈：现有策略要么目标不对齐、要么无法支持少步生成、要么扩展成本过高。

Method: 提出 Causal Forcing++ 框架，核心为因果一致性蒸馏（causal CD），学习与因果 ODE 蒸馏相同的 AR 条件流映射，但仅从相邻时间步之间的单步在线教师 ODE 步骤获取监督，无需预计算和存储完整 PF-ODE 轨迹，使得初始化更高效且更易优化。

Result: 在帧级 2 步设置下，VBench Total 提升 0.1，VBench Quality 提升 0.3，VisionReward 提升 0.335；首帧延迟降低 50%，Stage 2 训练成本降低约 4 倍。

Conclusion: Causal Forcing++ 通过因果一致性蒸馏有效解决了帧级少步自回归初始化的瓶颈问题，在视频生成质量和效率上均超越现有方法，并具备扩展到世界模型等更广泛应用场景的潜力。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

</details>


### [148] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索纯合成分层数据在图形设计分解中的有效性，基于CLD基线构建SynLayers合成数据集，实验表明纯合成数据训练可超越真实数据集，性能随数据量提升（约50K样本趋于饱和），且合成数据能避免层数分布不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成技术虽能产出高质量图像，但输出是扁平化的，前景、背景和文本被固定在同一画布上，导致后期灵活编辑困难，存在实用性上的"最后一英里"差距。现有方法要么依赖稀缺的专有分层资产，要么从有限的结构先验中构建部分合成数据，两者都面临可扩展性挑战。本文旨在探究纯合成分层数据能否在不依赖稀缺真实数据的情况下，有效改进图形设计分解任务。

Method: 基于CLD基线（SOTA层分解框架）进行数据为中心的研究。构建纯合成数据集SynLayers，利用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成文本监督信息，并自动化推理输入（VLM预测的边界框）。通过控制训练数据的规模、来源（纯合成 vs. 真实数据PrismLayersPro）以及层数分布，进行系统性对比实验。

Result: (1) 纯合成数据训练可超越非可扩展替代方案（如PrismLayersPro真实数据集）；(2) 性能随训练数据规模持续提升，约在50K样本时趋于饱和；(3) 合成数据能实现层数分布的平衡控制，避免真实数据中常见的层数不平衡问题。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据是图形设计分解任务中可扩展且有效的替代方案，能够克服真实数据稀缺和分布不平衡的局限性，为分层设计编辑系统提供了实用的数据基础。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

</details>


### [149] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScreen是一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，通过双知识库检索区域级历史病例证据，结合证据感知推理模块提升筛查性能，并利用对比检索生成的异常图增强定位可解释性，在真实疾病筛查基准上取得了优于现有方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有疾病筛查模型存在可解释性差和性能欠佳的问题，缺乏有效机制来参考历史病例或提供透明的推理路径。

Method: 提出EviScreen框架，包括：(1) 双知识库检索区域级历史证据实现回溯可解释性；(2) 证据感知推理模块结合当前病例和历史证据进行预测；(3) 基于对比检索的异常图增强定位可解释性，替代传统的后验显著性图。

Result: 在建立的真实疾病筛查基准上取得优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现了显著更高的特异性。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过引入历史病例证据和透明的推理机制，同时提升了疾病筛查的性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

</details>


### [150] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM是一个26亿参数的开源世界模型，支持一分钟高保真720p视频生成，具备精确相机控制能力，在效率上显著优于现有基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在长视频生成（分钟级）方面存在效率低、计算成本高、缺乏精确相机控制等问题，且开源模型与大规模工业基线之间存在质量差距。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：帧级Gated DeltaNet（GDN）与softmax注意力结合，实现长上下文记忆高效建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：精确保持6-DoF轨迹；(3) 两阶段生成流水线：长视频精炼器提升阶段一输出的一致性与质量；(4) 鲁棒标注流水线：从公开视频中提取精确度量级6-DoF相机位姿，生成时空一致的动作标签。

Result: 仅用约21.3万公开视频片段训练，15天在64块H100上完成训练；单GPU生成60秒720p视频；蒸馏变体可在单张RTX 5090上运行，经NVFP4量化后34秒完成60秒720p视频去噪；相比开源基线模型动作跟随精度更高，视觉质量相当但吞吐量提升36倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM在高效率、高质量和精确控制之间取得了良好平衡，为可扩展世界建模提供了有效的开源方案，大幅降低了计算和数据门槛。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

</details>


### [151] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于经验驱动的长时域图像编辑框架，包含规划器（生成结构化原子分解）和协调器（选择工具和区域执行步骤），通过视觉语言评判器提供结果奖励来训练协调器，并利用成功轨迹优化规划器，实现比单步或基于规则的多步方法更连贯、可靠的编辑效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在处理抽象、多步骤指令（如“让这个广告更素食友好”）时效果不佳；基于智能体的方法虽能分解任务，但依赖手工构建的流水线或教师模仿，灵活性受限且学习过程与实际编辑结果脱钩。

Method: 提出经验驱动框架：(1) 规划器生成结构化的原子步骤分解；(2) 协调器为每一步选择工具和编辑区域；(3) 视觉语言评判器基于结果提供指令遵循度和视觉质量的奖励信号；(4) 协调器通过最大化奖励进行训练；(5) 成功的轨迹用于细化规划器。

Result: 该方法在连贯性和可靠性上优于单步基线方法和基于规则的多步基线方法。

Conclusion: 将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，能够产生比单步或基于规则的多步方法更连贯、可靠的编辑结果。

Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

</details>


### [152] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Warp-as-History接口，通过将相机运动产生的扭曲帧作为伪历史输入，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现冻结视频生成模型的零样本相机轨迹控制，轻量LoRA微调可进一步提升效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制视频生成方法通常需要在大规模相机标注视频上进行后训练，或需要测试时优化/额外去噪引导，缺乏一种简单、训练免费且无需额外成本的相机轨迹控制方案。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：根据目标相机轨迹，从过去观测中构建相机扭曲的伪历史帧，将其通过模型的视觉历史通路输入，同时对齐目标帧的位置编码并移除无效的扭曲历史token。无需训练、架构修改或测试时优化。此外，仅需一个相机标注视频的轻量离线LoRA微调即可进一步提升性能。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验证明该方法在零样本设置下能有效跟随相机轨迹，轻量LoRA微调进一步提升了相机遵从度、视觉质量和运动动态，且能泛化到未见视频。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History以极简接口实现了冻结视频生成模型的零样本相机轨迹控制能力，避免了昂贵的后训练和测试时优化，轻量微调能显著提升效果。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [153] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了PDI-Bench，一个用于量化评估生成视频中几何一致性的框架。通过分割和点跟踪获取物体视角观测，将其提升到3D世界坐标，并计算投影几何残差来评估尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个维度。实验表明，PDI能够揭示现有感知指标无法捕捉的几何特定失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成视频评估方法过度依赖人工判断或学习型评分器，这些方法主观性强，且难以诊断几何层面的失败，亟需一个客观、量化的几何一致性审计框架。

Method: 对生成视频进行分割和点跟踪（使用SAM 2、MegaSaM、CoTracker3），通过单目重建将结果提升到3D世界坐标，然后计算投影几何残差，涵盖尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个失败维度。

Result: 在多个最先进的视频生成器上，PDI揭示了一致的几何特定失败模式，这些模式是传统感知指标无法捕捉的，为物理可信的视频生成提供了诊断信号。

Conclusion: PDI-Bench为生成视频的几何一致性提供了有效的量化评估工具，有助于推动物理可信视频生成和物理世界模型的发展。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [154] [Articraft: An Agentic System for Scalable Articulated 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15187)
*Matt Zhou,Ruining Li,Xiaoyang Lyu,Zhaomou Song,Zhening Huang,Chuanxia Zheng,Christian Rupprecht,Andrea Vedaldi,Shangzhe Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Articraft系统，利用大语言模型自动生成3D铰接物体资产，通过将生成问题转化为程序编写问题，并构建了包含10K+资产的数据集，在质量和规模上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前理解铰接3D物体的瓶颈在于缺乏大规模且多样化的数据集，现有数据集规模有限，难以支撑铰接物体理解和生成的研究。

Method: 提出Articraft智能体系统：1) 将铰接3D资产生成问题转化为程序编写问题；2) 设计领域特定SDK，用于定义部件、组合几何、指定关节和编写测试；3) 提供受限工作空间和结构化反馈机制的编程框架，帮助LLM高效生成代码并验证资产质量。

Result: 1) 生成的资产质量优于现有铰接资产生成器和通用编码智能体；2) 构建了Articraft-10K数据集（10K+资产，245个类别）；3) 展示了该数据在铰接资产模型训练、机器人仿真和虚拟现实等下游任务中的实用性。

Conclusion: 利用LLM和程序化生成方法可以有效解决铰接3D资产数据稀缺问题，生成的高质量数据集对铰接物体理解和相关应用具有重要价值。

Abstract: A bottleneck in learning to understand articulated 3D objects is the lack of large and diverse datasets. In this paper, we propose to leverage large language models (LLMs) to close this gap and generate articulated assets at scale. We reduce the problem of generating an articulated 3D asset to that of writing a program that builds it. We then introduce a new agentic system, Articraft, that writes such programs automatically. We design a programmatic interface and harness to help the LLM do so effectively. The LLM writes code against a domain-specific SDK for defining parts, composing geometry, specifying joints, and writing tests to validate the resulting assets. The harness exposes a restricted workspace and interface to the LLM, validates the resulting assets, and returns structured feedback. In this way, the LLM is not distracted by details such as authoring a URDF file or managing a complex software environment. We show that this produces higher-quality assets than both state-of-the-art articulated-asset generators and general-purpose coding agents. Using Articraft, we build Articraft-10K, a curated dataset of over 10K articulated assets spanning 245 categories, and show its utility both for training models of articulated assets and in downstream applications such as robotics simulation and virtual reality.

</details>


### [155] [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190)
*Yanzuo Lu,Ronglai Zuo,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN训练框架和CM-GRPO强化学习方法，解决因果自回归视频扩散模型中训练与推理历史分布不匹配的问题，提升长时生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因果自回归视频扩散模型在训练和推理时面临历史分布差异（history distribution gap），限制了长时生成的保真度。

Method: 1) RAVEN：将自回放（self rollout）重新打包成交替的干净历史端点和噪声去噪状态序列，对齐训练与推理注意力，并用下游块损失监督历史表征。2) CM-GRPO：将一致性采样步骤重新表述为条件高斯转移，直接对核函数应用在线强化学习，避免了先验工作中的Euler-Maruyama辅助过程。

Result: RAVEN在质量、语义和动态度评估上超越近期因果视频蒸馏基线；CM-GRPO与RAVEN结合可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 提出的RAVEN框架和CM-GRPO方法有效缓解了训练-推理历史分布不匹配问题，提升了因果自回归视频扩散模型的长时生成质量。

Abstract: Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.

</details>


### [156] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出将潜空间中的线性流匹配替换为球面线性插值（SLERP），通过将VAE潜变量投影到固定半径球面上、使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验，并微调解码器，使得生成路径严格位于球面上且速度场纯角度化，从而在无需额外编码器或对齐目标的情况下提升类条件ImageNet-256的FID指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的潜空间流匹配方法沿欧几里得弦传输高斯噪声和VAE潜变量，但两者实际分布在薄球壳上，导致路径偏离数据流形；本文旨在通过将生成路径约束在球面测地线上解决这一几何不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 将每个潜token分解为径向和角度分量，通过component-swap探针实验发现语义和感知内容主要承载于方向而非半径；2) 将数据潜变量投影到固定token半径，采用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验；3) 冻结编码器、微调解码器；4) 用球面线性插值（SLERP）替换线性插值，使得路径严格保持在球面上，速度场纯角度化。

Result: 在匹配训练条件下，该方法在类条件ImageNet-256上一致提升了不同图像分词器的FID指标，且不改变扩散架构、无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 球面流匹配通过几何先验解决了潜空间中线性路径与数据流形不匹配的问题，是一种轻量且有效的改进方案。

Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

</details>


### [157] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EntityBench基准（140个episode/2,491个shot），用于评估多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头实体一致性，并配套提出EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，实验表明显式实体记忆能显著提升角色保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性度量简单，难以进行标准化比较，尤其缺乏对长序列中角色、物体、位置一致性的系统评估。

Method: 构建EntityBench基准：从真实叙事媒体提取140个episode（2,491个shot），包含每镜实体调度表（easy/medium/hard三级，最多50个shot、13个跨镜角色、8个跨镜位置、22个跨镜物体，回访间隔达48个shot）。配套三支柱评估套件（镜头内质量、提示对齐、跨镜一致性），设保真门控筛选实体外观。提出EntityMem系统：在生成前将验证过的实体视觉参考存入持久记忆库。

Result: 跨镜实体一致性随回访距离增加急剧下降；显式实体记忆方法在角色保真度（Cohen's d=+2.33）和出现率上均最优。

Conclusion: 长序列多镜头生成的跨镜实体一致性仍是挑战，显式实体记忆是提升一致性的有效策略。

Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

</details>


### [158] [Image Restoration via Diffusion Models with Dynamic Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14267)
*Yang Zheng,Wen Li,Zhaoqiang Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出利用动态分辨率扩散模型将数据投影到低维子空间以加速图像修复推理，基于此框架构建了SubDPS和SubDAPS方法，并进一步提出增强变体SubDAPS++，实验表明在多数场景下优于现有扩散模型方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像修复任务中计算开销大：像素空间方法计算成本高，而潜在空间方法虽压缩了特征但需反复编解码，实际运行效率甚至更差。因此需要一种既能降低计算负担又不牺牲重建质量的方法。

Method: 1) 微调预训练扩散模型以获取动态分辨率先验；2) 将像素空间的DPS和DAPS方法适配到低维子空间框架，提出SubDPS和SubDAPS；3) 基于SubDAPS进一步提出增强版SubDAPS++，同时提升重建效率和质量。

Result: 在多种图像数据集和修复任务上的实验表明，所提方法在大多数实验场景中优于最新的基于扩散模型的方法。

Conclusion: 动态分辨率子空间投影策略能有效加速扩散模型的图像修复推理过程，兼顾效率与质量。

Abstract: Diffusion models (DMs) have exhibited remarkable efficacy in various image restoration tasks. However, existing approaches typically operate within the high-dimensional pixel space, resulting in high computational overhead. While methods based on latent DMs seek to alleviate this issue by utilizing the compressed latent space of a variational autoencoder, they require repeated encoder-decoder inference. This introduces significant additional computational burdens, often resulting in runtime performance that is even inferior to that of their pixel-space counterparts. To mitigate the computational inefficiency, this work proposes projecting data into lower-dimensional subspaces using dynamic resolution DMs to accelerate the inference process. We first fine-tune pre-trained DMs for dynamic resolution priors and adapt DPS and DAPS, which are two widely used pixel-space methods for general image restoration tasks, into the proposed framework, yielding methods we refer to as SubDPS and SubDAPS, respectively. Given the favorable inference speed and reconstruction fidelity of SubDAPS, we introduce an enhanced variant termed SubDAPS++ to further boost both reconstruction efficiency and quality. Empirical evaluations across diverse image datasets and various restoration tasks demonstrate that the proposed methods outperform recent DM-based approaches in the majority of experimental scenarios. The code is available at https://github.com/StarNextDay/SubDAPS.git.

</details>


### [159] [ICED: Concept-level Machine Unlearning via Interpretable Concept Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14309)
*Shen Lin,Jing Lin,Junhao Dong,Piotr Koniusz,Li Xu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种可解释的概念级机器遗忘框架，通过多模态大语言模型构建任务相关概念词汇表，将视觉表示分解为稀疏非负的概念组合，实现细粒度知识操作，在VLMs中精准去除目标概念而保留非目标语义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLM机器遗忘方法在图像或实例层面进行操作，难以精确移除目标知识而不影响无关语义，因为单张图像通常包含多个纠缠的概念（包括要遗忘的目标概念和应保留的上下文信息）。

Method: 1) 使用多模态大语言模型从遗忘集构建紧凑的任务特定概念词汇表；2) 将视觉表示分解为稀疏、非负的语义概念组合，提供细粒度知识操作的显式接口；3) 基于分解结果进行概念级优化，选择性抑制目标概念，同时保留实例内非目标语义和全局跨模态知识。

Result: 在域内和域外遗忘设置下的广泛实验表明，该方法相比现有VLM遗忘方法，能实现更全面的目标遗忘，更好地保留同一图像内的非目标知识，并保持有竞争力的模型效用。

Conclusion: 概念级的细粒度遗忘框架能有效解决VLMs中图像级遗忘难以精确分离目标和无关语义的问题，为可解释的知识操作提供了新思路。

Abstract: Machine unlearning in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) is typically performed at the image or instance level, making it difficult to precisely remove target knowledge without affecting unrelated semantics. This issue is especially pronounced since a single image often contains multiple entangled concepts, including both target concepts to be forgotten and contextual information that should be preserved. In this paper, we propose an interpretable concept-level unlearning framework for VLMs, which constructs a compact task-specific concept vocabulary from the forgetting set using a multimodal large language model. In addition to modality alignment, visual representations are decomposed into sparse, nonnegative combinations of semantic concepts, providing an explicit interface for fine-grained knowledge manipulation. Based on this decomposition, our method formulates unlearning as concept-level optimization, where target concepts are selectively suppressed while intra-instance non-target semantics and global cross-modal knowledge are preserved. Extensive experiments across both in-domain and out-of-domain forgetting settings demonstrate that our method enables more comprehensive target forgetting, better preserves non-target knowledge within the same image, and maintains competitive model utility compared with existing VLM unlearning methods.

</details>


### [160] [FactorizedHMR: A Hybrid Framework for Video Human Mesh Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14854)
*Patrick Kwon,Chen Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FactorizedHMR，一种将人体网格恢复分解为确定性躯干-根节点回归和概率性非躯干关节完成的两阶段框架，利用流匹配和合成数据管线处理模糊性，在遮挡和世界空间指标上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人体网格恢复存在固有的模糊性：在遮挡或深度线索不足时，多个3D身体可以解释同一图像证据。且这种模糊性在身体不同部位不均匀——躯干姿态和根结构相对确定，而四肢等远端关节更不确定。

Method: 1) 两阶段框架：确定性回归模块恢复稳定的躯干-根节点锚点，概率性流匹配模块完成剩余非躯干关节。2) 组合目标表示+几何感知监督+特征感知无分类器引导，保持躯干锚点同时改善歧义关节的恢复。3) 合成数据管线提供配对图像-相机-运动监督。

Result: 在相机空间和世界空间基准上与强基线保持竞争力，在严重遮挡恢复和漂移敏感的世界空间指标上取得最明显增益。

Conclusion: 通过显式区分确定性躯干和不确定性四肢并分别处理，FactorizedHMR有效提升了遮挡场景下的人体网格恢复质量，特别是世界空间下的漂移控制。

Abstract: Human Mesh Recovery (HMR) is fundamentally ambiguous: under occlusion or weak depth cues, multiple 3D bodies can explain the same image evidence. This ambiguity is not uniform across the body, as torso pose and root structure are often relatively well constrained, whereas distal articulations such as the arms and legs are more uncertain. Building on this observation, we propose FactorizedHMR, a two-stage framework that treats these two regimes differently. A deterministic regression module first recovers a stable torso-root anchor, and a probabilistic flow-matching module then completes the remaining non-torso articulation. To make this completion reliable, we combine a composite target representation with geometry-aware supervision and feature-aware classifier-free guidance, preserving the torso-root anchor while improving single-reference recovery of ambiguity-prone articulation. We also introduce a synthetic data pipeline that provides the paired image-camera-motion supervision under diverse viewpoints. Across camera-space and world-space benchmarks, FactorizedHMR remains competitive with strong baselines, with the clearest gains in occlusion-heavy recovery and drift-sensitive world-space metrics.

</details>


### [161] [Your CLIP has 164 dimensions of noise: Exploring the embeddings covariance eigenspectrum of contrastively pretrained vision-language transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14893)
*Jakub Grzywaczewski,Dawid Płudowski,Przemysław Biecek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过对VLM潜在空间进行谱分解，发现其中存在共享噪声子空间，去除这些噪声维度不会损害甚至能提升下游任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比预训练的视觉-语言模型（VLM）的共享潜在空间存在结构异常，并包含非语义的多模态噪声，需要深入理解并处理这一现象。

Method: 利用协方差矩阵的谱分解，将VLM潜在空间分解为多模态语义信号分量和共享噪声子空间，分析噪声几何结构在不同数据子集上的子群不变性。

Result: 噪声几何结构在不同数据子集上表现出强子群不变性；去除共享噪声维度对下游任务性能无害甚至有益。

Conclusion: VLM潜在几何结构的很大一部分由共享的架构级噪声主导，而非仅由任务相关语义决定；该工作提供了对现代VLM表征结构的新机制性理解。

Abstract: Contrastively pre-trained Vision-Language Models (VLMs) serve as powerful feature extractors. Yet, their shared latent spaces are prone to structural anomalies and act as repositories for non-semantic, multi-modal noise. To address this phenomenon, we employ spectral decomposition of covariance matrices to decompose the VLM latent space into a multi-modal semantic signal component and a shared noise subspace. We observe that this noise geometry exhibits strong subgroup invariance across distinct data subsets. Crucially, pruning these shared noise dimensions is mainly harmless, preserving or actively improving downstream task performance. By isolating true semantic signals from artifactual noise, this work provides new mechanistic insights into the representational structure of modern VLMs, suggesting that a substantial fraction of their latent geometry is governed by shared, architecture-level noise rather than task-relevant semantics alone.

</details>


### [162] [MHSA: A Lightweight Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14966)
*Wei Ding,Yilin Li,Yudong Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Xingwu Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MHSA框架，通过训练轻量级MLP生成器校正跨模态注意力图，在不修改LVLM参数的情况下有效缓解幻觉，实现了从幻觉检测到缓解的拓展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LVLMs在多模态任务中表现优异但仍存在生成内容与视觉输入不一致的幻觉问题，现有DHCP方法仅能检测幻觉而无法缓解幻觉，亟需有效的幻觉缓解方案。

Method: MHSA训练一个三层MLP生成器来产生校正后的注意力，利用DHCP判别器和LVLM自身的监督信号指导训练；推理时直接替换原始跨模态注意力为校正后的注意力，无需修改LVLM参数。

Result: MHSA在多个数据集和LVLM上有效缓解了判别性和生成性幻觉。

Conclusion: MHSA将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解，为LVLM幻觉研究提供了新视角，有助于提升模型可靠性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the visual input. Prior work DHCP (Detecting Hallucinations by Cross-modal Attention Pattern) has explored hallucination detection from the perspective of cross-modal attention, but does not address hallucination mitigation. In this paper, we propose MHSA (Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention), a lightweight framework that mitigates hallucinations by learning to correct cross-modal attention patterns in LVLMs. MHSA trains a simple three-layer MLP generator to produce corrected attention, guided by supervisory signals from the DHCP discriminator and the LVLM itself. During inference, MHSA mitigates both discriminative and generative hallucinations across various datasets and LVLMs by simply replacing the original cross-modal attention with the corrected one, without modifying any LVLM parameters. By extending cross-modal attention mechanisms from hallucination detection to hallucination mitigation, MHSA offers a novel perspective on hallucination research in LVLMs and helps enhance their reliability.

</details>


### [163] [MicroscopyMatching: Towards a Ready-to-use Framework for Microscopy Image Analysis in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14980)
*Xiaofei Hui,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Shuohong Wang,Jeff W. Lichtman,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 MicroscopyMatching，首个即用型显微镜图像分析框架，通过将多种分析任务（分割、追踪、计数）统一转化为匹配问题，并利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力，在多样化显微镜分析设置下可靠执行关键分析任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动分析显微镜图像成本高且耗时；现有深度学习方法因显微镜分析设置的多样性（生物对象类型、样本处理协议、成像设备、分析任务等差异）需大量适配，给实验室带来不可持续负担，导致生物医学研究进度严重受阻，亟需一个可靠且广泛适用的分析工具。

Method: 从根本不同的视角，将多样化的显微镜图像分析任务（分割、追踪、计数）重新表述为统一的匹配问题，并利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力来有效处理该问题。

Result: 提出了 MicroscopyMatching，首个即用型显微镜图像分析框架，可在多样化显微镜分析设置下可靠执行分割、追踪、计数等关键分析任务。

Conclusion: MicroscopyMatching 填补了可靠且广泛适用的显微镜图像分析工具的空白，有望大幅推进生物医学研究进度。

Abstract: Analyzing microscopy images to extract biological object properties (e.g., their morphological organization, temporal dynamics, and population density) is fundamental to various biomedical research. Yet conducting this manually is costly and time-consuming. Though deep learning-based approaches have been explored to automate this process, the substantial diversity of microscopy analysis settings in practice (including variations of biological object types, sample processing protocols, imaging equipment, and analysis tasks, etc.) often renders them ineffective. As a result, these approaches typically require extensive adaptation for different settings, which, however, can impose burdens that are often practically unsustainable for laboratories, forcing biomedical researchers to still commonly rely on manual analysis, thereby severely bottlenecking the pace of biomedical research progress. This situation has created a pressing and long-standing need for a reliable and broadly applicable microscopy image analysis tool, yet such a tool is still missing. To address this gap, we present the first ready-to-use microscopy image analysis framework, MicroscopyMatching, that can reliably perform key analysis tasks (including segmentation, tracking, and counting) across diverse microscopy analysis settings. From a fundamentally different perspective, MicroscopyMatching reformulates diverse microscopy image analysis tasks as a unified matching problem, effectively handling this problem by exploiting the robust matching capability from pre-trained latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [164] [Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991)
*Francesco Pastori,Francesca Fati,Marina Rosanu,Luigi De Vitis,Lucia Ribero,Gabriella Schivardi,Giovanni Damiano Aletti,Nicoletta Colombo,Jvan Casarin,Francesco Multinu,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度学习的方法，利用治疗前对比增强CT自动提取的3D病灶掩膜，预测卵巢癌患者对新辅助化疗的应答情况，在测试集上ROC-AUC达0.73，F1-score达0.70。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤，约60%患者确诊时已处于晚期，5年生存率仅约30%。早期识别新辅助化疗无效患者可避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机，这是当前关键的未满足临床需求。

Method: 提出非侵入性深度学习框架：首先从治疗前对比增强CT中自动提取3D病灶掩膜；然后使用部分微调的预训练图像编码器对轴向切片进行编码；通过基于注意力机制的模块将切片级表示聚合为体素级嵌入；训练结合分类损失、有监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘，以提高应答者与非应答者之间的区分能力。

Result: 基于欧洲肿瘤研究所（意大利米兰）280例患者（147例应答者，133例非应答者）的单中心回顾性队列开发，在测试集上ROC-AUC为0.73（95% CI: 0.58-0.86），F1-score为0.70（95% CI: 0.56-0.82）。

Conclusion: 提出的架构能够学习临床相关的预测模式，为基于影像的卵巢癌新辅助化疗应答分层工具提供了稳健基础。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the most lethal gynecologic malignancy: around 60% of patients are diagnosed at an advanced stage, with an associated 5-year survival rate of about 30%. Early identification of non-responders to neoadjuvant chemotherapy remains a key unmet need, as it could prevent ineffective therapy and avoid delays in optimal surgical management. This work proposes a non-invasive deep learning framework to predict neoadjuvant chemotherapy response from pre-treatment contrast-enhanced CT by leveraging automatically derived 3D lesion masks. The approach encodes axial slices with a partially fine-tuned pretrained image encoder and aggregates slice-level representations into a volumetric embedding through an attention-based module. Training combines classification loss with supervised contrastive regularization and hard-negative mining to improve separation between ambiguous responders and non-responders. The method was developed on a retrospective single-center cohort from the European Institute of Oncology (Milan, IT), including 280 eligible patients (147 responder, 133 non-responder). On the test cohort, the model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.58-0.86) and an F1-score of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.56-0.82). Overall, these results suggest that the proposed architecture learns clinically relevant predictive patterns and provides a robust foundation for an imaging-based stratification tool.

</details>


### [165] [EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042)
*Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Mariam Hassan,Lan Feng,Wentao Pan,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EverAnimate是一种高效的post-training方法，通过持久化潜在上下文记忆（包括Persistent Latent Propagation和Restorative Flow Matching两种机制）解决长时程动画视频生成中的累积漂移问题（低层质量漂移和高层语义漂移），仅需轻量LoRA微调即显著超越SOTA方法（10秒：PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；90秒：提升15%/15%和32%/27%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程动画视频生成面临累积漂移问题：一是低层质量漂移（如静态背景逐渐退化），二是高层语义漂移（如角色身份和视角依赖属性不一致）。现有基于chunk的生成方法难以解决高度动态人体运动与相对静态环境之间的生成矛盾。

Method: EverAnimate包含两个互补机制：(i) Persistent Latent Propagation（持久化潜在传播），跨chunk维护上下文记忆，在潜在空间中传播身份和运动信息，缓解时间遗忘；(ii) Restorative Flow Matching（恢复性流匹配），在采样过程中通过速度调整引入隐式恢复目标，提升chunk内保真度。整体采用轻量LoRA微调。

Result: 在短时程（10秒）场景下，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在长时程（90秒）场景下，增益提升至15%/15%和32%/27%，全面超越SOTA长动画生成方法。

Conclusion: EverAnimate通过持久化潜在上下文记忆机制有效解决了长时程动画生成中的累积漂移问题，仅需轻量LoRA微调即可在短时程和长时程场景下均取得显著优于现有方法的性能。

Abstract: We propose EverAnimate, an efficient post-training method for long-horizon animated video generation that preserves visual quality and character identity. Long-form animation remains challenging because highly dynamic human motion must be synthesized against relatively static environments, making chunk-based generation prone to accumulated drift: (i) low-level quality drift, such as progressive degradation of static backgrounds, and (ii) high-level semantic drift, such as inconsistent character identity and view-dependent attributes. To address this issue, EverAnimate restores drifted flow trajectories by anchoring generation to a persistent latent context memory, consisting of two complementary mechanisms. (i) Persistent Latent Propagation maintains a context memory across chunks to propagate identity and motion in latent space while mitigating temporal forgetting. (ii) Restorative Flow Matching introduces an implicit restoration objective during sampling through velocity adjustment, improving within-chunk fidelity. With only lightweight LoRA tuning, EverAnimate outperforms state-of-the-art long-animation methods in both short- and long-horizon settings: at 10 seconds, it improves PSNR/SSIM by 8%/7% and reduces LPIPS/FID by 22%/11%; at 90 seconds, the gains increase to 15%/15% and 32%/27%, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [166] [Memory Forensics Techniques for Automated Detection and Analysis of Go Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14020)
*Hala Ali,Andrew Case,Irfan Ahmed*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个针对Go二进制文件运行时分析的记忆取证框架，通过解析Go内部结构、ABI感知逆向分析和goroutine堆栈分析，成功从恶意软件中恢复C2端点、加密密钥、持久化机制等运行时证据，并实现了Volatility 3插件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有静态分析和逆向工程工具对Go二进制文件的支持仅限于编译时产物，无法恢复仅在内存中存在的运行时执行状态和证据，导致对Go恶意软件的分析存在空白。

Method: 1) 解析Go内部结构重建类型和函数元数据，恢复堆分配和静态字符串；2) ABI感知的逆向分析，从调用点推导执行路径和参数值；3) 分析goroutine堆栈识别活跃执行函数并恢复运行时参数值；4) 实现为Volatility 3插件。

Result: 在BRICKSTORM后门、Obscura勒索软件、Pantegana RAT等真实恶意软件上成功恢复了C2端点、持久化机制、加密密钥、勒索信和执行状态，包括已发布威胁情报中缺失的关键运行时证据。

Conclusion: 该框架有效弥补了Go二进制文件运行时分析的空缺，能够恢复传统静态方法无法获取的运行时内存证据，提升了对复杂Go恶意软件的分析能力。

Abstract: The Go programming language has become increasingly popular among malware developers due to its ability to produce statically linked, cross-platform executables that challenge traditional analysis techniques. These binaries embed a substantial runtime and compiler-generated metadata and are compiled with aggressive optimizations that discard type information for function parameters and local variables. Go's design further complicates analysis by representing strings as pointer-length pairs rather than null-terminated sequences, employing a caller-allocated stack model that obscures argument boundaries, and fragmenting program state across concurrent goroutines. Although existing static analysis and reverse engineering tools provide Go-specific support, they remain limited to compile-time artifacts and cannot recover runtime execution state and artifacts that persist solely in memory. To address this gap, we present the first memory forensics framework for runtime analysis of Go binaries. By parsing Go's internal structures, our framework reconstructs type and function metadata, recovers heap-allocated and static strings, and distinguishes application-level functions. Through ABI-aware backward analysis, it derives execution paths and argument values from call sites. To capture runtime state beyond what static analysis reveals, it analyzes goroutine stacks to identify actively executing functions and recover their runtime argument values. We implemented all capabilities as Volatility 3 plugins and evaluated them against malware seen in recent incidents, such as the BRICKSTORM backdoor, Obscura ransomware, and Pantegana RAT, as well as open-source samples for reproducibility. The framework successfully recovered C2 endpoints, persistence mechanisms, encryption keys, ransom notes, and execution state, including critical runtime artifacts that were absent from published threat intelligence.

</details>


### [167] [ExploitBench: A Capability Ladder Benchmark for LLM Cybersecurity Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14153)
*Seunghyun Lee,David Brumley*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ExploitBench 是一个能力分级的漏洞利用基准测试，将利用过程分解为16个可度量标志，从代码覆盖到任意代码执行。在41个V8漏洞上的测试表明，公开模型能常规触发崩溃但无法实现任意代码执行，而私有模型在约半数案例中实现了任意代码执行。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全基准将崩溃视为利用成功，但漏洞利用是从触发bug到构建可重用原语和控制的渐进能力阶梯，需要更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 将漏洞利用分解为16个可度量能力标志（覆盖、崩溃、沙箱原语、任意读写、控制流劫持、任意代码执行），使用确定性预言机验证：每轮随机挑战-响应验证原语、差分执行对照ground-truth二进制测量进展、信号处理程序证明验证代码执行。在41个V8漏洞上测试。

Result: 公开模型能常规触发漏洞并导致崩溃，但无法实现任意代码执行；私有模型在约一半案例中实现了任意代码执行。存在显著的能力鸿沟。

Conclusion: 漏洞利用构建对抗加固目标是一项新兴的前沿能力，现有公开模型在此任务上存在显著能力瓶颈。

Abstract: Exploitation is not a binary event. It is a ladder of acquiring progressive capabilities, from executing a single buggy line of code to taking full control of the target. However, existing LLM security benchmarks treat a crash as exploitation success. That single binary outcome collapses the hard parts of exploitation: the transition from triggering a bug to constructing reusable primitives and control.
  We present ExploitBench, a capability-graded benchmark that decomposes exploitation into 16 measurable flags, from coverage and crash through sandbox primitives, arbitrary read/write, control-flow hijack, and arbitrary code execution. Each capability is verified by a deterministic oracle that uses a per-run randomized challenge-response for primitives, differential execution against ground-truth binaries to measure progress, and a signal-handler proof for code execution.
  We instantiate ExploitBench on 41 V8 bugs because V8 is both widely deployed and exploitation-hardened. We report three arms: <model,env> as the primary measurement of model-environment capability, <model,env, adaptive coaching> as a secondary arm that adds adaptive coaching to test whether targeted feedback shifts outcomes, and <model,env,harness> as an ablation that swaps in the model's native CLI to check whether vendor-side optimizations increase exploitation capabilities.
  Our results show a sharp capability split between publicly deployed frontier models and the private frontier. Across the 8 publicly deployed models tested, reaching the vulnerable code and triggering a crash is routine, but arbitrary code execution is not. The private model shows arbitrary code execution on approximately half. Overall, results suggest that exploit construction against hardened targets is an emerging frontier capability.

</details>


### [168] [DSTAN-Med: Dual-Channel Spatiotemporal Attention with Physiological Plausibility Filtering for False Data Injection Attack Detection in IoT-Based Medical Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14165)
*Md Mehedi Hasan,Rafiqul Islam,Md Zakir Hossain*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出DSTAN-Med框架，通过双通道注意力机制（DAM）独立处理传感器间空间相关性和时间依赖性，结合生理合理性滤波器（PPF）抑制违背领域知识的虚假数据注入攻击，在三个IoMT数据集上平均灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习检测器将传感器间空间相关性与时间依赖性在共享隐空间中混合，无法解耦FDI攻击同时留下的空间和时间特征；且缺乏利用领域知识约束输出以防止生理上不可能的攻击模式的方法。

Method: 提出DSTAN-Med监督框架，包含：(1) 双通道注意力机制（DAM），通过独立传感器级（SWA）和时间级（TWA）自注意力路径在正交张量轴上操作；(2) 残差1D-CNN模块用于局部时间特征提取；(3) 零参数生理合理性滤波器（PPF），抑制违反领域知识边界的攻击特征。

Result: 在PhysioNet/CinC 2012、MIMIC-III Waveform和WESAD三个数据集上，DSTAN-Med相比最强Transformer基线（TranAD）平均灵敏度提升7.4-8.3个百分点（p<0.01，McNemar检验，Holm-Bonferroni校正）；PPF独立贡献3.1-4.2个百分点的精确率提升，灵敏度损失可忽略；消融实验证明各组件均不可或缺，移除残差连接导致灵敏度下降14个百分点。

Conclusion: DSTAN-Med通过解耦时空特征和引入领域知识约束，显著提升IoMT传感器流FDI攻击检测性能，代码已开源。

Abstract: False data injection (FDI) attacks on Internet of Medical Things (IoMT) sensor streams falsify vital signs in transit, threatening patient safety and defeating clinical monitoring systems that lack cyber-physical anomaly detection capability. Existing deep learning detectors conflate inter-sensor spatial correlations with temporal dependencies in a shared latent space, preventing disentanglement of the distinct spatial and temporal signatures that FDI attacks imprint simultaneously; no current method exploits domain knowledge to constrain outputs against physiologically impossible attack patterns. We propose DSTAN-Med, a supervised framework comprising a Dual-channel Attention Mechanism (DAM) that routes multivariate sensor windows through independent sensor-wise (SWA) and time-wise (TWA) self-attention pathways operating on orthogonal tensor axes, a residual 1D-CNN block for local temporal feature extraction, and a zero-parameter Physiological Plausibility Filter (PPF) that suppresses attack signatures violating domain-knowledge bounds. Evaluated across three IoMT sensor datasets - PhysioNet/CinC 2012 (ICU vital signs), MIMIC-III Waveform (continuous ICU waveforms), and WESAD (wearable biosensor signals) - DSTAN-Med achieves mean sensitivity gains of 7.4-8.3 percentage points over the strongest Transformer baseline (TranAD), with improvements significant at p < 0.01 (McNemar's test, Holm-Bonferroni correction). The PPF contributes independent precision gains of 3.1-4.2 percentage points at negligible sensitivity cost across all three corpora. Ablation studies confirm that each component is individually necessary; removal of residual connections alone reduces sensitivity by 14.0 percentage points. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/mehedi93hasan/DSTAN-MED.

</details>


### [169] [Web Agents Should Adopt the Plan-Then-Execute Paradigm](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14290)
*Julien Piet,Annabella Chow,Yiwei Hou,Muxi Lyu,Sylvie Venuto,Jinhao Zhu,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: ReAct架构不适合网页Agent，应改用"计划-执行"模式：先制定任务程序，再执行。网页内容混合多方输入，ReAct让不可信数据直接控制模型，易受提示注入攻击。计划-执行模式限制了不可信数据的影响范围。WebArena上所有任务兼容此模式，80%可纯程序化完成。主要障碍是浏览器工具（点击、输入等）缺乏语义抽象，需要构建类型化接口将网页交互转为任务级操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ReAct架构下，网页Agent的每一步决策都暴露于混合了多方输入的网页内容（如卖家信息、用户评论、广告），这为提示注入攻击提供了直接路径，攻击者可通过不可信内容篡改模型的控制流。因此需要一种更安全的架构。

Method: 提出plan-then-execute范式：Agent先在未见运行时网页内容的情况下，承诺一个任务特定的执行程序（可包含条件分支和参数），然后执行该程序。执行过程中，不可信数据只能影响预定义图结构内的值或分支，不能重新定义用户任务或让模型运行时合成新动作。

Result: 在WebArena基准上，所有任务都兼容plan-then-execute，其中80%可完全通过程序化计划完成（无需运行时LLM子程序）。当前主要障碍是浏览器工具（click, type, scroll等）的语义依赖于页面上下文，缺乏类型化接口来映射为任务级操作。

Conclusion: 网页Agent不需要默认的反应式（ReAct）架构，需要的是类型化的、完整的、可审计的网站API，将交互从点击和按键提升到任务级操作。这是一个基础设施问题，而非建模问题。

Abstract: ReAct has become the default architecture across LLM agents, and many existing web agents follow this paradigm. We argue that it is the wrong default for web agents. Instead, web agents should default to plan-then-execute: commit to a task-specific program before observing runtime web content, then execute it. The reason is that web content mixes inputs from many parties. An e-commerce product page may combine a seller's listing, customer reviews and sponsored advertisements.
