Under ReAct, all of this content flows into the model when deciding on the next action, creating a direct path for prompt injections to steer the agent's control flow. Plan-then-execute changes this boundary: untrusted data may influence values or branches inside a predefined execution graph, but it cannot redefine the user task or cause the model to synthesize new actions at runtime. We analyze WebArena, a popular web agent benchmark, and find that all tasks are compatible with plan-then-execute, while 80% can be completed with a purely programmatic plan, without any runtime LLM subroutine. We identify the main barrier to adopting plan-then-execute on the web: For it to work well, tools must map cleanly to semantic actions, with effects known before execution, so agents have enough information to plan. The web does not naturally expose that interface. Browser tools such as click, type, and scroll have page-dependent meanings. Planning at this layer is near-sighted: the agent can only see actions on the current page, and later actions appear only after it acts. Closing this gap requires typed interfaces that turn website interactions from clicks and keystrokes to task-level operations. This is an infrastructure problem, not a modeling problem. Web tasks do not need reactivity by default; they need typed, complete, auditable website APIs.

</details>


### [170] [To See is Not to Learn: Protecting Multimodal Data from Unauthorized Fine-Tuning of Large Vision-Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14291)
*Chengshuai Zhao,Zhen Tan,Dawei Li,Zhiyuan Yu,Huan Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: MMGuard是一种通过注入人眼不可察觉的扰动来生成不可学习样本的方法，使LVLM在微调时过拟合噪声，从而在推理时性能下降，实现主动防御未经授权的模型微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（如机器遗忘、水印）均为事后处理，只能在知识产权侵犯发生之后才采取行动，缺乏主动保护多模态数据免受未经授权LVLM微调的能力。

Method: MMGuard通过两类扰动注入生成不可学习样本：(1) 优化捷径扰动——最小化训练损失使模型过拟合噪声；(2) 跨模态绑定干扰——战略性地转移LVLM注意力，强制在噪声与训练目标之间建立虚假相关性，并具有理论保证。同时结合集成学习策略提升跨模型迁移性。

Result: 在9个开源LVLM和6个数据集上进行了全面评估，在白盒、灰盒和黑盒威胁模型下均展示了有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的保护效果。

Conclusion: MMGuard在主动防御针对微调的攻击方面具有机制性优势，是一种有效、隐蔽且鲁棒的多模态数据保护方法。

Abstract: The rapid advancement of Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) is increasingly accompanied by unauthorized scraping and training on multimodal web data, posing severe copyright and privacy risks to data owners. Existing countermeasures, such as machine unlearning and watermarks, are inherent post-hoc approaches that act only after intellectual property infringement has already occurred. In this work, we propose MMGuard to empower data owners to proactively protect their multimodal data against unauthorized LVLM fine-tuning. MMGuard generates unlearnable examples by injecting human-imperceptible perturbations that actively exploit the learning dynamics of LVLMs. By minimizing the training loss, the perturbation creates an optimization shortcut, causing the model to overfit to the noise and thereby degrading downstream performance when the perturbation is absent during inference. To further strengthen this defense, MMGuard introduces a cross-modal binding disruption, strategically shifting LVLM attention to enforce a spurious correlation between the noise and the training target with theoretical guarantees. Enhanced by an ensemble learning strategy for cross-model transferability, MMGuard is evaluated against nine open-source LVLMs across six datasets. Our comprehensive results demonstrate effective, stealthy, and robust protection under white-box, gray-box, and black-box threat models, establishing a mechanistic advantage in proactively defending against aggressive fine-tuning exploitation.

</details>


### [171] [The Great Pretender: A Stochasticity Problem in LLM Jailbreak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14418)
*Jean-Philippe Monteuuis,Cong Chen,Jonathan Petit*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了越狱攻击成功率(ASR)指标的不稳定性问题，指出由于随机性影响，已发表的ASR数值被系统性夸大且不可跨论文比较。作者提出了新指标和两个框架(CAS-eval和CAS-gen)，CAS-eval评估表明攻击在多次尝试时ASR可下降30个百分点，而CAS-gen能帮助恢复这30个百分点的损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到越狱攻击提示在目标模型上表现不一致（50%连续成功率 vs 80%报告ASR），质疑ASR作为主要基准指标的稳定性，并指出已发表ASR数值被系统性夸大且不可跨论文比较。

Method: 研究随机性在攻击评估和攻击生成阶段的影响；提出CAS-eval评估框架和CAS-gen攻击生成框架；提出新的稳定性度量指标。

Result: CAS-eval评估显示攻击在需要多次成功时ASR可下降30个百分点；CAS-gen框架能帮助恢复这30个百分点的损失。

Conclusion: ASR作为越狱攻击的主要指标是不稳定的，已发表数值被夸大且不可比较；CAS-eval和CAS-gen框架分别解决了评估和生成阶段的随机性问题。

Abstract: "Oh-Oh, yes, I'm the great pretender. Pretending that I'm doing well. My need is such, I pretend too much..." summarizes the state in the area of jailbreak creation and evaluation. You find this method to generate adversarial attacks proposed by a reputable institution (e.g., BoN from Anthropic or Crescendo from Microsoft Research). However, this method does not deliver on the promise claimed in the paper despite having top ASR scores against industry-grade LLMs. You successfully generate the jailbreak prompts against your target (open) model. However, the generated jailbreak prompt works against the target model with a 50% consecutive success rate (5 out of 10 attempts) despite having an 80% ASR (on paper) on the latest closed-source model (with a guardrail system)! This observation leads us to think. First, Attack Success Rate (ASR), the primary metric for LLM jailbreak benchmarking, is not a stable quantity. Second, published ASR numbers are therefore systematically inflated and incomparable across papers. Therefore, we wonder "Why a successful jailbreak prompt does not perform consistently well against a target model on which the prompts have been optimized?". To answer this question, we study the impact of stochasticity not only during attack evaluation but also during attack generation. Our evaluation includes several jailbreak attacks, models (different sizes and providers), and judges. In addition, we propose a new metric and two new frameworks (CAS-eval and CAS-gen). Our evaluation framework, CAS-eval, shows that an attack can have an ASR drop of up to 30 percentage points when a jailbreak prompt needs to succeed on more than one attempt. Thankfully, our attack generation framework (CAS-gen) improves previous jailbreak methods and helps them recover this loss of 30 percentage points!

</details>


### [172] [Defenses at Odds: Measuring and Explaining Defense Conflicts in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14514)
*Xiangtao Meng,Wenyu Chen,Chuanchao Zang,Xinyu Gao,Jianing Wang,Li Wang,Zheng Li,Shanqing Guo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究了LLM在顺序部署中跨防御的相互作用，发现38.9%的防御序列会出现风险恶化，并提出了一种基于层冲突感知的轻量级缓解方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM防御方法几乎都在一次性部署假设下独立评估，但实际中防御是顺序部署的。现有工作忽视了后续防御是否会破坏先前建立的防护效果这一问题。

Method: 在三个风险维度、三个模型家族上评估了144种有序防御序列，利用层表示散度、激活修补和PCA轨迹分析进行机制分析，并提出层冲突分数（layer-wise conflict score）量化防御诱导激活子空间间的几何张力。

Result: 38.9%的序列出现可测量的风险恶化，相互作用高度不对称且依赖顺序。冲突序列中关键层的参数更新呈现强反对齐，而良性排序保持近正交。防御崩溃源于共享层中的激活模式反转。

Conclusion: 提出冲突引导的层冻结（conflict-guided layer freezing）作为轻量级缓解方法，通过选择性冻结高冲突层来保持先前防护效果且不降低后续防御性能。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) deployed in high-stakes applications must simultaneously manage multiple risks, yet existing defenses are almost exclusively evaluated in isolation under a one-shot deployment assumption. In practice, providers patch models incrementally throughout their lifecycle-responding to newly exposed vulnerabilities or targeted data-removal requests without retraining from scratch. This raises a fundamental but underexplored question: does a later defense preserve the protections established by an earlier one? We present the first systematic study of cross-defense interactions under sequential deployment. Evaluating 144 ordered sequences across three risk dimensions and three model families, we find that 38.9% exhibit measurable risk exacerbation on the originally defended dimension. These interactions are highly asymmetric and order-dependent. To explain these phenomena, we conduct a mechanistic analysis on representative deployment sequences. Using layer-wise representational divergence and activation patching, we localize each defense to a compact set of critical layers. In conflicting sequences, the overlapping critical layers exhibit strongly anti-aligned parameter updates, whereas benign orderings maintain near-orthogonal updates. PCA trajectory analysis reveals that defense collapse stems from activation pattern reversals in these shared layers. We further introduce a layer-wise conflict score that quantifies the geometric tension between defense-induced activation subspaces, offering mechanistic insight into the observed reversals. Guided by this diagnosis, we propose conflict-guided layer freezing, a lightweight mitigation that selectively freezes high-conflict layers during sequential deployment, preserving prior protections without degrading secondary defense performance.

</details>


### [173] [Privacy Auditing with Zero (0) Training Run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14591)
*Tudor Cebere,Mathieu Even,Linus Bleistein,Aurélien Bellet*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出Zero-Run隐私审计方法，在无需重训练或控制数据插入的条件下，利用两个固定数据集（已知训练集成员与非成员）对模型进行事后差分隐私审计，并通过因果推断校正分布偏移带来的混淆效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有隐私审计方法需要对训练流程进行干预（多次重训练或随机化数据包含），这对于大型已部署系统（如基础模型）往往不可行。因此需要一种事后(post-hoc)的、无需干预的审计框架。

Method: 提出Zero-Run审计框架：(1) 将分布偏移与算法泄露的联合效应建模为自适应组合，给出保守的全局校正；(2) 基于观测数据条件化，逐点调整成员猜测，得到更尖锐的实例级边界。借鉴因果推断思想形式化混淆效应。

Result: 在合成数据和大型模型上的实验表明，Zero-Run审计能够在无法重训练或控制数据插入的场景下实现实用的隐私评估。

Conclusion: Zero-Run提供了一种在观测性设置下有效的差分隐私审计方法，解决了分布偏移带来的混淆问题，拓展了隐私审计在大型已部署系统上的适用性。

Abstract: Privacy auditing provides empirical lower bounds on the differential privacy parameters of learning algorithms. Existing methods, however, require interventional access to the training pipeline, either to retrain multiple times or to randomize data inclusion. This is often infeasible for large deployed systems such as foundation models. We introduce Zero-Run privacy auditing, a post-hoc framework for auditing models using two fixed datasets: examples known to be training-set members and examples known to be non-members. In this observational regime, membership is no longer randomized; instead, member and non-member data often differ in distribution, so membership inference scores may reflect a distribution shift rather than algorithmic leakage. Drawing on ideas from causal inference, we formalize this confounding effect and propose two complementary corrections that yield valid privacy audits. Our first approach models the combined effect of distribution shift and algorithmic leakage as an adaptive composition, producing conservative global corrections. Our second approach conditions on observed data and adjusts pointwise membership guesses, yielding sharper instance-dependent bounds. Experiments on synthetic data and large-scale models show that Zero-Run auditing enables practical privacy evaluation when retraining or controlled data insertion is infeasible.

</details>


### [174] [One Step to the Side: Why Defenses Against Malicious Finetuning Fail Under Adaptive Adversaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14605)
*Itay Zloczower,Eyal Lenga,Gilad Gressel,Yisroel Mirsky*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文指出，尽管模型发布前经过安全对齐，但通过微调可以轻易移除安全防护。现有15种防御机制存在共同弱点：仅模糊或误导通往有害行为的路径，而非移除有害行为本身。研究者开发了一种统一自适应攻击，能够攻破所有防御机制，证明当前方法并未提供稳健的安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型提供者允许用户通过API微调或发布开源权重，但现有安全防护在恶意微调下容易被移除。虽然已有多种防御方法被提出，但这些方法仅在固定攻击下进行评估，未考虑自适应攻击者。作者希望揭示这些防御的局限性，并为未来防御评估提供统一的自适应攻击基准。

Method: 研究者首先调查了15种近期防御方法，识别出几种防御机制；然后分析了这些机制的共同弱点——仅掩盖或误导有害行为路径而非移除行为本身；最后开发了一种统一的自适应攻击方法，能够针对所有防御机制进行有效攻击。

Result: 统一自适应攻击成功攻破了所有15种防御机制，表明当前防御方法主要阻止了其设计时针对的特定攻击，但无法抵御自适应攻击者。这些防御并未提供真正的稳健安全性。

Conclusion: 当前针对恶意微调的防御方法不具鲁棒性，仅能阻止已知攻击。研究者希望其提出的统一自适应攻击能帮助未来研究者在部署前对新防御进行压力测试，推动更安全的模型微调机制设计。

Abstract: Model providers increasingly release open weights or allow users to fine-tune foundation models through APIs. Although these models are safety-aligned before release, their safeguards can often be removed by fine-tuning on harmful data. Recent defenses aim to make models robust to such malicious fine-tuning, but they are largely evaluated only against fixed attacks that do not account for the defense. We show that these robustness claims are incomplete. Surveying 15 recent defenses, we identify several defense mechanisms and show that they share a single weakness: they obscure or misdirect the path to harmful behavior without removing the behavior itself. We then develop a unified adaptive attack that breaks defenses across all defense mechanisms. Our results show that current approaches do not provide robust security; they mainly stop the attacks they were designed against. We hope that our unified adaptive adversary for this domain will help future researchers and practitioners stress-test new defenses before deployment.

</details>


### [175] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: A Practical Side-Channel Attack on Smartphones via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14633)
*Yukun Cheng,Changhai Ou,Shiyu Zhu,Jinyuan Zhang,Zhenfang Qiu,Xingshuo Han,Tianwei Zhang,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TESLA，一种利用电容触摸屏电磁辐射的新型接触式旁路攻击，可高精度窃取PIN码、键盘输入、应用类别和手写轨迹等敏感信息，在多种商用手机和实际场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电容触屏侧信道攻击往往需要严格条件或有创测量，缺乏一种非接触式、统一泄漏模型的方法来全面窃取触屏交互中的敏感信息。

Method: TESLA利用触摸屏扫描过程中固有的电磁辐射，通过秘密放置EM探头近距采集信号，提取触控交互的时空演化特征，形成统一泄漏基，进而恢复多种敏感信息。

Result: 在iPhone X、小米10 Pro、三星S10和华为Mate 30 Pro上，PIN码识别成功率99.3%，键盘输入重建97.6%，应用推断95.0%，手写字符识别76.8%，手写轨迹Jaccard指数0.74。

Conclusion: TESLA相比现有攻击具有更广的攻击目标范围、更高效的样本采集和实际场景可操作性，验证了电容触屏电磁辐射作为统一侧信道泄漏源的严重安全威胁。

Abstract: Capacitive touchscreens in modern smartphones introduce severe side-channel vulnerabilities. However, existing attacks often require restrictive conditions or invasive measurements. This paper presents TESLA, a novel, contactless electromagnetic (EM) side-channel attack that exploits inherent EM emanations during touchscreen scanning. We demonstrate that these emanations encode the spatiotemporal evolution of touch interactions, forming a unified leakage basis. By secretly placing an EM probe near the victim's device, TESLA enables attackers to extract highly sensitive information, including screen-unlocking PIN codes, keyboard inputs, interacting application categories, and continuous handwriting trajectories. Compared to existing attacks, TESLA offers a broader range of attack targets, more efficient sample acquisition, and operations in practical attack scenarios. Extensive evaluations on popular commercial smartphones, specifically the iPhone X, Xiaomi 10 Pro, Samsung S10, and Huawei Mate 30 Pro, validate the effectiveness of TESLA. It achieves remarkable inference accuracy in diverse settings such as private meeting rooms and public libraries, with success rates of 99.3% for PIN code recognition, 97.6% for keyboard input reconstruction, and 95.0% for application inference, respectively. Simultaneously, it attains a 76.8% character recognition accuracy and a high geometric similarity (Jaccard index of 0.74) for 2D handwriting trajectory reconstruction.

</details>


### [176] [Adapting AlphaEvolve to Optimize Fully Homomorphic Encryption on TPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14718)
*Shruthi Gorantala,Jianming Tong,Asra Ali,Baiyu Li,Jonathan Katz,Jeremy Kun,Thomas Steinke,Abhradeep Thakurta,Julian Walker,Amir Yazdanbakhsh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出AlphaEvolve，一种利用LLM驱动代码生成和硬件实时反馈的进化搜索框架，自动探索全同态加密（FHE）在TPU上的硬件感知优化。在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，24小时内分别将TFHE引导延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转和乘法延迟提升1.31倍和1.18倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHE因高计算开销难以大规模部署，现有TPU等专用加速器虽能缓解，但将复杂密码学内核映射到TPU架构（MXU与VPU协同、向量寄存器文件数据移动）面临挑战。现有编译器栈难以有效利用底层硬件，开发者需手动试错，导致碎片化执行和资源利用率低。

Method: 将优化过程建模为进化搜索问题，利用AlphaEvolve闭环系统，结合LLM驱动代码生成、硬件执行真实反馈和严格的正确性测试，自动探索硬件感知的密码学内核优化。

Result: 在Google Cloud TPUv5e上，24小时内TFHE引导延迟提升2.5倍，CKKS旋转延迟提升1.31倍，CKKS乘法延迟提升1.18倍，均超越人工优化基线。

Conclusion: AlphaEvolve能够自动发现超越人工设计的实现级优化，帮助研究者在密码学、编译器与硬件加速器之间高效导航优化权衡，有望加速FHE的实际部署。

Abstract: The deployment of Fully Homomorphic Encryption (FHE) at scale is hindered due to its heavy computational overhead. While specialized hardware accelerators like Google Tensor Processing Units (TPUs) can help, mapping complex cryptographic kernels onto such architectures remains a challenge. Efficient execution requires co-optimization between the systolic array-based Matrix Multiplication Unit (MXU) and Vector Processing Units (VPUs), as well as the orchestration of data movement across the vector register files. Existing compiler stacks often abstract low-level hardware utilization, requiring developers to adopt a manual trial-and-error process that often results in fragmented execution and underutilized resources. To accelerate this development process, we use AlphaEvolve to automate the exploration of hardware-aware cryptographic-kernel optimizations. We frame optimization as an evolutionary search problem, utilizing the closed-loop system provided by AlphaEvolve, that leverages LLM-driven code generation. We use real-world feedback from hardware execution and rigorous correctness testing to guide the evolution process. We evaluate AlphaEvolve optimization on primitives for both the TFHE (Jaxite) and CKKS (CROSS) FHE schemes on Google Cloud TPUv5e, a contemporary TPU architecture. Within 24 hours of automated exploration, AlphaEvolve discovered implementation-level optimizations that improve TFHE bootstrap latency by 2.5x and CKKS rotation and multiplication latency by 1.31x and 1.18x, respectively, relative to human-engineered state of the art. These results demonstrate that AlphaEvolve can be used to enable researchers to navigate the optimization trade-offs between cryptography, compilers, and hardware accelerators.

</details>


### [177] [EVA: Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14750)
*Yi Wang,Hongye Qiu,Yue Xu,Sibei Yang,Zhan Qin,Minlie Huang,Wenjie Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出EVA框架，通过直接模型编辑（定位并精确修改特定神经元）而非重训练或外部过滤器来增强LLM/VLM对越狱攻击的防御能力，在不牺牲通用能力的前提下实现高效安全对齐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有防御方法（安全微调、外部过滤）虽有一定效果，但存在计算开销大、安全-效用权衡（损害良性任务性能）等问题，需要更精准高效的防御方案。

Method: 将安全对齐重构为精确知识校正任务：识别并精确编辑模型中导致对有害指令脆弱性的特定神经元，而非重新训练大量参数或使用外部过滤器。

Result: 在LLM和VLM上均优于基线方法，有效缓解越狱攻击，同时保持通用推理能力。

Conclusion: EVA提供了一种精准高效的部署后安全对齐方案，通过神经编辑实现安全性与通用性的兼顾。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision Language Models (VLMs) have demonstrated impressive capabilities but remain vulnerable to jailbreaking attacks, where adversaries exploit textual or visual triggers to bypass safety guardrails. Recent defenses typically rely on safety fine-tuning or external filters to reduce the model's likelihood of producing harmful content. While effective to some extent, these methods often incur significant computational overheads and suffer from the safety utility trade-off, degrading the model's performance on benign tasks. To address these challenges, we propose EVA (Editing for Versatile Alignment against Jailbreaks), a novel framework that pioneers the application of direct model editing for safety alignment. EVA reframes safety alignment as a precise knowledge correction task. Instead of retraining massive parameters, EVA identifies and surgically edits specific neurons responsible for the model's susceptibility to harmful instructions, while leaving the vast majority of the model unchanged. By localizing the updates, EVA effectively neutralizes harmful behaviors without compromising the model's general reasoning capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EVA outperforms baselines in mitigating jailbreaks across both LLMs and VLMs, offering a precise and efficient solution for post-deployment safety alignment.

</details>


### [178] [Known By Their Actions: Fingerprinting LLM Browser Agents via UI Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14786)
*William Lugoloobi,Samuelle Marro,Jabez Magomere,Joss Wright,Chris Russell*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文发现，通过被动JavaScript追踪器捕获的LLM智能体的行为动作和交互时间，可以高精度（F1高达96%）识别其底层模型。这种攻击面具有跨模型家族和数据量的泛化能力，而简单的随机延时注入无法提供有效防御。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究基于LLM的智能体在浏览网页时，网站能否被动识别出驱动智能体的底层模型。这一能力意味着严重的安全风险——攻击者可根据已知模型漏洞实施针对性攻击。

Method: 横跨14个前沿LLM和四个网页环境（信息检索与购物任务），通过被动JavaScript追踪器捕获智能体的动作序列及交互时间，训练分类器进行模型身份识别。实验包括跨模型大小/家族泛化、少量轨迹训练、早期帧识别以及随机延时注入防御效果评估。

Result: 智能体动作和交互时序足以将底层模型识别为F1高达96%。分类器可在不同模型规模和家族间泛化；仅需少量交互轨迹即可训练强分类器；智能体身份可在单轮互动初期被识别。随机延时注入会显著降低分类器性能，但针对延时数据重新训练后性能可大幅恢复。

Conclusion: 基于LLM的智能体存在被动模型身份泄露的严重安全隐患，简单延时防御不可靠，需要更鲁棒的防护措施。

Abstract: As LLM-based agents increasingly browse the web on users' behalf, a natural question arises: can websites passively identify which underlying model powers an agent? Doing so would represent a significant security risk, enabling targeted attacks tailored to known model vulnerabilities. Across 14 frontier LLMs and four web environments spanning information retrieval and shopping tasks, we show that an agent's actions and interaction timings, captured via a passive JavaScript tracker, are sufficient to identify the underlying model with up to 96\% F1. We formalise this attack surface by demonstrating that classifiers trained on agent actions generalise across model sizes and families. We further show that strong classifiers can be trained from few interaction traces and that agent identity can be inferred early within an episode. Injecting randomised timing delays between actions substantially degrades classifier performance, but does not provide robust protection: a classifier retrained on delayed traces largely recovers performance. We release our harness and a labelled corpus of agent traces \href{https://github.com/KabakaWilliam/known_actions}{here}.

</details>


### [179] [Toward Securing AI Agents Like Operating Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14932)
*Lukas Pirch,Micha Horlboge,Patrick Großmann,Syeda Mahnur Asif,Klim Kireev,Thorsten Holz,Konrad Rieck*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文从操作系统安全视角系统分析LLM智能体的安全问题，发现现有智能体在资源隔离、权限分离等方面存在严重缺陷，但许多漏洞可通过成熟的OS安全技术缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体系统迅速普及，但其结合了无约束能力与敏感用户数据访问权限，带来重大安全风险，亟需系统性的安全分析框架。

Method: 通过操作系统安全视角审视智能体安全挑战，调研开源智能体生态，推导统一架构，系统分析攻击向量，并对四种OpenClaw类智能体进行案例评估。

Result: 即使在有限的攻击者能力下，现有智能体的多种保护机制在实践中失效；安全运行需要详细的系统知识和谨慎配置；部分智能体能力在设计上固有地不安全。

Conclusion: 许多安全漏洞可通过操作系统安全的成熟技术缓解，并提出了面向智能体系统安全设计的建议。

Abstract: Autonomous agents based on large language models (LLMs) are rapidly emerging as a general-purpose technology, with recent systems such as OpenClaw extending their capabilities through broad tool use, third-party skills, and deeper integration into user environments. At the same time, these agentic systems introduce substantial security risks by combining unconstrained capabilities with access to sensitive user data. In this work, we investigate the security of LLM-based agents through the lens of operating systems. We argue that both face strikingly similar challenges in isolating resources, separating privileges, and mediating communication.
  Guided by this perspective, we survey the current landscape of open-source agents, derive a unified agent architecture, and systematically analyze potential attack vectors. To validate this analysis, we conduct a case study evaluating four widely used OpenClaw-like agents. Even under modest attacker capabilities, we find that several protection mechanisms fail in practice and that secure operation requires detailed system knowledge and careful configuration. However, we also observe that while some agentic capabilities remain insecure by design, many vulnerabilities can be mitigated using well-established techniques from operating system security. We conclude with a set of recommendations for the secure design of agentic systems.

</details>


### [180] [PickleFuzzer: A Case Study in Fuzzing for Discrepancies Between Python Pickle Implementations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15084)
*Justin Applegate,Andreas Kellas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: PickleFuzzer 是一个基于生成式模糊测试的工具，通过差分测试发现 Python 三种原生 pickle 实现之间的不一致性，并识别出 14 个新差异，其中 4 个关键差异可绕过 Hugging Face 等平台的安全扫描工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python 的 pickle 序列化协议存在安全风险，现有防御方法（限制导入、静态/动态分析）依赖于对 Pickle 虚拟机（PVM）操作码的准确解释，而 Python 三种原生 PVM 模块之间的不一致性会导致恶意 payload 检测漏报，破坏现有防御机制。

Method: 设计基于语法的生成式模糊测试工具 PickleFuzzer，通过定义 pickle 对象生成语法（弥补缺失的 pickle 规范），将生成的 pickle 对象分别传递给三个 PVM 实现执行，通过比较异常抛出和关键内部状态变化来检测差异，无需规范衍生的 oracle。

Result: 发现 14 个新差异，其中 4 个为关键差异，可用于绕过 Hugging Face 等模型托管平台的安全扫描工具；已向 Python 软件基金会报告，并因安全漏洞获得漏洞奖励平台 750 美元奖金。

Conclusion: 差分测试是识别 pickle 实现中安全相关不一致性的有效方法，未来可引导更定向的模糊测试以发现更深层的 pickle 漏洞。

Abstract: Python's native serialization protocol, pickle, is a powerful but insecure format for transferring untrusted data. It is frequently used, especially for saving machine learning models, despite known security challenges. While developers sometimes mitigate this risk by restricting imports during unpickling or using static and dynamic analysis tools, these approaches are error-prone and depend heavily on accurate interpretations of the Pickle Virtual Machine (PVM) opcodes. Discrepancies across Python's three native PVM modules can lead to incorrect detection of malicious payloads and undermine existing defenses.
  To efficiently and scalably identify discrepancies, we present PickleFuzzer, a custom generation-based fuzzer that identifies inconsistencies across pickle implementations. PickleFuzzer generates pickle objects, passes them to each implementation, and detects differences in thrown exceptions or changes to key internal states. It generates pickle objects using a grammar, which we developed to account for the missing pickle specification. It determines discrepancies by comparing the execution behaviors of each test implementation, rather than requiring a specification-derived oracle. PickleFuzzer detected 14 new discrepancies between the pickle implementations. Four discrepancies are critical and can be used to bypass security-critical scanning tools like those deployed on the popular model hosting platform, Hugging Face. We disclosed all findings to the Python Software Foundation for remediation, and additionally disclosed the security issues to a bug bounty platform and were awarded a $750 bounty. We demonstrate that differential testing is a viable approach for identifying security-relevant discrepancies in important pickle implementations, and our work can lead to promising future directions for finding deeper pickle bugs with more directed fuzzing.

</details>


### [181] [Talk is (Not) Cheap: A Taxonomy and Benchmark Coverage Audit for LLM Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15118)
*Karthik Raghu Iyer,Yazdan Jamshidi,Nicholas Bray,Alexey A. Shvets*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于STRIDE模型的4×6目标-技术矩阵框架，用于审计LLM攻击基准测试的覆盖范围。该框架源自932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026）构建的507叶分类法，包含2521个攻击组。应用发现六个公开基准测试中，三个主要框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）仅覆盖矩阵25%的非重叠单元格，且整个STRIDE威胁类别（服务中断、模型内部）缺乏标准化评估，同时存在严重的命名碎片化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM攻击基准测试缺乏系统性覆盖评估，个体基准的完整性检验无法揭示集体覆盖范围是否存在盲区。攻击命名碎片化和威胁类别缺失使得安全评估存在结构性漏洞，需要一种基准外部验证机制来度量整体威胁面的覆盖程度。

Method: 从932篇arXiv安全研究（2023-2026）中提取推理时攻击，构建包含507个叶节点（401个数据填充+106个威胁模型推导）的分类法，据此形成4×6目标-技术STRIDE矩阵。将六个公开基准测试映射到该矩阵，计算覆盖率和重叠情况，并分析2521个攻击组的命名分布特性。

Result: 三个主要框架（HarmBench、InjecAgent、AgentDojo）占矩阵最多25%的非重叠单元格；服务中断和模型内部威胁类别完全缺乏标准化评估，而已发表的该类攻击可实现46×令牌放大和96%攻击成功率；单个攻击存在多达29种表面形式；安全与对齐绕过类别高度集中。

Conclusion: 当前LLM攻击基准测试存在显著的覆盖盲区，尤其是服务中断和模型内部威胁类别被系统性忽视。提出的矩阵框架可作为可扩展的评估工具，用于追踪未来基准测试是否逐步缩小评估缺口，促进社区形成对威胁面覆盖的共识。

Abstract: We introduce a reusable framework for auditing whether LLM attack benchmarks collectively cover the threat surface: a 4$\times$6 Target $\times$ Technique matrix grounded in STRIDE, constructed from a 507-leaf taxonomy -- 401 data-populated and 106 threat-model-derived leaves -- of inference-time attacks extracted from 932 arXiv security studies (2023--2026). The matrix enables benchmark-external validation -- auditing collective coverage rather than individual benchmark consistency. Applying it to six public benchmarks reveals that the three primary frameworks (HarmBench, InjecAgent, AgentDojo) occupy non-overlapping cells covering at most 25\% of the matrix, while entire STRIDE threat categories (Service Disruption, Model Internals) lack any standardized evaluation, despite published attacks in these categories achieving 46$\times$ token amplification and 96\% attack success rates through mechanisms which no benchmark tests. The corpus of 2,521 unique attack groups further reveals pervasive naming fragmentation (up to 29 surface forms for a single attack) and heavy concentration in Safety \& Alignment Bypass, structural properties invisible at smaller scale. The taxonomy, attack records, and coverage mappings are released as extensible artifacts; as new benchmarks emerge, they can be mapped onto the same matrix, enabling the community to track whether evaluation gaps are closing.

</details>


### [182] [MetaBackdoor: Exploiting Positional Encoding as a Backdoor Attack Surface in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15172)
*Rui Wen,Mark Russinovich,Andrew Paverd,Jun Sakuma,Ahmed Salem*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaBackdoor，一种利用位置信息（如文本长度）而非内容作为触发信号的新型后门攻击方法，可诱导大语言模型泄露敏感信息或执行恶意工具调用，且可与内容后门组合，扩展了LLM后门攻击的威胁模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM后门攻击依赖内容触发器（如特定词语），但这一假设不必要且限制性强。作者希望探索位置信息作为新的攻击面，揭示Transformer架构中位置编码带来的安全漏洞。

Method: 利用Transformer模型必须编码token位置这一特性，将长度相关的位​​置结构作为非内容触发信号。具体包括：基于长度的位置触发器激活后门；实现敏感信息泄露（如系统提示）、自我激活（多轮对话自然进入触发区域）以及后门组合。

Result: 长度触发器可有效激活隐藏后门；后门模型能在满足长度条件时泄露内部敏感信息；多轮对话可自然进入触发区域诱导恶意工具调用；MetaBackdoor可与内容后门组合形成更精确、更难检测的激活条件。

Conclusion: 位置编码是先前被忽视的攻击面，扩展了LLM后门威胁模型；现有基于文本检测的防御策略无法应对位置触发器，需要新的防御方法。

Abstract: Backdoor attacks pose a serious security threat to large language models (LLMs), which are increasingly deployed as general-purpose assistants in safety- and privacy-critical applications. Existing LLM backdoors rely primarily on content-based triggers, requiring explicit modification of the input text. In this work, we show that this assumption is unnecessary and limiting. We introduce MetaBackdoor, a new class of backdoor attacks that exploits positional information as the trigger, without modifying textual content. Our key insight is that Transformer-based LLMs necessarily encode token positions to process ordered sequences. As a result, length-correlated positional structure is reflected in the model's internal computation and can be used as an effective non-content trigger signal.
  We demonstrate that even a simple length-based positional trigger is sufficient to activate stealthy backdoors. Unlike prior attacks, MetaBackdoor operates on visibly and semantically clean inputs and enables qualitatively new capabilities. We show that a backdoored LLM can be induced to disclose sensitive internal information, including proprietary system prompts, once a length condition is satisfied. We further demonstrate a self-activation scenario, where normal multi-turn interaction can move the conversation context into the trigger region and induce malicious tool-call behavior without attacker-supplied trigger text. In addition, MetaBackdoor is orthogonal to content-based backdoors and can be composed with them to create more precise and harder-to-detect activation conditions.
  Our results expand the threat model of LLM backdoors by revealing positional encoding as a previously overlooked attack surface. This challenges defenses that focus on detecting suspicious text and highlights the need for new defense strategies that explicitly account for positional triggers in modern LLM architectures.

</details>


### [183] [Capacitive Touchscreens at Risk: Recovering Handwritten Trajectory on Smartphone via Electromagnetic Emanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.11484)
*Yukun Cheng,Shiyu Zhu,Changhai Ou,Xingshuo Han,Yuan Li,Shihui Zheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示并利用了电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道漏洞，提出TESLA攻击框架，通过非接触方式捕获书写时产生的电磁信号，实时恢复二维手写轨迹，在商用手机上达到77%字符识别准确率和0.74的Jaccard指数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现电容触摸屏存在严重的电磁侧信道安全漏洞，即其电磁辐射会泄露手写轨迹的细粒度信息，构成隐私威胁。

Method: 提出TESLA非接触攻击框架：捕获用户在触摸屏上书写时产生的电磁信号，通过回归方法将这些信号实时映射为二维手写轨迹。

Result: 在多种商用智能手机上评估，TESLA达到77%的字符识别准确率和0.74的Jaccard指数，能恢复高度可识别的运动轨迹。

Conclusion: 电容触摸屏的电磁侧信道存在严重安全风险，攻击者可在非接触条件下实时窃取手写内容，需引起警惕。

Abstract: This paper reveals and exploits a critical security vulnerability: the electromagnetic (EM) side channel of capacitive touchscreens leaks sufficient information to recover fine-grained, continuous handwriting trajectories. We present Touchscreen Electromagnetic Side-channel Leakage Attack (TESLA), a non-contact attack framework that captures EM signals generated during on-screen writing and regresses them into two-dimensional (2D) handwriting trajectories in real time. Extensive evaluations across a variety of commercial off-the-shelf (COTS) smartphones show that TESLA achieves 77% character recognition accuracy and a Jaccard index of 0.74, demonstrating its capability to recover highly recognizable motion trajectories that closely resemble the original handwriting under realistic attack conditions.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [184] [AIS: Adaptive Importance Sampling for Quantized RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13907)
*Jiajun Zhou,Wei Shao,Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Ngai Wong*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出自适应重要性采样（AIS）方法，用于解决低精度（FP8） rollout与BF16训练器之间的不匹配问题。该不匹配会引入非平稳偏差，早期提供探索红利但后期导致训练崩溃。AIS通过三个实时诊断指标动态调整校正强度，在保留FP8 1.5-2.76倍加速的同时，使性能匹配BF16基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM强化学习受限于rollout生成的高成本，因此采用低精度rollout（如FP8）与BF16训练器搭配以提升吞吐量、降低内存压力。但由此产生的rollout-训练不匹配会偏置策略梯度，严重时导致训练崩溃，亟需一种既能利用低精度加速优势又能消除其负面影响的校正方法。

Method: 提出自适应重要性采样（AIS），通过三个实时诊断指标——权重可靠性、散度严重性和方差放大——计算混合系数，在未校正梯度与完全重要性加权梯度之间插值，压制不稳定偏差分量同时保留探索收益。集成到GRPO中。

Result: 在扩散模型LLaDA-8B-Instruct和自回归模型Qwen3-8B、Qwen3.5-9B的数学推理与规划基准上，AIS在大多数任务上匹配BF16基线性能，同时保持FP8 1.5-2.76倍的rollout加速。

Conclusion: AIS有效解决了低精度rollout带来的非平稳偏差问题，实现了加速与性能的权衡，为LLM强化学习中采用低精度计算提供了可行的校正方案。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) for large language models (LLMs) is dominated by the cost of rollout generation, which has motivated the use of low-precision rollouts (e.g., FP8) paired with a BF16 trainer to improve throughput and reduce memory pressure. This introduces a rollout-training mismatch that biases the policy gradient and can cause training to collapse outright on reasoning benchmarks. We show that the mismatch is non-stationary and acts as a double-edged sword: early in training it provides a stochastic exploration bonus, exposing the gradient to trajectories the trainer would otherwise under-sample, but the same perturbation transitions into a destabilizing source of bias as the policy concentrates.
  To solve this, we propose Adaptive Importance Sampling (AIS), a correction framework that adjusts the strength of its intervention on a per-batch basis. AIS combines three real-time diagnostics, namely weight reliability, divergence severity, and variance amplification, into a single mixing coefficient that interpolates between the uncorrected and fully importance-weighted gradients, suppressing the destabilizing component of the mismatch while preserving its exploratory benefit. We integrate AIS into GRPO and evaluate it on the diffusion-based LLaDA-8B-Instruct and the autoregressive Qwen3-8B and Qwen3.5-9B across mathematical reasoning and planning benchmarks. AIS matches the BF16 baseline on most tasks while retaining the 1.5 to 2.76x rollout speedup of FP8.

</details>


### [185] [Covariance-aware sampling for Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13910)
*Andrea Schioppa,Tim Salimans*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种协方差感知采样器，通过Tweedie公式和结构化傅里叶空间分解显式建模逆向过程协方差，在少步数场景下提升像素空间扩散模型的采样质量，仅需额外一次JVP计算即可超越现有二阶采样器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有少步数采样器仅依赖逆向分布的预测均值，忽略了协方差信息，导致采样质量受限。

Method: 利用Tweedie公式估计协方差，结合结构化傅里叶空间分解对协方差矩阵进行高效分解，作为DDIM的扩展实现，每步仅增加一次Jacobian-Vector Product计算。

Result: 在相同NFE下，该方法在像素级DM上持续生成优于Heun、DPM-Solver++和aDDIM等先进二阶采样器的样本。

Conclusion: 显式建模逆向过程协方差可以有效提升少步数扩散模型采样的质量，且计算开销极小。

Abstract: We present a covariance-aware sampler that improves the quality of pixel-space Diffusion Model (DM) sampling in the few-step regime. We hypothesize that in the few-step regime samplers fail because they rely solely on the predicted mean of the reverse distribution, while our solution explicitly models the reverse-process covariance. Our method combines Tweedie's formula to estimate the covariance with an efficient, structured Fourier-space decomposition of the covariance matrix. Implemented as an extension of DDIM, our method requires only a minimal overhead: one extra Jacobian-Vector Product (JVP) per step. We demonstrate that for pixel-based DMs, our method consistently produces superior samples compared to state-of-the-art second order samplers (Heun, DPM-Solver++) and the recent aDDIM sampler, at an identical number of function evaluations (NFE).

</details>


### [186] [Multi-Scale Dequant: Eliminating Dequantization Bottleneck via Activation Decomposition for Efficient LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13915)
*Lingchao Zheng,Yuwei Fan,Jun Li,Chengqiu Hu,Qichen Liao,Junyi Fan,Rui Shi,Fangzheng Miao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出多尺度反量化框架MSD，通过将高精度激活分解为多个低精度分量，直接与量化后的低比特权重进行矩阵乘法，从而消除LLM推理中反量化操作带来的流水线延迟和算力瓶颈。在INT8和MXFP4两种权重量化格式上，MSD均实现了接近或超越传统反量化方案的精度，同时显著提升推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现代AI加速器（如昇腾NPU）上，反量化操作（将低比特权重恢复为高精度）已成为LLM推理的关键瓶颈。由于计算单元与向量单元分离，反量化操作消耗的时钟周期甚至超过矩阵乘法本身，导致高吞吐张量核心利用率不足。

Method: MSD摒弃了将低比特权重提升至BF16精度的传统做法，转而将高精度BF16激活分解为多个低精度分量，每个分量可直接与量化权重通过硬件加速的GEMM进行乘法运算。计算范式从精度转换转变为多尺度近似，避免了GEMM前的INT8到BF16权重转换。针对INT8权重（W4A16）采用两遍INT8分解达到近16有效比特；针对MXFP4权重（W4A16）采用两遍MXFP4分解达到近6.6有效比特。

Result: MSD避免了反量化导致的Vector-Cube流水线停顿，将注意力机制中KV缓存HBM流量降低至多2.5倍。数值仿真表明MSD在矩阵乘法和Flash Attention核上相比反量化基线不降低精度，且在多数设置下L2误差更低。

Conclusion: MSD通过多尺度分解策略有效消除了LLM推理中的反量化瓶颈，在保持精度的同时显著提升硬件利用率，为量化推理提供了新的高效范式。

Abstract: Quantization is essential for efficient large language model (LLM) inference, yet the dequantization step-converting low-bit weights back to high-precision for matrix multiplication has become a critical bottleneck on modern AI accelerators. On architectures with decoupled compute units (e.g., Ascend NPUs), dequantization operations can consume more cycles than the matrix multiplication itself, leaving the high-throughput tensor cores underutilized. This paper presents Multi-Scale Dequant (MSD), a quantization framework that removes weight/KV dequantization from the GEMM critical path. Instead of lifting low-bit weights to BF16 precision, MSD decomposes high-precision BF16 activations into multiple low-precision components, each of which can be multiplied directly with quantized weights via native hardware-accelerated GEMM. This approach shifts the computational paradigm from precision conversion to multi-scale approximation, avoiding INT8-to-BF16 weight conversion before GEMM. We instantiate MSD for two weight formats and derive tight error bounds for each. For INT8 weights (W4A16), two-pass INT8 decomposition achieves near 16 effective bits. For MXFP4 weights (W4A16), two-pass MXFP4 decomposition yields near 6.6 effective bits with error bound 1/64 per block surpassing single-pass MXFP8(5.24 bits) while maintaining the same effective GEMM compute time. We further derive closed-form latency and HBM traffic models showing that MSD avoids the Vector-Cube pipeline stall caused by dequantization and reduces KV cache HBM traffic by up to 2.5 times in attention. Numerical simulations on matrix multiplication and Flash Attention kernels confirm that MSD does not degrade accuracy compared to dequantization baselines, and in many settings achieves lower L2 error.

</details>


### [187] [A Regret Perspective on Online Multiple Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13916)
*Qingyang Hao,Kongchang Zhou,Fang Kong,Hongxin Wei*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出加权遗憾（Weighted Regret）统一评估在线多重检验（OMT）中假阳性与假阴性的非对称代价，证明确定性程序必然遭受Ω(T)线性遗憾惩罚，并设计出基线无关的元包装器DOMT（解耦OMT），通过历史解耦的随机扰动在突发性环境中实现Ω(√T)阶最优遗憾降低，同时保持精确渐近安全性并约束有限样本误差膨胀。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统在线多重检验（OMT）在评估时孤立地考虑错误发现率（FDR）和统计功效，忽视了现代自动化流程中假阳性和假阴性高度非对称的成本。作者希望引入一个统一指标来同时评估这两种错误代价。

Method: 提出加权遗憾（Weighted Regret）作为统一评价指标；证明遗憾守恒对偶性（Duality of Regret Conservation），即严格FDR控制的确定性程序必然遭受Ω(T)线性遗憾；提出Decoupled-OMT（DOMT）元包装器，通过引入历史解耦的、严格非负的随机扰动来缓解阈值枯竭。

Result: DOMT保持精确渐近安全性，在稳态环境下严格约束有限样本误差膨胀，保证零额外假阴性，在突发性环境中实现Ω(√T)阶最优遗憾降低，并导出"冷启动税"（Cold-Start Tax）刻画算法优越性的精确相变。

Conclusion: 实验验证DOMT能持续降低经验加权遗憾，实现阶最优的次线性阈值枯竭缓解，从而在非平稳帕累托前沿上导航。

Abstract: Online Multiple Testing (OMT), a fundamental pillar of sequential statistical inference, traditionally evaluates the False Discovery Rate (FDR) and statistical power in isolation, obscuring the highly asymmetric costs of false positives and false negatives in modern automated pipelines. To unify this evaluation, we introduce $\textit{Weighted Regret}$. Under this metric, we prove the $\textit{Duality of Regret Conservation}$: purely deterministic procedures ensuring strict FDR control inevitably incur an $Ω(T)$ linear regret penalty, as threshold depletion during signal-sparse cold starts forces massive false negatives. Tailored for exogenous testing streams, we propose Decoupled-OMT (DOMT) as a baseline-agnostic meta-wrapper. By incorporating a history-decoupled, strictly non-negative random perturbation, DOMT rescues purely deterministic baselines from severe threshold depletion. Crucially, it preserves exact asymptotic safety in stationary environments and rigorously bounds finite-sample error inflation during cold-starts. Guaranteeing zero additional false negatives, it yields an order-optimal $Ω(\sqrt{T})$ regret reduction in bursty environments, with a derived ``Cold-Start Tax'' characterizing the exact phase transition of algorithmic superiority. Experiments validate that DOMT consistently curtails empirical weighted regret, achieving an order-optimal sublinear mitigation of threshold depletion to navigate the non-stationary Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [188] [To discretize continually: Mean shift interacting particle systems for Bayesian inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14142)
*Ayoub Belhadji,Daniel Sharp,Youssef M. Marzouk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于交互粒子系统的最小化最大平均差异（MMD）的新方法，用于从非归一化密度中构建加权样本（求积规则），该方法扩展了均值漂移算法，具有归一化常数不变性，支持无梯度和有梯度实现，在多模态混合、贝叶斯层次模型、PDE约束反问题等基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 贝叶斯推断等领域需要从非归一化概率密度中计算期望，而现有方法在高维、多模态等复杂场景下存在收敛慢、模式坍塌等问题。

Method: 通过交互粒子系统最小化目标分布与加权样本之间的MMD，扩展经典均值漂移算法及经验分布最优量化方法到连续分布情形；设计对未知归一化常数具有不变性的动力学，同时支持无梯度（仅需密度评估）和梯度（利用得分函数）两种实现。

Result: 在多种基准问题上验证：收敛速度快，能捕捉各向异性和多模态性，避免模式坍塌，可扩展至高维。

Conclusion: 所提MMD最小化交互粒子系统为贝叶斯推断中的期望近似提供了一种高效、鲁棒且可扩展的新框架。

Abstract: Integration against a probability distribution given its unnormalized density is a central task in Bayesian inference and other fields. We introduce new methods for approximating such expectations with a small set of weighted samples -- i.e., a quadrature rule -- constructed via an interacting particle system that minimizes maximum mean discrepancy (MMD) to the target distribution. These methods extend the classical mean shift algorithm, as well as recent algorithms for optimal quantization of empirical distributions, to the case of continuous distributions. Crucially, our approach creates dynamics for MMD minimization that are invariant to the unknown normalizing constant; they also admit both gradient-free and gradient-informed implementations. The resulting mean shift interacting particle systems converge quickly, capture anisotropy and multi-modality, avoid mode collapse, and scale to high dimensions. We demonstrate their performance on a wide range of benchmark sampling problems, including multi-modal mixtures, Bayesian hierarchical models, PDE-constrained inverse problems, and beyond.

</details>


### [189] [On the Burden of Achieving Fairness in Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14260)
*Ziang Gao,Pengqi Liu,Archer Yi Yang,Mouloud Belbahri,Jesse C. Cresswell,Masoud Asgharian*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文揭示了分裂共形校准中，使用单一合并阈值会导致组间覆盖扭曲，并推导出守恒定律和下界，证明了等覆盖率和等集合大小两种公平定义之间存在根本性张力，量化了组间分离策略与合并策略之间的成本权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 共形预测通常使用单一的合并阈值进行校准，但这可能隐藏评分分布在跨组间的异质性，导致组间覆盖扭曲，影响公平性。

Method: 从分裂共形校准背后的总体评分分布出发，推导守恒定律和下界证明；论证等覆盖率和等集合大小两种公平定义之间的根本性张力；量化分离策略与合并策略之间的成本。

Result: 1) 合并校准会以跨组分位数异质性的规模引入不可约的组间覆盖扭曲；2) 等覆盖率和等集合大小两种公平定义存在根本性张力；3) 校准选择不消除跨组异质性，而是决定扭曲表现在覆盖维度还是大小维度。

Conclusion: 校准选择不是消除跨组异质性的工具，而是决定了扭曲呈现的维度（覆盖 vs. 集合大小），为实践中分析面向公平的校准选择提供了原则性视角。

Abstract: Conformal prediction is often calibrated with a single pooled threshold, but this can hide cross-group heterogeneity in score distributions and distort group-wise coverage. We study this phenomenon through the population score distributions underlying split conformal calibration. First, we derive a conservation law and lower bound showing that pooled calibration incurs irreducible group-wise coverage distortion at a scale set by cross-group quantile heterogeneity. Second, we demonstrate that the two leading fairness definitions for conformal prediction, Equalized Coverage and Equalized Set Size, are fundamentally in tension. Third, we quantify the cost of moving between policies which treat groups separately or pool them. Experiments on synthetic and real data confirm the same bidirectional trade-off after finite-sample calibration. Our results show that, for the policy families studied here, calibration choice does not remove cross-group heterogeneity; it determines whether the resulting distortion appears in the coverage or size dimension, providing a principled lens for analyzing fairness-oriented calibration choices in practice.

</details>


### [190] [Large Dimensional Kernel Ridge Regression: Extending to Product Kernels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14524)
*Yang Zhou,Yicheng Li,Yuqian Cheng,Qian Lin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在更广泛的大维度核函数族中建立了核岭回归的泛化误差收敛率，复现了之前仅在球面内积核等限制性条件下观测到的饱和效应、多重下降行为以及周期平台现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的饱和效应和多重下降行为主要在内积核或超收缩性等强特征函数假设下得到，对于其他核函数是否成立尚不明确，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 建立了一个新的广泛的大维度核函数族，推导了相应的泛化误差收敛率，从而在更一般的设定下分析核岭回归的统计性质。

Result: 1) 当源条件 s≤1 时达到极小最优性；2) 当 s>1 时出现饱和效应；3) 收敛率呈现周期性平台现象，样本量 n 与多重下降行为相关。

Conclusion: 本文表明之前在球面内积核等限制性条件下发现的多种关键现象在更广泛的核函数族中依然成立，拓展了高维核岭回归理论的理解。

Abstract: Recent studies have reported $\textit{saturation effects}$ and $\textit{multiple descent behavior}$ in large dimensional kernel ridge regression (KRR). However, these findings are predominantly derived under restrictive settings, such as inner product kernels on sphere or strong eigenfunction assumptions like hypercontractivity. Whether such behaviors hold for other kernels remains an open question. In this paper, we establish a broad, new family of large dimensional kernels and derive the corresponding convergence rates of the generalization error. As a result, we recover key phenomena previously associated with inner product kernels on sphere, including: $i)$ the $\textit{minimax optimality}$ when the source condition $s\le 1$; $ii)$ the $\textit{saturation effect}$ when $s>1$; $iii)$ a $\textit{periodic plateau phenomenon}$ in the convergence rate and a $\textit {multiple-descent behavior}$ with respect to the sample size $n$.

</details>


### [191] [K-Models: a Flexible and Interpretable Method for Ordinal Clustering with Application to Antigen-Antibody Interaction Profiles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14828)
*Giulia Patanè,Alessandra Menafoglio,Alexander Krauth,Peter Fechner,Luca Dede',Bianca Maria Colosimo,Federica Nicolussi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出K-Models框架，在功能数据聚类中融入序数约束并估计数据生成过程的关键底层元素，在保持与现有技术相当性能的同时显著提升可解释性，并通过模拟实验和生物传感器ROI曲线等实际应用验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有功能数据聚类方法过于注重划分精度而牺牲可解释性，难以在数据生成过程具有特定底层结构且聚类间存在序数关系时提取有意义的洞察。

Method: 提出K-Models框架，将序数约束整合到聚类过程中，并估计驱动观测功能轮廓的随机过程的关键底层元素，从而同时改进可解释性和结构识别能力。

Result: 通过仿真实验和真实世界应用（特别是基于反射式传感器监测生物分子相互作用的ROI曲线数据）进行验证，K-Models在保持与最先进技术相当性能的同时增强了可解释性。

Conclusion: K-Models为分析具有底层序数结构的功能数据提供了一种有价值的工具，通过将结构假设融入聚类过程，在性能和可解释性之间取得了有效平衡。

Abstract: Existing clustering methods for functional data often prioritize partitioning accuracy over interpretability, making it challenging to extract meaningful insights when the data-generating process follows a specific underlying structure and an ordinal relationship among clusters is suspected. This work introduces K-Models, a novel framework that integrates ordinal constraints and estimates key underlying elements of the random process generating the observed functional profiles, improving both interpretability and structure identification. The proposed method is evaluated through simulations and real-world applications. In particular, it is tested on Region of Interest (ROI) curves, which represent reaction profiles from a reflectometric sensor monitoring biomolecular interactions, such as antigen-antibody binding. These curves represent changes in reflected light intensity over time at multiple measurement spots with immobilized antigens during analyte exposure, capturing the binding dynamics of the system. The goal is to identify intrinsic signal patterns solely from the observed dynamics, making this dataset an ideal benchmark for assessing the added interpretability of the proposed approach. By incorporating structural assumptions into the clustering process, K-Models enhances interpretability while maintaining performance comparable to state-of-the-art techniques, providing a valuable tool for analyzing functional data with an underlying ordinal structure.

</details>


### [192] [Average Gradient Outer Product in kernel regression provably recovers the central subspace for multi-index models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15082)
*Libin Zhu,Damek Davis,Dmitriy Drusvyatskiy,Maryam Fazel*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在预测误差仍较大时，通过核岭回归拟合数据并计算平均梯度外积（AGOP）来恢复数据低维中心子空间的问题。理论证明：当目标函数具有低度成分时，子空间恢复所需的样本量远低于准确预测所需的样本量（n ≍ d^{p+δ} vs n ≍ d^{p*}），揭示了预测与表示之间的分离现象，并为迭代核方法（如RFM）的样本效率提供了理论解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究学习器能否在样本量不足以实现准确预测的情况下，仍能发现数据中有用的低维结构；具体而言，研究从有限样本中恢复多指标多项式函数f*(x)=h(Ux)所依赖的未知r维中心子空间的可能性。

Method: 采用核岭回归（KRR）拟合数据，然后从拟合好的预测器中计算平均梯度外积（AGOP），并提取其前r维特征空间作为中心子空间的估计。

Result: 在合理假设下，AGOP的前r维特征空间能够恢复中心子空间，即使预测误差仍然很大。具体地，若目标函数f*具有度p*，准确预测需要n ≍ d^{p*}个样本；而若f*的低度p分量已携带所有相关方向，则子空间恢复仅需n ≍ d^{p+δ}（δ∈(0,1)）个样本，实现了显著的样本效率提升。

Conclusion: 预测与表示之间存在分离现象：低维结构发现（表示学习）所需样本量可远小于准确预测所需样本量。这为迭代核方法（如递归特征机RFM）在实践中具有样本效率提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study a prototypical situation when a learned predictor can discover useful low-dimensional structure in data, while using fewer samples than are needed for accurate prediction. Specifically, we consider the problem of recovering a multi-index polynomial $f^*(x)=h(Ux)$, with $U\in\mathbb{R}^{r\times d}$ and $r\ll d$, from finitely many data/label pairs. Importantly, the target function depends on input $x$ only through the projection onto an unknown $r$-dimensional central subspace. The algorithm we analyze is appealingly simple: fit kernel ridge regression (KRR) to the data and compute the Average Gradient Outer Product (AGOP) from the fitted predictor. Our main results show that under reasonable assumptions the top $r$-dimensional eigenspace of AGOP provably recovers the central subspace, even in regimes when the prediction error remains large. Specifically, if the target function $f^*$ has degree $p^*$, it is known that $n\asymp d^{p^*}$ samples are necessary for KRR to achieve accurate prediction. In contrast, we show that if a low degree $p$ component of $f^*$ already carries all relevant directions for prediction, subspace recovery occurs in the much lower sample regime $n\asymp d^{p+δ}$ for any $δ\in(0,1)$. Our results thus demonstrate a separation between prediction and representation, and provide an explanation for why iterative kernel methods such as Recursive Feature Machines (RFM) can be sample-efficient in practice.

</details>


### [193] [From Data to Action: Accelerating Refinery Optimization with AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15085)
*Dániel Pfeifer,Ábrahám Papp,Tibor Bernáth,Tamás Zoltán Varga,Márk Czifra,Botond Szilágyi,Edith Alice Kovács*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将异常检测方法与线性规划（LP）输出结合使用，以识别炼油厂调度与规划中的数据供应错误和商业机会。通过改进ECOD方法并引入高维数据中选择最相关信息对的新方法，结合2D异常检测算法，在MOL炼油厂的实际应用中取得了成效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 炼油厂优化中使用的LP模型虽然数学上正确，但由于模型简化和数据供应错误，结果的可信度不足。LP求解器缺乏记忆能力，无法利用历史数据提供额外洞察，因此需要新的方法辅助决策。

Method: 提出基于ECOD变换的异常检测方法，并引入高维数据中选择最相关信息对的技术，结合两种2D异常检测算法，与LP输出协同使用。

Result: 在MOL炼油厂的调度与规划架构中，成功识别出多个商业机会和数据供应错误。

Conclusion: 异常检测方法与LP输出结合能有效提升炼油厂运营中数据质量与决策支持能力，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Nowadays refinery optimization utilizes sheer amounts of data, which can be handled with modern Linear Programming (LP) software, but the interpreting and applying the results remains challenging. Large petrochemical companies use massive models, with hundreds of thousands of input matrix elements. The LP solution is mathematically correct, but simplifications are made in the model, and data supply errors may occur. Therefore, further insight is needed to trust the results. The LP solver does not have a memory, so additional understanding could be gained by analyzing historical data and comparing it to the current plan. As such, machine learning approaches were suggested to support decision making based on the LP solution. Among these, Anomaly Detection tools are proposed to be used in tandem with the LP output. A transformed version of the popular ECOD methodology is applied. New methods are proposed to handle high-dimensional data: choosing the most informative pairs. Then, this is used alongside two 2D Anomaly Detection algorithms, revealing several business opportunities and data supply errors in the MOL refinery scheduling and planning architecture.

</details>


### [194] [Logging Policy Design for Off-Policy Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15108)
*Connor Douglas,Joel Persson,Foster Provost*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究如何设计日志策略以最小化离线策略评估(OPE)的误差，刻画了奖励-覆盖率的权衡关系，并在不同信息条件下推导出最优日志策略，为企业选择推荐系统提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线策略评估(OPE)在实际应用中高度依赖于收集数据的日志策略，但目前缺乏系统性的日志策略设计方法。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究如何设计日志策略以最小化对给定目标策略的OPE误差。

Method: 提出统一的日志策略设计框架，在三种信息条件下（已知目标策略和奖励分布、两者均未知、通过先验或噪声估计部分已知）推导最优日志策略。通过刻画奖励-覆盖率的权衡关系（集中概率于高奖励动作可降低方差但可能遗漏目标策略所需信号）来指导策略设计。

Result: 表征了奖励-覆盖率的根本性权衡关系；在三种信息条件下分别推导出最优日志策略；提供了企业选择多个候选推荐系统时的可操作指导；证明了数据收集时处理选择（treatment selection）的重要性；在操作约束下提炼了实用的日志策略设计原则。

Conclusion: 日志策略的设计对OPE准确性至关重要，最优策略取决于可获取的信息量。当无法实现理论最优时，实际设计原则仍可有效指导企业进行日志策略选择。

Abstract: Off-policy evaluation (OPE) estimates the value of a target treatment policy (e.g., a recommender system) using data collected by a different logging policy. It enables high-stakes experimentation without live deployment, yet in practice accuracy depends heavily on the logging policy used to collect data for computing the estimate. We study how to design logging policies that minimize OPE error for given target policies. We characterize a fundamental reward-coverage tradeoff: concentrating probability mass on high-reward actions reduces variance but risks missing signal on actions the target policy may take. We propose a unifying framework for logging policy design and derive optimal policies in canonical informational regimes where the target policy and reward distribution are (i) known, (ii) unknown, and (iii) partially known through priors or noisy estimates at logging time. Our results provide actionable guidance for firms choosing among multiple candidate recommendation systems. We demonstrate the importance of treatment selection when gathering data for OPE, and describe theoretically optimal approaches when this is a firm's primary objective. We also distill practical design principles for selecting logging policies when operational constraints prevent implementing the theoretical optimum.

</details>


### [195] [RoSHAP: A Distributional Framework and Robust Metric for Stable Feature Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15154)
*Lanxin Xiang,Liang Shi,Youhui Ye,Boyu Jiang,Dawei Zhou,Feng Guo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑特征归因随机性的鲁棒归因框架 RoSHAP，通过自助重采样和核密度估计建模SHAP值的分布，利用渐近正态性降低计算成本，实现对特征活跃性、强度和稳定性的综合排序，在仿真和真实实验中优于标准单次归因方法，且用更少特征达到全模型预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有特征归因方法（如SHAP）在随机种子、数据划分等条件下表现出显著的随机变异性，导致归因值和特征排名不稳定，影响模型可解释性和决策可靠性。亟需一种能够刻画归因随机性并给出稳定特征排序的框架。

Method: 1）通过自助重采样（bootstrap）生成多组训练数据，对每组数据计算SHAP值；2）利用核密度估计建模SHAP值的概率分布；3）在温和正则条件下证明聚合归因分数渐近服从高斯分布，从而简化分布估计的计算成本；4）提出RoSHAP指标，综合评估特征的活跃性（active）、强度（strong）和稳定性（stable），实现鲁棒的特征排序。

Result: 在仿真和真实数据实验中，RoSHAP框架在识别信号特征方面优于标准单次运行归因方法；基于RoSHAP选取的特征构建的模型，在显著减少预测变量的同时，预测性能与全特征模型相当。

Conclusion: RoSHAP通过建模归因随机性并引入稳健排序准则，有效提升了机器学习模型特征归因的稳定性和可解释性，为可靠、一致的决策分析提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Feature attribution analysis is critical for interpreting machine learning models and supporting reliable data-driven decisions. However, feature attribution measures often exhibit stochastic variation: different train--test splits, random seeds, or model-fitting procedures can produce substantially different attribution values and feature rankings. This paper proposes a framework for incorporating stochastic nature of feature attribution and a robust attribution metric, RoSHAP, for stable feature ranking based on the SHAP metric. The proposed framework models the distribution of feature attribution scores and estimates it through bootstrap resampling and kernel density estimation. We show that, under mild regularity conditions, the aggregated feature attribution score is asymptotically Gaussian, which greatly reduces the computational cost of distribution estimation. The RoSHAP summarizes the distribution of SHAP into a robust feature-ranking criterion that simultaneously rewards features that are active, strong, and stable. Through simulations and real-data experiments, the proposed framework and RoSHAP outperform standard single-run attribution measures in identifying signal features. In addition, models built using RoSHAP-selected features achieve predictive performance comparable to full-feature models while using substantially fewer predictors. The proposed RoSHAP approach improves the stability and interpretability of machine learning models, enabling reliable and consistent insights for analysis.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [196] [Double/debiased machine learning of quantile treatment effects on long-term outcomes in clinical trials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14275)
*Ziyang Liu,Niwen Zhou,Peng Wu,Xu Guo*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合随机试验数据与外部观察数据来估计长期结局分位处理效应的方法，并开发了双稳健估计量，在有限样本中表现良好，能揭示跨分位的异质性长期处理效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机临床试验中长期结局通常不可得，而短期替代结局虽常被观察但信息有限；外部观察数据虽包含长期结局但存在混杂偏倚。现有基于替代结局的数据整合方法仅关注平均处理效应，无法揭示处理效应的异质性分布特征。

Method: 在治疗随机化、阳性假设和基于替代结局的可传输性假设下建立识别条件；开发双稳健估计量进行推断，该估计量可容纳灵活的机器学习方法进行 nuisance 估计，只要评分相关或结局回归相关的 nuisance 函数之一被一致估计，估计量就能保持一致性，且在正则条件下渐近正态。

Result: 仿真和真实数据结果表明，所提方法在有限样本中表现良好，能够揭示不同分位点上的异质性长期处理效应。

Conclusion: 本文成功将替代结局数据整合方法从平均处理效应扩展到分位处理效应估计，为利用外部观察数据推断试验人群长期结局的异质性因果关系提供了理论基础和实用工具。

Abstract: Long-term outcomes are often unavailable in randomized clinical trials, although short-term surrogate outcomes are commonly observed. External observational data may contain the long-term outcome, but causal comparisons based on such data alone are vulnerable to confounding. Existing surrogate-based data integration methods for long-term outcomes have focused primarily on average treatment effects. We study estimation of quantile treatment effects for long-term outcomes in the trial population by combining randomized trial data with external observational data. Under treatment randomization, positivity, and a surrogate-based transportability assumption, we establish identification and develop a doubly robust estimator for inference. The estimator accommodates flexible machine learning methods for nuisance estimation, remains consistent if either the score-related or outcome regression-related nuisance functions are consistently estimated, and is asymptotically normal under regularity conditions. Simulation and real-data results demonstrate that the proposed method performs well in finite samples and can reveal heterogeneous long-term treatment effects across quantiles.

</details>


### [197] [Asymptotic Anytime-Valid Inference for U-statistics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14692)
*Leheng Cai,Qirui Hu,Weijia Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了连续监测下二阶U-统计量的渐近任意时有效置信序列。非退化情形下利用Hoeffding投影归约为部分和的时间一致中心极限定理；退化情形下提出SAGE边界，基于截断谱估计实现数据驱动。宽度达到时间一致最优速率：非退化$\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$，退化$\log\log n/n$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在连续监测的场景下，传统置信区间无法保证任意停止时间下的有效性，而U-统计量作为广泛使用的统计量（如方差、相关系数、AUC等），缺乏时间一致有效的序贯推断方法。尤其是退化情形下，极限分布为非高斯，给序贯推断带来重大挑战。

Method: 非退化情形：利用Hoeffding投影将U-统计量分解为线性主部与可忽略余项，结合时间一致CLT与留一法Jackknife估计实现数据驱动的置信序列。退化情形：证明U-统计量近似为中心化二次高斯混沌，提出谱分配高斯混沌游走（SAGE）边界，并基于截断谱估计实现可操作的插件法程序。

Result: 非退化情形下置信序列宽度达$\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$，退化情形下达$\log\log n/n$，均达到时间一致最优速率。数值实验验证了理论有效性。

Conclusion: 建立了二阶U-统计量在连续监测下的完整序贯推断框架，分别处理了非退化与退化两种情形，提供了理论最优的置信序列构造方法，并通过数值实验支持了方法的实用性。

Abstract: We study asymptotic anytime-valid confidence sequences for degree-two U-statistics under continuous monitoring. In the nondegenerate case, Hoeffding's projection reduces the problem to a time-uniform central limit theory for the partial sums of the first-order projection, while the canonical remainder is shown to be negligible under mild moment assumptions. A leave-one-out jackknife estimator then yields a fully data-driven procedure, leading to confidence sequences with asymptotic coverage guarantee for the parameter of interest. In the degenerate case, we show that the U-statistic is approximated by a centered quadratic Gaussian-chaos rather than by a simple Gaussian, which poses significant challenges for sequential inference. To address this issue, we novelly develop the Spectrally Allocated Gaussian-chaos Excursion (SAGE) boundary, and then provide plug-in implementations based on truncated spectrum estimation with consistency guarantees. The resulting widths can attain the expected time-uniform optimal rates: $\sqrt{\log\log n/n}$ in the nondegenerate regime and $\log\log n/n$ in the degenerate regime. Several widely used U-statistics are discussed within the proposed framework, and numerical experiments further support the validity of the derived theory.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [198] [Unsupervised learning of acquisition variability in structural connectomes via hybrid latent space modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13933)
*Gaurav Rudravaram,Lianrui Zuo,Karthik Ramadass,Elyssa McMaster,Jongyeon Yoon,Aravind R. Krishnan,Adam M. Saunders,Chenyu Gao,Nancy R. Newlin,Praitayini Kanakaraj,Lori L. Beason Held,Murat Bilgel,Laura A. Barquero,Micah DArchangel,Tin Q. Nguyen,Laurie B. Cutting,Derek Archer,Timothy J. Hohman,Daniel C. Moyer,Bennett A. Landman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种无需手动调参的无监督框架（Joint-VAE），通过架构退火（architectural annealing）实现连续-离散混合潜在空间建模，在7,416个结构连接组数据集上有效分离了dMRI采集效应（扫描仪/协议差异，ARI=0.53）与生物学变异（年龄/疾病状态）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dMRI中不同站点、扫描仪和协议带来的采集差异给结构连接组分析引入变异性，传统降维方法将所有方差建模为连续型，导致采集效应被吸收进连续潜在空间；现有混合潜在空间模型虽能分离离散和连续成分，但需要手动调整容量使得离散成分捕获预期变异。

Method: 提出Joint-VAE框架：在编码器输出后、解码器前引入架构退火（architectural annealing），自适应平衡离散和连续潜在变量；不通过损失函数退火，而是直接对编码器输出进行结构化的退火处理。数据集包含N=7,416个结构连接组（dMRI），年龄2-102岁，13个研究、25种采集参数组合，含认知正常（5,900）、MCI（877）和AD（639）样本。对比基线：标准VAE、PCA+k-means、仅通过损失函数退火的混合模型。

Result: 架构退火在站点学习上显著优于基线（ARI=0.53, p<0.05）；Joint-VAE恢复出的聚类与扫描仪和协议差异对齐，有效分离了采集相关变异与生物变异。

Conclusion: 混合连续-离散潜在空间+架构退火（而非损失退火）提供了一种有效的无监督机制来捕获dMRI中的采集变异性，在联合建模平滑和类别化结构的同时，无需手动容量调参。

Abstract: Acquisition differences across sites, scanners, and protocols in dMRI introduce variability that complicates structural connectome analysis. This motivates deep learning models that can represent high-dimensional connectomes in a low-dimensional space while explicitly separating acquisition-related effects from biological variation. Conventional dimensionality reduction methods model all variance as continuous, so acquisition effects often get absorbed into a continuous latent space. Recent hybrid latent-space models combine discrete and continuous components to address this, but typically require manual capacity tuning to ensure the discrete component captures the intended variability. We introduce an unsupervised framework that removes this manual tuning by architecturally annealing encoder outputs before decoding, allowing the model to adaptively balance discrete and continuous latent variables during training. To evaluate it, we curated a dataset of N=7,416 structural connectomes derived from dMRI, spanning ages 2 to 102 and 13 studies with 25 unique acquisition-parameter combinations. Of these, 5,900 are cognitively unimpaired, 877 have mild cognitive impairment (MCI), and 639 have Alzheimer's disease (AD). We compare against a standard VAE, PCA with k-means clustering, and hybrid models that anneal only through the loss function. Our architectural annealing produces stronger site learning (ARI=0.53, p<0.05) than these baselines. Results show that a hybrid continuous-discrete latent space, with architectural rather than loss-based annealing, provides a useful unsupervised mechanism for capturing acquisition variability in dMRI: by jointly modeling smooth and categorical structure, the Joint-VAE recovers clusters aligned with scanner and protocol differences.

</details>


### [199] [TabPFN-3: Technical Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13986)
*Léo Grinsztajn,Klemens Flöge,Oscar Key,Felix Birkel,Philipp Jund,Brendan Roof,Mihir Manium,Shi Bin,Hoo,Magnus Bühler,Anurag Garg,Dominik Safaric,Jake Robertson,Benjamin Jäger,Simone Alessi,Adrian Hayler,Vladyslav Moroshan,Lennart Purucker,Philipp Singer,Alan Arazi,Julien Siems,Jan Hendrik Metzen,Georg Grab,Nick Erickson,Siyuan Guo,Eliott Kalfon,Simon Bing,David Salinas,Clara Cornu,Lilly Charlotte Wehrhahn,Diana Kriuchkova,Kursat Kaya,Lydia Sidhoum,Marie Salmon,Jerry Chen,Madelon Hulsebos,Yann LeCun,Samuel Müller,Bernhard Schölkopf,Sauraj Gambhir,Noah Hollmann,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TabPFN-3 是首个扩展到百万级训练样本、大幅提升速度与性能的表格数据基础模型，在 TabArena 等基准上显著超越所有现有模型，并首次在表格基础模型中引入测试时计算缩放，支持时间序列、关系数据和表格-文本数据，推理速度比前代快 20 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格数据是科学和工业中高价值预测问题的核心；尽管 TabPFN 开启了该领域的基础模型革命，但现有模型在样本规模（难以扩展到百万级）、效率（训练和推理时间长）以及多样性数据（时间序列、关系数据、表格-文本数据）处理上仍存在明显局限。本文旨在通过 TabPFN-3 克服这些瓶颈。

Method: TabPFN-3 完全在基于先验的合成数据上预训练；引入行分块（row-chunking）和精简 KV 缓存机制，支持单张 H100 上处理 100 万行；首次在表格基础模型中实现测试时计算缩放（test-time compute scaling）；针对时间序列推出专用检查点 TabPFN-TS-3。

Result: 在 TabArena 上，TabPFN-3 前向传播显著超越所有调优/集成基线，在速度-性能帕累托前沿上占优；在多类别数据集上排名第一；在百万行、200 特征数据集上击败 8 小时调优的 GBDT 基线；TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) 在 TabArena 上以超 200 Elo 领先所有非 TabPFN 模型，在大数据子集上领先 420 Elo；比 AutoGluon 1.5 extreme 快 10 倍；在 RelBenchV1 上达到新 SOTA；在 TabSTAR 上 SOTA；TabPFN-TS-3 在 fev-bench 排名第二；SHAP 值计算加速最高 120 倍；整体推理速度比 TabPFN-2.5 快 20 倍。

Conclusion: TabPFN-3 大幅推进了表格数据预测的前沿，在规模、速度、准确性和多模态泛化能力上取得突破性进展，证明了纯合成数据预训练+测试时计算缩放在表格基础模型中的巨大潜力，为工业级大规模表格预测任务提供了高效可行的基础模型解决方案。

Abstract: Tabular data underpins most high-value prediction problems in science and industry, and TabPFN has driven the foundation model revolution for this modality. Designed with feedback from our users, TabPFN-3 builds on this foundation to scale state-of-the-art performance to datasets with 1M training rows and substantially reduce training and inference time. Pretrained exclusively on synthetic data from our prior, TabPFN-3 dramatically pushes the frontier of tabular prediction and brings substantial gains on time series, relational, and tabular-text data. On the standard tabular benchmark TabArena, a forward pass of TabPFN-3 outperforms all other models, including tuned and ensembled baselines, by a significant margin, and pareto-dominates the speed/performance frontier. On more diverse datasets, TabPFN-3 ranks first on datasets with many classes, and beats 8-hour-tuned gradient-boosted-tree baselines on datasets up to 1M training rows and 200 features. TabPFN-3 introduces test-time compute scaling to tabular foundation models. Our API offering TabPFN-3-Plus (Thinking) exploits this to beat all non-TabPFN models by over 200 Elo on TabArena, rising to 420 Elo on the largest data subset, and outperforms AutoGluon 1.5 extreme while being 10x faster, without using LLMs, real data, internet search or any other model besides TabPFN. TabPFN-3 extends the capabilities of our models, enabling SOTA prediction on relational data (new SOTA foundation model on RelBenchV1) and tabular-text data (SOTA on TabSTAR via TabPFN-3-Plus); and improves existing integrations: a specialized checkpoint, TabPFN-TS-3, ranks 2nd on the time-series benchmark fev-bench, and SHAP-value computation is up to 120x faster. TabPFN-3 achieves this performance while being up to 20x faster than TabPFN-2.5. In addition, a reduced KV cache and row-chunking scale to 1M rows on one H100 with fast inference speed.

</details>


### [200] [Finite Sample Bounds for Learning with Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14168)
*Devin Smedira,Abhijith Jayakumar,Sidhant Misra,Marc Vuffray,Andrey Y. Lokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次提供了使用分数匹配学习多项式指数族分布的非渐近样本复杂度分析，证明了样本界对模型维度的多项式依赖性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数匹配在高维连续指数族学习中计算高效且被广泛使用，但其统计性质的理论理解仍然缺乏，特别是样本复杂度的非渐近分析尚属空白。

Method: 对分数匹配方法进行非渐近样本复杂度分析，推导了学习多项式指数族结构所需的样本界。

Result: 导出的样本界对模型维度呈多项式依赖关系，且是首个此类非渐近界。

Conclusion: 该工作填补了分数匹配非渐近理论分析的空白，为高维统计中连续指数族分布的学习提供了理论保障。

Abstract: Learning of continuous exponential family distributions with unbounded support remains an important area of research for both theory and applications in high-dimensional statistics. In recent years, score matching has become a widely used method for learning exponential families with continuous variables due to its computational ease when compared against maximum likelihood estimation. However, theoretical understanding of the statistical properties of score matching is still lacking. In this work, we provide a non-asymptotic sample complexity analysis for learning the structure of exponential families of polynomials with score matching. The derived sample bounds show a polynomial dependence on the model dimension. These bounds are the first of its kind, as all prior work has shown only asymptotic bounds on the sample complexity.

</details>


### [201] [Angel or Demon: Investigating the Plasticity Interventions' Impact on Backdoor Threats in Deep Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14587)
*Oubo Ma,Ruixiao Lin,Yang Dai,Jiahao Chen,Chunyi Zhou,Linkang Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了14,664个案例，发现现代DRL中的可塑性干预措施中，只有SAM会加剧后门威胁，而其他干预措施则会缓解后门威胁。病理分析揭示加剧源于后门梯度放大，缓解源于激活路径中断和表示空间压缩。据此提出后门注入的SCC概念框架，并发现异常损失景观锐度可作为DRL后门检测的关键指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往研究主要关注vanilla场景下的DRL后门攻击，而可塑性干预作为现代DRL智能体的内置组件，其对后门脆弱性的影响尚未被系统研究，这种缺失对实际DRL部署构成风险。

Method: 对14,664个案例进行实证研究，整合代表性可塑性干预措施和后门攻击场景；通过病理分析（后门梯度放大、激活路径中断、表示空间压缩）揭示机制；提出SCC概念框架和基于损失景观锐度的后门检测方法。

Result: SAM是唯一加剧后门威胁的干预措施；其他干预措施均缓解后门威胁；加剧机制为后门梯度放大；缓解机制为激活路径中断和表示空间压缩。

Conclusion: 提出了SCC（后门注入的概念框架）用于解构可塑性干预与后门之间的机制交互；发现异常损失景观锐度可作为DRL后门检测的关键指标。

Abstract: Extensive research has highlighted the severe threats posed by backdoor attacks to deep reinforcement learning (DRL). However, prior studies primarily focus on vanilla scenarios, while plasticity interventions have emerged as indispensable built-in components of modern DRL agents. Despite their effectiveness in mitigating plasticity loss, the impact of these interventions on DRL backdoor vulnerabilities remains underexplored, and this lack of systematic investigation poses risks in practical DRL deployments. To bridge this gap, we empirically study 14,664 cases integrating representative interventions and attack scenarios. We find that only one intervention (i.e., SAM) exacerbates backdoor threats, while other interventions mitigate them. Pathological analysis identifies that the exacerbation is attributed to backdoor gradient amplification, while the mitigation stems from activation pathway disruption and representation space compression. From these findings, we derive two novel insights: (1) a conceptual framework SCC for robust backdoor injection that deconstructs the mechanistic interplay between interventions and backdoors in DRL, and (2) abnormal loss landscape sharpness as a key indicator for DRL backdoor detection.

</details>


### [202] [Policy Optimization in Hybrid Discrete-Continuous Action Spaces via Mixed Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14297)
*Matias Alvo,Daniel Russo,Yash Kanoria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出混合策略优化（HPO）方法，针对混合离散-连续动作空间的强化学习问题，通过结合路径梯度和得分函数梯度构建无偏混合梯度估计器，在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题中显著优于PPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法面临两难：基于得分函数的策略梯度方法在高维连续动作空间中存在严重的信用分配问题，梯度质量差；而可微模拟反向传播虽好，但离散动作或非光滑动力学导致梯度有偏或无效。需要一种能兼顾离散和连续动作、保持梯度无偏性的方法。

Method: 提出混合策略优化（HPO），在光滑区域通过可微模拟反向传播（路径梯度），在非光滑或离散部分使用得分函数梯度，构建混合梯度估计器，保证无偏性。同时将具有动作不连续性的问题重新表述为混合形式以扩大适用性。

Result: 在库存控制和切换线性二次调节器问题上，HPO显著优于PPO，且随着连续动作维度增加，性能差距进一步扩大。混合梯度的交叉项在接近离散最优响应时可忽略，从而支持连续和离散组件的近似解耦更新，降低最优解附近的方差。

Conclusion: HPO有效解决了混合离散-连续动作空间的强化学习挑战，通过混合梯度策略兼顾了可微模拟的高效性和得分函数的普适性，理论分析和实验均验证了其优势。

Abstract: We study reinforcement learning in hybrid discrete-continuous action spaces, such as settings where the discrete component selects a regime (or index) and the continuous component optimizes within it -- a structure common in robotics, control, and operations problems. Standard model-free policy gradient methods rely on score-function (SF) estimators and suffer from severe credit-assignment issues in high-dimensional settings, leading to poor gradient quality. On the other hand, differentiable simulation largely sidesteps these issues by backpropagating through a simulator, but the presence of discrete actions or non-smooth dynamics yields biased or uninformative gradients. To address this, we propose Hybrid Policy Optimization (HPO), which backpropagates through the simulator wherever smoothness permits, using a mixed gradient estimator that combines pathwise and SF gradients while maintaining unbiasedness. We also show how problems with action discontinuities can be reformulated in hybrid form, further broadening its applicability. Empirically, HPO substantially outperforms PPO on inventory control and switched linear-quadratic regulator problems, with performance gaps increasing as the continuous action dimension grows. Finally, we characterize the structure of the mixed gradient, showing that its cross term -- which captures how continuous actions influence future discrete decisions -- becomes negligible near a discrete best response, thereby enabling approximate decentralized updates of the continuous and discrete components and reducing variance near optimality. All resources are available at github.com/MatiasAlvo/hybrid-rl.

</details>


### [203] [Language-Induced Priors for Domain Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14301)
*Qiyuan Chen,Jiayu Zhou,Raed Al Kontar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出语言诱导先验（LIP）框架，利用预训练大语言模型从目标域文本描述中提取偏好信息，通过期望最大化算法识别源域相关性，解决了冷启动场景下源域选择困难导致的负迁移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 域适应中存在根本性悖论：冷启动阶段目标数据稀缺时，统计方法无法区分相关与无关源域，常导致负迁移。现有方法忽视了目标域文本描述这一可用的先验资源。

Method: 提出概率框架LIP，将目标域语义描述转化为选择模型，从预训练LLM中学习偏好；将LIP集成到EM算法中以识别源域相关性；框架与任意参数化模型兼容，当目标信号弱时LIP引导源选择，样本积累后逐步细化。

Result: 理论上证明，在正确先验下估计器近似匹配冷启动MSE的oracle界，且无论LIP质量如何均保持渐近一致性；在描述性（高斯估计）、预测性（C-MAPSS数据集）、规定性（MuJoCo hopper）三类任务上均验证了有效性。

Conclusion: LIP框架有效利用LLM从文本描述中提取先验知识，解决了冷启动域适应中的源域选择难题，兼具理论保障与实证效果，且具有广泛的模型兼容性。

Abstract: Domain adaptation faces a fundamental paradox in the cold-start regime. When target data is scarce, statistical methods fail to distinguish relevant source domains from irrelevant ones, which often leads to negative transfer. In this paper, we address this challenge by leveraging expert textual descriptions of the target domain, a resource that is often available but overlooked. We propose a probabilistic framework that translates these semantic descriptions into a choice model, namely a Language-Induced Prior (LIP), that learns the preferences from a pretrained Large Language Model (LLM). The LIP is then integrated into an Expectation-Maximization algorithm to identify source relevance. Methodologically, this framework is compatible with any parametric model where a likelihood is available. It allows the LIP to guide the selection of sources when target signals are weak, while gradually refining these choices as samples accumulate. Theoretically, we prove that the estimator roughly matches an oracle cold-start MSE under a correct prior, while remaining asymptotically consistent regardless of the quality of the LIP. Empirically, we validated the framework on a descriptive (Gaussian estimation), a predictive (C-MAPSS dataset), and a prescriptive task (MuJoCo hopper).

</details>


### [204] [Nearest-Neighbor Radii under Dependent Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14343)
*Yuanyuan Gao,Yilong Hou,Zhexiao Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了相依采样（强混合依赖观测）下最近邻半径的几何性质，建立了多项式混合下的无分布几乎必然收敛性和几何混合下的非渐近矩界，这些结果依赖于局部内在维度而非环境维度，并通过合成实验和真实时间序列基准验证了理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典和现代机器学习中最近邻方法通常假设独立采样，但实际数据往往存在时间或空间依赖。本文旨在探究相依采样是否会改变最近邻邻域的尺度，填补依赖数据下最近邻几何性质理论分析的空白。

Method: 采用强混合依赖模型（多项式混合和几何混合），推导最近邻半径的分布-free几乎必然收敛性和非渐近矩界；理论分析利用了局部内在维度而非环境维度，适用于高维流形数据。

Result: 建立了多项式混合条件下的几乎必然收敛性，以及几何混合条件下的非渐近矩界（依赖于局部内在维度）；合成实验和真实时间序列基准验证了理论结果，表明依赖采样下最近邻几何仍保持信息性。

Conclusion: 依赖采样不会改变最近邻半径的尺度性质，最近邻几何在相依观测下仍然有效且信息丰富，为依赖数据场景下的最近邻方法提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Nearest-neighbor methods are fundamental to classical and modern machine learning, yet their geometric properties are typically analyzed under independent sampling. In this paper, we study the nearest-neighbor radii under dependent sampling. We consider strong mixing dependent observations and ask whether dependence changes the scale of nearest-neighbor neighborhoods. We establish distribution-free almost sure convergence under polynomial mixing and sharp non-asymptotic moment bounds under geometric mixing. The moment bounds depend on the local intrinsic dimension rather than the ambient dimension, making the results applicable to high-dimensional data concentrated near lower-dimensional manifolds. Synthetic experiments and real-world time-series benchmarks support the theory, showing that nearest-neighbor geometry remains informative under dependence sampling.

</details>


### [205] [Fast Rates for Inverse Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14599)
*Andreas Schlaginhaufen,Maryam Kamgarpour*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对有限时域、Borel状态和动作空间的MDP中，具有线性奖励类别的熵正则化最小-最大逆强化学习（Min-Max-IRL）建立了新的结构和统计结果。结构上证明了MLE与Min-Max-IRL在总体水平以及确定性动力学下的经验水平等价；统计上利用伪自协和性证明了轨迹级KL散度和Hessian范数下的平方参数误差以$\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$快速衰减，且保证在模型误设定下成立且无需探索假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为熵正则化Min-Max-IRL建立严格的结构等价性（与MLE的关系）和快速统计收敛率，并在一般Borel空间上扩展奖励可辨识性结果，同时放宽对探索假设的要求。

Method: 利用伪自协和性（pseudo-self-concordance）分析Min-Max-IRL损失函数的几何性质，结合线性奖励函数类与有限时域MDP模型，在总体和确定性动力学经验水平上建立MLE等价性，并推导收敛率。

Result: 轨迹级KL散度和Hessian范数下的平方参数误差均以$\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$的快速率衰减；奖励可辨识性结果推广至一般Borel空间；导数分析获得软最优值函数关于奖励参数的导数新结果。

Conclusion: 熵正则化Min-Max-IRL在理论上具有与MLE等价的结构和快速统计收敛性，适用于模型误设定且无需探索假设，为逆强化学习提供了坚实的理论保障。

Abstract: We establish novel structural and statistical results for entropy-regularized min-max inverse reinforcement learning (Min-Max-IRL) with linear reward classes in finite-horizon MDPs with Borel state and action spaces. On the structural side, we show that maximum likelihood estimation (MLE) and Min-Max-IRL are equivalent at the population level, and at the empirical level under deterministic dynamics.
