On the statistical side, exploiting pseudo-self-concordance of the Min-Max-IRL loss, we prove that both the trajectory-level KL divergence and the squared parameter error in the Hessian norm decay at the fast rate $\mathcal{O}(n^{-1})$, where $n$ is the number of expert trajectories. Our guarantees apply under misspecification and require no exploration assumptions. We further extend reward-identifiability results to general Borel spaces and derive novel results on the derivatives of the soft-optimal value function with respect to reward parameters.

</details>


### [206] [In-Context Learning for Data-Driven Censored Inventory Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14840)
*Sohom Mukherjee,Anh-Duy Pham,Richard Pibernik,Yunbei Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为ICGPS的在线库存控制方法，通过离线元训练的生成模型（ChronosFlow）在上下文中进行后验采样，以解决决策依赖型审查（newsvendor问题）。理论上证明了其贝叶斯遗憾界由TS基准遗憾与部署惩罚项构成，且离线预测质量可迁移至在线性能。实验表明ICGPS在鲁棒性和性能上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于参数化Thompson采样(TS)的方法在先验不匹配时脆弱，而离线插补方法难以迁移到在线学习场景。作者希望结合两者的优势，通过学习的潜在需求补全来采取oracle动作，从而解决决策依赖型审查下的库存控制问题。

Method: 提出上下文生成后验采样(ICGPS)：离线元训练生成模型，在线通过上下文自回归生成进行后验采样。理论分析将贝叶斯遗憾分解为理想补全核下的TS基准遗憾加上部署惩罚项（O(√T)倍补全失配平方根）。通过将审查反馈降级为bandit凸优化反馈，导出R-NV的亚线性贝叶斯遗憾。实例化ChronosFlow-ICGPS：冻结时序Transformer主干+可训练条件归一化流头部，实现快速审查一致采样。

Result: 理论：建立了ICGPS的贝叶斯遗憾界，证明了在合理覆盖和稳定性假设下在线补全失配受离线审查预测失配控制。实验：ChronosFlow-ICGPS匹配正确指定的TS，优于短视和UCB基线，对先验不匹配和分布偏移鲁棒，在真实SuperStore数据集上尤其在高审查率下表现良好。

Conclusion: ICGPS提供了一种将离线生成式预训练与在线决策相结合的通用模板，能够将离线预测质量有效迁移到在线性能，既具备TS的理论保证，又克服了其对先验敏感的缺陷。

Abstract: We study inventory control with decision-dependent censoring, focusing on the censored or repeated newsvendor (R-NV), where each order quantity determines whether demand is fully observed or censored by sales. Existing approaches based on parametric Thompson sampling (TS) can be brittle under prior mismatch, while offline imputation methods need not transfer to online learning. Motivated by the predictive view of decision making, we combine these ideas by taking oracle actions on learned completions of latent demand. We propose in-context generative posterior sampling (ICGPS), which uses modern generative models that are meta-trained offline and deployed online by in-context autoregressive generation. Theoretically, we show that the Bayesian regret of ICGPS with a learned completion kernel is bounded by the Bayesian regret of a TS benchmark with the ideal completion kernel plus a deployment penalty scaling as $\sqrt{T}$ times the square root of the completion mismatch. This yields a plug-in template for operational problems with known TS regret bounds. For R-NV, we derive sublinear Bayesian regret by reducing censored feedback to bandit convex optimization feedback. We also show that, under reasonable coverage and stability assumptions, the online completion mismatch is controlled by the offline censored predictive mismatch, so offline predictive quality transfers to online performance. Practically, we instantiate ICGPS with ChronosFlow, which combines a frozen time-series transformer backbone with a trainable conditional normalizing-flow head for fast censoring-consistent sampling. In benchmark experiments, ChronosFlow-ICGPS matches correctly specified TS, outperforms myopic and UCB-style baselines, and is robust to prior mismatch and distribution shift. ChronosFlow-ICGPS also performs well for the real-world SuperStore dataset, especially under heavy censoring.

</details>


### [207] [InfoSFT: Learn More and Forget Less with Information-Aware Token Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14967)
*Mahdi Sabbaghi,George Pappas,Adel Javanmard,Hamed Hassani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: InfoSFT提出了一种基于信息论原理的权重方案，通过聚焦于中等置信度（既不太熟悉也不太陌生）的令牌来改进监督微调，仅需一行代码修改即可在数学、代码和思维链任务上提升泛化能力，同时更好地保留预训练能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准SFT均匀拟合所有样本，包括那些在基模型下似然度低的样本，这会导致对特定样本的过拟合而非学习目标行为，并因过大的策略偏移而损害已有能力。现有方法通过过滤或降权低似然数据来缓解，但往往抑制了基模型尚未学习的新行为。

Method: InfoSFT提出一种基于信息论的加权方案，为SFT目标函数中的每个令牌分配权重，聚焦于信息量最大、中等置信度的令牌——既不过于熟悉（高似然）也不过于不稳定（低似然）。该方法仅需对标准令牌级损失函数进行一行代码修改。

Result: 在数学、代码和思维链任务上，使用多种模型家族，InfoSFT相比标准SFT和基于似然加权的基线方法，展现了更好的泛化性能，同时更好地保留了模型原有的预训练能力。

Conclusion: InfoSFT通过简单的一行代码修改，提供了一种有效的替代标准SFT的方案，在提升目标行为学习的同时减少对已有能力的干扰。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) provides the standard approach for teaching LLMs new behaviors from offline expert demonstrations. However, standard SFT uniformly fits all samples -- including those with low likelihood under the base model -- which can disproportionately drive training updates toward overfitting specific samples rather than learning the target behavior. Moreover, adapting to these unlikely samples induces substantial policy shifts that degrade prior capabilities. Existing methods mitigate this by filtering, regenerating, or down-weighting low-likelihood data. In doing so, they often suppress precisely the novel behaviors the base model has yet to learn.
  We propose InfoSFT, a principled weighting scheme for the SFT objective that concentrates learning signals on maximally informative, medium-confidence tokens -- those neither overly familiar to the base model nor too unlikely to cause instability. Requiring only a one-line modification to the standard token-wise loss, InfoSFT demonstrably improves generalization over vanilla SFT and likelihood-weighted baselines across math, code, and chain-of-thought tasks with diverse model families, while better preserving pre-existing capabilities.

</details>


### [208] [Vision-Based Runtime Monitoring under Varying Specifications using Semantic Latent Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13923)
*Bardh Hoxha,Oliver Schön,Hideki Okamoto,Lars Lindemann,Georgios Fainekos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了在部分可观测条件下，基于视觉观测对过去时间信号时态逻辑（ptSTL）进行认证运行时监控。提出了两种可重用接口：语义基向量（语义基监测器）和滚动预测监测器，前者通过单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口实现任意公式的确定性解码与单次共形校准，后者仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构时态历史。实验表明，滚动监测器在短时域上更紧，而语义基监测器在长时域上紧致度提升高达4倍，并在Waymo真实数据上验证了共形覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监控方法在部分可观测条件下难以提供有限样本保证，且通常需要针对每个公式重新训练，缺乏可重用性。本文旨在设计一种可重用的认证运行时监控框架，使得一次训练和校准后即可认证目标片段内的任意公式，无需逐公式重新训练。

Method: 提出语义基向量（semantic basis）作为最小预测目标，证明其在单调1-Lipschitz可重用接口类中的最优性；引入基于解析树的确定性解码器和单次共形校准方法，避免联合界（union bound）退化。同时提出滚动预测监测器（rolling prediction monitor），仅预测当前谓词值并在线重构历史，降低学习难度但长期保守。在行人路口基准和Waymo驾驶数据上验证。

Result: 滚动监测器在短时域上获得更紧的认证界，语义基监测器在长时域上紧致度比滚动监测器提升高达4倍。在Waymo真实驾驶数据上，两种监测器均经验证满足共形覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 语义基方法在保证可重用性的同时，在长时域上显著优于滚动预测方法，且无需联合界即可实现整个片段的一次性共形校准，为部分可观测下的时态逻辑监控提供了理论完备且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We study certified runtime monitoring of past-time signal temporal logic (ptSTL) from visual observations under partial observability. The monitor must infer safety-relevant quantities from images and provide finite-sample guarantees, while being \emph{reusable}: once trained and calibrated, it should certify any formula in a target fragment without per-formula retraining. For fragments induced by a finite dictionary of temporal atoms, we prove that the \emph{semantic basis}, the vector of atom robustness scores, is the minimum prediction target within the class of monotone, 1-Lipschitz reusable interfaces: any formula is evaluated by a deterministic decoder derived from the parse tree, and a single conformal calibration pass certifies the entire fragment with no union bound. We also introduce a \emph{rolling prediction monitor} that predicts only current predicate values and reconstructs temporal history online; this is easier to learn but grows conservative at long horizons. On a pedestrian-crossroad benchmark, rolling achieves tighter certified bounds at short horizons while the semantic-basis monitor is up to 4-times tighter at long horizons. We validate the presented monitors on real-world Waymo driving data, where both monitors satisfy the conformal coverage guarantee empirically.

</details>


### [209] [Architecture-Aware Explanation Auditing for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14255)
*Sibo Jia,Zihang Zhao,Kunrong Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于架构感知的解释审计协议，证明解释方法的忠实度受其与模型原生决策机制的结构距离约束。在WM-811K晶圆图数据集上，ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout在Deletion AUC上显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM（0.211 vs 0.432-0.525），尽管分类精度更低。模型无关方法RISE将所有架构压缩至约0.1的Deletion AUC，表明差距源于解释器路径。不同扰动基线下排序反转，证实忠实度排名是(模型、解释器、扰动算子)三元组的联合属性。结论：解释路径应与模型架构基于读出结构协同设计，部署热图应附带定量忠实度指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业视觉检测中深度分类器的热图解释可能视觉上合理但未能识别真正驱动模型决策的图像区域，现有工作缺乏对解释方法忠实度与模型原生决策机制之间关系的系统性理解。

Method: 提出架构感知的解释审计协议，基于"原生读出假说"（native-readout hypothesis），通过测量解释方法与模型原生决策机制的结构距离来量化忠实度边界。采用三种子零填充扰动协议，在WM-811K数据集上比较不同架构（ViT、Swin、ResNet、DenseNet）与解释方法组合的性能，并引入模糊填充敏感性分析和MVTec AD边界条件研究。

Result: ViT-Tiny+Attention Rollout的Deletion AUC为0.211，显著优于Swin-Tiny/ResNet18+CBAM/DenseNet121+Grad-CAM的0.432-0.525（Cohen's d > 1.1）。RISE将所有方法压缩至约0.1。模糊填充下排序反转。Swin-Tiny作为Transformer因空间特征层级结构兼容Grad-CAM。

Conclusion: 解释忠实度的关键因素是读出结构而非架构家族；原生读出是兼容性原则而非最优性保证；忠实度排名是(模型、解释器、扰动算子)三元组的联合属性；解释路径应与模型架构协同设计，部署热图需附带定量忠实度指标。

Abstract: Industrial visual inspection systems increasingly rely on deep classifiers whose heatmap explanations may appear visually plausible while failing to identify the image regions that actually drive model decisions. This paper operationalizes an architecture-aware explanation audit protocol grounded in the native-readout hypothesis: the perturbation-based faithfulness of an explanation method is bounded by its structural distance from the model's native decision mechanism. On WM-811K wafer maps (9 classes, 172k images) under a three-seed zero-fill perturbation protocol, ViT-Tiny + Attention Rollout attains Deletion AUC 0.211 against 0.432-0.525 for Swin-Tiny / ResNet18+CBAM / DenseNet121 + Grad-CAM (abs(Cohen's d) > 1.1), despite lower classification accuracy. Swin-Tiny disentangles architecture family from readout structure: despite being a Transformer, its spatial feature-map hierarchy makes it Grad-CAM compatible, showing that the operative factor is readout structure rather than architecture family. A model-agnostic control (RISE) compresses all families to Deletion AUC about 0.1, indicating the gap arises from the explainer pathway; notably, RISE outperforms all native methods, so native readout is a compatibility principle rather than an optimality guarantee. A blur-fill sensitivity analysis shows that the family ordering reverses under a different perturbation baseline, reinforcing that faithfulness rankings are joint properties of (model, explainer, perturbation operator) triples. An exploratory boundary-condition study on MVTec AD (pretrained models) indicates that audit results are dataset/task dependent and identifies conditions requiring qualification. The protocol yields actionable guidance: explanation pathways should be co-designed with model architectures based on readout structure, and deployed heatmaps should be accompanied by quantitative faithfulness metrics.

</details>


### [210] [GeoViSTA: Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer for Multimodal Environment Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14406)
*Yuhao Liu,Sadeer Al-Kindi,Ashok Veeraraghavan,Guha Balakrishnan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GeoViSTA，一种视觉-表格联合架构，通过双向交叉注意力和地理感知机制融合遥感图像与结构化社会经济数据，在疾病死亡率、火灾频率等任务上优于基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有地理空间基础模型主要处理遥感图像，无法直接建模表格形式的结构化社会经济协变量，导致模态鸿沟限制了模型对环境、社会和健康相关结果的综合推理能力。

Method: 提出GeoViSTA架构，包含：(1) 视觉-表格双模态输入处理；(2) 双边交叉注意力在模态间交换空间和语义信息；(3) 地理感知注意力机制对齐连续图像块与不规则人口普查区令牌；(4) 自监督联合掩码自编码目标，强制模型利用局部空间上下文和跨模态线索恢复缺失图像块和表格行。

Result: GeoViSTA的统一嵌入在疾病特异性死亡率和火灾频率预测等高影响下游任务的线性探测中优于基线模型，并在未见的区域表现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 联合建模物理环境与结构化社会经济上下文能够产生高度可迁移的表征，适用于全局地理空间推理任务。

Abstract: Large-scale pretraining on Earth observation imagery has yielded powerful representations of the natural and built environment. However, most existing geospatial foundation models do not directly model the structured socioeconomic covariates typically stored in tabular form. This modality gap limits their ability to capture the complete total environment, which is critical for reasoning about complex environmental, social, and health-related outcomes. In this work, we propose GeoViSTA (Geospatial Vision-Tabular Transformer), a vision-tabular architecture that learns unified geospatial embeddings from co-registered gridded imagery and tabular data. GeoViSTA utilizes bilateral cross-attention to exchange spatial and semantic information across modalities, guided by a geography-aware attention mechanism that aligns continuous image patches with irregular census-tract tokens. We train GeoViSTA with a self-supervised joint masked-autoencoding objective, forcing it to recover missing image patches and tabular rows using local spatial context and cross-modal cues. Empirically, GeoViSTA's unified embeddings improve linear probing performance on high-impact downstream tasks, outperforming baselines in predicting disease-specific mortality and fire hazard frequency across held-out regions. These results demonstrate that jointly modeling the physical environment alongside structured socioeconomic context yields highly transferable representations for holistic geospatial inference.

</details>


### [211] [Understanding Imbalanced Forgetting in Rehearsal-Based Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14785)
*Alberto Tamajo,Srinandan Dasmahapatra,Rahman Attar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于回放的类增量学习中存在的非平衡遗忘现象，发现不同类别在增量步骤后遗忘程度差异显著。通过构建三个最后一层系数（捕获梯度层面的干扰源），作者证明这些系数能可靠预测类别遗忘排名，其中自诱导干扰系数是最强预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管回放策略能缓解灾难性遗忘，但近期研究表明即使平衡分配回放样本，某些类别的遗忘程度仍远大于其他类别，这一非平衡遗忘现象尚缺乏深入理解与系统性研究。

Method: 从理论分析出发，构建三个最后一层系数分别捕获梯度层面的干扰源；通过实验验证这些系数对增量步骤后各类别遗忘排名的预测能力；设计控制实验探究系数间的因果关系。

Result: 三个系数联合能可靠预测类别遗忘排名，其中自诱导干扰系数是最强预测因子；控制实验表明自诱导干扰系数受新类别干扰系数影响。

Conclusion: 研究提供了对非平衡遗忘机制的理解，为通过减少类别间干扰源差异来缓解非平衡遗忘指明了方向。

Abstract: Neural networks suffer from catastrophic forgetting in class-incremental learning (CIL) settings. Rehearsal$\unicode{x2013}$replaying a subset of past samples$\unicode{x2013}$is a well-established mitigation strategy. However, recent results suggest that, despite balanced rehearsal allocation, some classes are forgotten substantially more than others. Despite its relevance, this imbalanced forgetting phenomenon remains underexplored. This work shows that imbalanced forgetting arises systematically and severely in rehearsal-based CIL and investigates it extensively. Specifically, we construct, from a principled analysis, three last-layer coefficients that capture different gradient-level sources of interference affecting each past class during an incremental step. We then demonstrate that, together, they reliably predict how past classes will rank in terms of forgetting at the end of that step. While predictive performance alone does not establish causality, these results support the interpretation of the coefficients as a plausible mechanistic account linking last-layer gradient-level interactions during training to class-level forgetting outcomes. Notably, one coefficient$\unicode{x2013}$capturing self-induced interference$\unicode{x2013}$emerges as the strongest predictor, with controlled experiments providing evidence consistent with this coefficient being influenced by the new-class interference coefficient. Overall, our findings provide valuable insights and suggest promising directions for mitigating imbalanced forgetting by reducing class-wise disparities in the identified sources of interference.

</details>


### [212] [Octopus: History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization for Continual Learning in Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14938)
*Yuehao Liu,Shanyan Guan,Weijia Zhang,Xuanming Shang,Yanhao Ge,Wei Li,Chao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于无历史梯度正交化（HiFGO）的两阶段持续学习框架Octopus，在不依赖历史数据的情况下实现梯度级正交约束，有效缓解多模态大模型的灾难性遗忘问题，在UCIT基准上取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM持续学习方法存在架构方法计算开销大且泛化差、重演方法依赖历史数据引发隐私和存储问题、传统正则化方法难以完全防止参数干扰等固有局限。

Method: 提出了Octopus框架，核心是无历史梯度正交化（HiFGO）方法，通过两阶段微调策略将任务适应与正则化解耦，在不依赖历史任务数据的情况下强制梯度级正交性。

Result: 在UCIT基准上，Avg和Last指标分别超越此前SOTA方法2.14%和6.82%。

Conclusion: Octopus通过无历史梯度正交化的两阶段持续学习框架，在避免历史数据依赖和额外计算开销的前提下，有效平衡了可塑性与稳定性，显著提升了多模态大模型的持续学习性能。

Abstract: Continual learning in multimodal large language models (MLLMs) aims to sequentially acquire knowledge while mitigating catastrophic forgetting, yet existing methods face inherent limitations: architecture-based approaches incur additional computational overhead and often generalize poorly to new tasks, rehearsal-based methods rely on storing historical data, raising privacy and storage concerns, and conventional regularization-based strategies alone are insufficient to fully prevent parameter interference. We propose Octopus, a two-stage continual learning framework based on History-Free Gradient Orthogonalization (HiFGO), which enforces gradient-level orthogonality without historical task data. Our proposed two-stage finetuning strategy decouples task adaptation from regularization, achieving a principled balance between plasticity and stability. Experiments on UCIT show that Octopus establishes state-of-the-art performance, surpassing prior SOTA by 2.14% and 6.82% in terms of Avg and Last.

</details>


### [213] [DiffusionOPD: A Unified Perspective of On-Policy Distillation in Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15055)
*Quanhao Li,Junqiu Yu,Kaixun Jiang,Yujie Wei,Zhen Xing,Pandeng Li,Ruihang Chu,Shiwei Zhang,Yu Liu,Zuxuan Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DiffusionOPD，一种基于在线策略蒸馏的多任务训练范式，将单任务探索与多任务集成解耦，通过连续状态马尔可夫过程的封闭形式KL散度目标进行均值匹配，在文本到图像扩散模型的多任务RL优化中取得SOTA结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于强化学习的扩散文本到图像模型优化主要限于单任务，扩展到多任务时联合优化存在跨任务干扰和训练不平衡问题，级联RL则繁琐且易发生灾难性遗忘。

Method: 先独立训练任务专用教师模型，再沿着学生自身rollout轨迹将这些能力蒸馏到统一的学生模型中；将在线策略蒸馏框架从离散token推广到连续状态马尔可夫过程，推导出闭式每步KL散度目标（通过均值匹配统一SDE和ODE细化），该解析梯度比传统PPO策略梯度具有更低方差和更好泛化性。

Result: 在训练效率和最终性能上始终优于多奖励RL和级联RL基线，在所有评估基准上达到SOTA。

Conclusion: DiffusionOPD通过解耦单任务探索与多任务融合，有效避免了联合优化问题，其解析KL梯度提供了更优的优化特性，为扩散模型多任务RL训练提供了新范式。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning has emerged as a powerful tool for improving diffusion-based text-to-image models, but existing methods are largely limited to single-task optimization. Extending RL to multiple tasks is challenging: joint optimization suffers from cross-task interference and imbalance, while cascade RL is cumbersome and prone to catastrophic forgetting. We propose DiffusionOPD, a new multi-task training paradigm for diffusion models based on Online Policy Distillation (OPD). DiffusionOPD first trains task-specific teachers independently, then distills their capabilities into a unified student along the student own rollout trajectories. This decouples single-task exploration from multi-task integration and avoids the optimization burden of solving all tasks jointly from scratch. Theoretically, we lift the OPD framework from discrete tokens to continuous-state Markov processes, deriving a closed-form per-step KL objective that unifies both stochastic SDE and deterministic ODE refinement via mean-matching. We formally and empirically demonstrate that this analytic gradient provides lower variance and better generality compared to conventional PPO-style policy gradients. Extensive experiments show that DiffusionOPD consistently surpasses both multi-reward RL and cascade RL baselines in training efficiency and final performance, while achieving state-of-the-art results on all evaluated benchmarks.

</details>


### [214] [Towards the Next Frontier of LLMs, Training on Private Data: A Cross-Domain Benchmark for Federated Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13936)
*Daniel M. Jimenez-Gutierrez,Enrique Zuazua,Georgios Kellaris,Joaquin del Rio,Oleksii Sliusarenko,Xabi Uribe-Etxebarria*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建的联邦微调框架，使多个机构在不共享私有数据的情况下联合微调大语言模型。通过在医疗和金融领域的四个数据集上评估三种参数高效微调策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3），发现联邦微调效果接近集中式训练且优于单机构学习，其中QLoRA和IA3在绿色AI视角下具有更优的效率-精度平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型的进一步发展需要利用分布在多个机构中的私有数据，但这些数据受到隐私、监管和组织壁垒的限制，且呈现非独立同分布（non-IID）特征。现有方法无法在保护数据隐私的同时有效利用这些分散的私有数据来提升LLM性能。

Method: 基于Sherpa.ai联邦学习平台构建联邦微调框架，使各节点在不交换私有数据的情况下联合微调共享LLM。采用三种PEFT策略（LoRA、QLoRA、IA3），在非IID设置下对四个医疗和金融领域的闭式问答与分类数据集进行评估。

Result: 联邦微调性能接近集中式训练，显著优于单机构独立学习。QLoRA和IA3在保持有限精度损失的同时提升了训练效率，证明了联邦PEFT在数据不可共享场景下适配LLM的可行性。

Conclusion: 联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）是解锁私有分布式机构数据用于LLM适配的可行方法，能够在保护隐私的同时实现接近集中式训练的性能，且QLoRA和IA3从绿色AI角度提供了更优的效率-精度权衡。

Abstract: The recent success of large language models (LLMs) has been largely driven by vast public datasets. However, the next frontier for LLM development lies beyond public data. Much of the world's most valuable information is private, especially in highly regulated sectors such as healthcare and finance, where data include patient histories or customer communications. Unlocking this data could represent a major leap forward, enabling LLMs with deeper domain expertise and stronger real-world utility. Yet, these data cannot be shared because they are distributed across institutions and constrained by privacy, regulatory, and organizational barriers. Moreover, institutional datasets are typically non-independent and identically distributed (non-IID), differing across sites in population characteristics, data modalities, documentation patterns, and task-specific label distributions.
  In this paper, we demonstrate a practical approach to unlocking private and distributed institutional data for LLM adaptation through federated collaboration across data silos. Built on the Sherpa.ai Federated Learning platform, our framework enables nodes to jointly fine-tune a shared LLM without exchanging private data. We evaluate this approach through a cross-domain benchmark in healthcare and finance, using four closed-ended question answering and classification datasets: MedQA, MedMCQA, FPB, and FiQA-SA. We compare three parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) strategies-LoRA, QLoRA, and IA3-across pretrained backbones under non-IID settings reflecting institutional data heterogeneity. Our results show that federated fine-tuning performs close to centralized training and outperforms isolated single-institution learning. From a Green AI perspective, QLoRA and IA3 improve efficiency with limited accuracy degradation, supporting federated PEFT as a viable approach for adapting LLMs where data cannot be shared.

</details>


### [215] [EvolveMem:Self-Evolving Memory Architecture via AutoResearch for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13941)
*Jiaqi Liu,Xinyu Ye,Peng Xia,Zeyu Zheng,Cihang Xie,Mingyu Ding,Huaxiu Yao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvolveMem提出了一种自演化记忆架构，不仅存储内容会更新，检索机制（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）也会通过LLM驱动的诊断模块进行自动优化。在LoCoMo和MemBench上分别比最强基线提升25.7%和18.9%，且演化策略可跨基准迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体长期记忆系统的检索基础设施（评分函数、融合策略、答案生成策略）在部署后固定不变，无法真正实现自适应的记忆演化。

Method: 将完整的检索配置暴露为结构化动作空间，通过LLM驱动的诊断模块进行闭环自我演化：读取失败日志→识别根本原因→提出配置调整→应用变更→自动回滚退化→停滞时探索。实现AutoResearch式的自主架构迭代。

Result: LoCoMo上比最强基线提升25.7%，比最小基线提升78.0%；MemBench上比最强基线提升18.9%；演化配置可跨基准正迁移。

Conclusion: 自演化过程能够自动收敛并发现有效的检索策略，包括原始动作空间中不存在的全新配置维度，且捕捉的是通用检索原理而非基准特定启发式。

Abstract: Long-term memory is essential for LLM agents that operate across multiple sessions, yet existing memory systems treat retrieval infrastructure as fixed: stored content evolves while scoring functions, fusion strategies, and answer-generation policies remain frozen at deployment. We argue that truly adaptive memory requires co-evolution at two levels: the stored knowledge and the retrieval mechanism that queries it. We present EvolveMem, a self-evolving memory architecture that exposes its full retrieval configuration as a structured action space optimized by an LLM-powered diagnosis module. In each evolution round, the module reads per-question failure logs, identifies root causes, and proposes targeted configuration adjustments; a guarded meta-analyzer applies them with automatic revert-on-regression and explore-on-stagnation safeguards. This closed-loop self-evolution realizes an AutoResearch process: the system autonomously conducts iterative research cycles on its own architecture, replacing manual configuration tuning. Starting from a minimal baseline, the process converges autonomously, discovering effective retrieval strategies including entirely new configuration dimensions not present in the original action space. On LoCoMo, EvolveMem outperforms the strongest baseline by 25.7% relative and achieves a 78.0% relative improvement over the minimal baseline. On MemBench, EvolveMem exceeds the strongest baseline by 18.9% relative. Evolved configurations transfer across benchmarks with positive rather than catastrophic transfer, indicating that the self-evolution process captures universal retrieval principles rather than benchmark-specific heuristics. Code is available at https://github.com/aiming-lab/SimpleMem.

</details>


### [216] [Collider-Bench: Benchmarking AI Agents with Particle Physics Analysis Reproduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13950)
*Darius A. Faroughy,Sofia Palacios Schweitzer,Ian Pang,Siddharth Mishra-Sharma,David Shih*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Collider-Bench，一个评估LLM智能体能否利用公开论文和科学软件复现大型强子对撞机（LHC）实验分析的基准。实验表明，现有通用编码智能体平均而言无法超过物理学家介入的方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准难以捕捉真实科学工作的复杂性和细微差别，缺乏评估LLM智能体在长周期工具使用任务中复现LHC实验分析能力的标准。

Method: 构建Collider-Bench基准，任务要求智能体将已发表分析转化为可执行的模拟-筛选流水线，提交预测的碰撞事件产额；使用标准直方图指标提供连续保真度评分，并通过LLM裁判检测代码质量和故障模式（伪造、幻觉、重复）。

Result: 通用编码智能体平均无法可靠优于物理学家介入的解决方案；公开了首批任务、容器化沙箱和事件模拟工具。

Conclusion: 现有通用agent在复杂科学复现任务上能力不足，Collider-Bench为评估和改进科学领域LLM智能体提供了有效基准。

Abstract: Autonomous language-model agents are increasingly evaluated on long-horizon tool-use tasks, but existing benchmarks rarely capture the complexity and nuance of real scientific work. To address this gap, we introduce Collider-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating whether LLM agents can reproduce experimental analyses from the Large Hadron Collider (LHC) using only public papers and open scientific software. Such analyses are often difficult to reproduce because the public toolchain only approximates the software used internally by the experimental collaborations, while the published papers inevitably omit implementation details needed for a faithful reconstruction. Agents must therefore rely on physical reasoning, domain knowledge, and trial-and-error to fill these gaps. Each task requires the agent to turn a published analysis into an executable simulation-and-selection pipeline and submit predicted collision event yields in specified signal regions. These predictions are evaluated with standard histogram metrics that provide continuous fidelity scores without a hand-written rubric. We also report the computational cost incurred by each agent per task. Finally, we evaluate the codebase and full session trace using an LLM judge to catch qualitative failure modes such as fabrications, hallucinations and duplications. We release an initial set of tasks drawn from LHC searches, together with a containerized sandbox and event simulation tools. We evaluate across a capability ladder of general purpose coding agents. Our results show that on average no agent reliably beats the physicist-in-the-loop solution.

</details>


### [217] [WarmPrior: Straightening Flow-Matching Policies with Temporal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13959)
*Sinjae Kang,Chanyoung Kim,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao,Kimin Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出WarmPrior——一种基于近期动作历史构建的简单时间感知先验分布，替换扩散/流匹配模型中的标准高斯源分布，可显著提升机器人操作任务的成功率。其原因在于产生了更直的路径（类似Rectified Flow中的最优传输耦合效果），并在行为克隆和强化学习中均带来性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于扩散和流匹配的生成式策略已成为机器人视觉运动控制的主流范式，但标准高斯源分布并非最优选择。作者希望探索源分布这一未被充分研究的设计维度，通过引入更合适的先验分布来提升生成式机器人控制策略的性能。

Method: 提出WarmPrior，从近期动作历史中构建简单的时间感知先验分布，替代标准高斯分布作为扩散/流匹配模型的源分布。该方法可以无缝集成到行为克隆和基于先验空间的强化学习中。

Result: 在机器人操作任务上持续提升成功率；产生更直的路径（straighter paths），类似于Rectified Flow中最优传输耦合的效果；在强化学习中同时改善样本效率和最终性能。

Conclusion: 源分布是生成式机器人控制中一个重要且被低估的设计维度，WarmPrior作为一种简单有效的改进方案，在多种设置下均能带来性能提升。

Abstract: Generative policies based on diffusion and flow matching have become a dominant paradigm for visuomotor robotic control. We show that replacing the standard Gaussian source distribution with WarmPrior, a simple temporally grounded prior constructed from readily available recent action history, consistently improves success rates on robotic manipulation tasks. We trace this gain to markedly straighter probability paths, echoing the effect of optimal-transport couplings in Rectified Flow. Beyond standard behavior cloning, WarmPrior also reshapes the exploration distribution in prior-space reinforcement learning, improving both sample efficiency and final performance. Collectively, these results identify the source distribution as an important and underexplored design axis in generative robot control.

</details>


### [218] [Towards Resource-Efficient LLMs: End-to-End Energy Accounting of Distillation Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13981)
*Katherine Lambert,Sasha Luccioni*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，用于测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本，揭示传统知识蒸馏和合成数据微调中之前被忽视的能耗开销，并发布了开源测量工具和标准化协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模语言模型的部署导致GPU需求和数据中心规模激增，引发了对电力消耗和电网压力的担忧。蒸馏常被视为获得更高效模型的路径，但现有研究很少考虑包括教师端数据生成、logit缓存和评估在内的完整端到端能耗和资源成本。

Method: 提出了一个全面的能耗核算框架，通过详细的阶段级GPU设备功耗追踪来测量蒸馏管道的完整计算成本。实验中对不同阶段的能耗进行分离和记录，系统测量了经典logit知识蒸馏和合成数据监督微调两种方法的能耗与排放，构建了能耗-质量帕累托前沿。

Result: 揭示了蒸馏管道中之前被忽视的能耗成本；构建了能量-质量帕累托前沿；得出了在能耗和预算约束下选择蒸馏方法和超参数的实用设计规则；发布了开源测量工具和核算协议。

Conclusion: 蒸馏的实际能耗成本比通常声称的要高，需要考虑完整的管道能耗；作者提供的开源工具和标准化协议为可比较、可复现的蒸馏研究提供了基础，使完整管道能耗影响得以明确核算。

Abstract: The rise in deployment of large language models has driven a surge in GPU demand and datacenter scaling, raising concerns about electricity use, grid stress, and the impacts of modern AI workloads. Distillation is often promoted as one of the most effective paths to obtain cheaper, more efficient models, yet these claims rarely account for the full end-to-end energy and resource costs, including crucial teacher-side workloads such as data generation, logit caching, and evaluation. We present a comprehensive energy accounting framework that measures the complete computational cost of distillation pipelines via detailed stage-wise tracking of GPU device power consumption. In our experiments, we separate and log empirical energy use across distinct phases and systematically measure the energy and emissions of two common distillation methods: the classic logit-based knowledge distillation and synthetic-data supervised fine-tuning, constructing energy-quality Pareto frontiers that expose the previously ignored costs. From these measurements and analyses, we derive practical design rules for selecting distillation methods and hyperparameters under energy and budget constraints, and release an open-source measurement harness and accounting protocol to provide a standardized foundation for comparable, reproducible distillation research, explicitly accountable for complete pipeline energy impact.

</details>


### [219] [HodgeCover: Higher-Order Topological Coverage Drives Compression of Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13997)
*Tao Zhong,Dongzhe Zheng,Christine Allen-Blanchette*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型中存在一种结构性的三专家不可合并循环障碍，将其精确建模为2-复形上的调和核，并提出了HodgeCover算法来识别和覆盖这些关键边和三角，从而在无需重新训练的情况下实现更优的专家压缩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有免学习的MoE压缩方法仅依赖成对专家兼容性评分，但忽略了三个专家两两兼容却整体不可合并的循环障碍，导致压缩结果存在结构性盲区。

Method: 将专家视为顶点，KL合并障碍为边，三元组障碍为面，构建2-复形；利用调和核（simplicial Laplacian的调和核）精确定位循环障碍；提出HodgeCover算法，贪心覆盖调和关键边和关键三角，并与权重剪枝混合。

Result: 在三个开源稀疏MoE模型上，HodgeCover在专家缩减维度上达到SOTA，在混合压缩前沿上领先，并能独特地在所有四个Hodge分量上平衡保留质量。

Conclusion: 揭示MoE结构的调和核能改变哪个压缩器在最重要的压缩场景下胜出，为免学习MoE压缩提供了新的理论视角和实用方法。

Abstract: Sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers route tokens through a handful of experts, and learning-free compression of these layers reduces inference cost without retraining. A subtle obstruction blocks every existing compressor in this family: three experts can each be pairwise compatible yet form an irreducible cycle when merged together, so any score that ranks experts on pairwise signals is structurally blind to which triples are jointly mergeable. We show the obstruction is a precise mathematical object, the harmonic kernel of the simplicial Laplacian on a 2-complex whose vertices are experts, whose edges carry KL merge barriers, and whose faces carry triplet barriers; Hodge-decomposing the edge-barrier signal isolates the kernel exactly. We turn the diagnostic into a selection objective: HodgeCover greedily covers the harmonic-critical edges and triplet-critical triangles, and a hybrid variant of HodgeCover pairs it with off-the-shelf weight pruning on survivors. On three open-weight Sparse MoE backbones under aggressive expert reduction, HodgeCover matches state-of-the-art learning-free baselines on the expert-reduction axis, leads on the aggressive-compression frontier of the hybrid axis, and uniquely balances retained mass across all four Hodge components. These results show that exposing the harmonic kernel of a learned MoE structure changes which compressor wins at the regime that matters most.

</details>


### [220] [Dywave: Event-Aligned Dynamic Tokenization for Heterogeneous IoT Sensing Signal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14014)
*Tomoyoshi Kimura,Denizhan Kara,Jinyang Li,Hongjue Zhao,Yigong Hu,Yizhuo Chen,Xiaomin Ouyang,Shengzhong Liu,Tarek Abdelzaher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Dywave，一种面向IoT传感信号的动态分词化框架，利用小波层级分解识别语义事件边界并自适应压缩冗余区间，在五个真实数据集上实现高达12%的准确率提升，并减少高达75%的输入序列长度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT系统持续采集多尺度、非平稳的异构传感信号，标准分词化技术难以有效处理其内在的时间结构和物理事件特征。

Method: Dywave采用基于小波的层级分解方法，识别与底层语义事件对应的时间边界，自适应压缩冗余区间，同时保持时间连贯性，构建与内在时间结构对齐的紧凑输入表示。

Result: 在五个真实IoT传感数据集（涵盖活动识别、压力评估、附近物体检测）上，Dywave相比最先进方法准确率提升高达12%，输入序列长度减少高达75%，计算效率显著提升，且对领域偏移和变长序列具有更好的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Dywave是一种有效的动态分词化框架，能够显著提升IoT传感信号处理任务的性能和效率，同时增强模型对领域偏移和序列长度变化的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Internet of Things (IoT) systems continuously collect heterogeneous sensing signals from ubiquitous sensors to support intelligent applications such as human activity analysis, emotion monitoring, and environmental perception. These signals are inherently non-stationary and multi-scale, posing unique challenges for standard tokenization techniques. This paper proposes Dywave, a dynamic tokenization framework for IoT sensing signals that constructs compact input representations aligned with intrinsic temporal structures and underlying physical events. Dywave leverages wavelet-based hierarchical decomposition, identifies meaningful temporal boundaries corresponding to underlying semantic events, and adaptively compresses redundant intervals while preserving temporal coherence. Extensive evaluations on five real-world IoT sensing datasets across activity recognition, stress assessment, and nearby object detection demonstrate that Dywave outperforms state-of-the-art methods by up to 12% in accuracy, while improving computational efficiency by reducing input token lengths by up to 75% across mainstream sequence models. Moreover, Dywave exhibits improved robustness to domain shifts and varying sequence lengths.

</details>


### [221] [R2R2: Robust Representation for Intensive Experience Reuse via Redundancy Reduction in Self-Predictive Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14026)
*Sanghyeob Song,Donghyeok Lee,Jinsik Kim,Sungroh Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出R2R2，一种在自预测学习（SPL）中通过冗余约简实现鲁棒表示的正则化方法，解决了高更新-数据比（UTD）下的过拟合问题。理论发现标准零中心化与SPL谱特性冲突，设计非中心化目标。在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时改进TD7约22%，并提升了SimbaV2-SPL的性能，达到新SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺的真实机器人等强化学习场景中，频繁的数据重用虽提升效率但导致过拟合。现有工作多关注评论员偏差，而高UTD下自预测学习（SPL）中的表示层不稳定性尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出R2R2：在SPL框架内通过冗余约简进行正则化。理论上发现标准零中心化与SPL谱特性冲突，据此设计非中心化目标。在原生SPL算法TD7上验证，并扩展至不含SPL的SOTA方法SimbaV2，集成SPL模块得到SimbaV2-SPL。

Result: 在11个连续控制任务上，UTD=20时R2R2使TD7提升约22%，在SimbaV2-SPL基础上带来额外增益，SimbaV2-SPL本身即达到新SOTA。

Conclusion: R2R2有效缓解高UTD下的过拟合，与现有改进正交，可推广至多种SPL算法。

Abstract: For reinforcement learning in data-scarce domains like real-world robotics, intensive data reuse enhances efficiency but induces overfitting. While prior works focus on critic bias, representation-level instability in Self-Predictive Learning (SPL) under high Update-to-Data (UTD) regimes remains underexplored. To bridge this gap, we propose Robust Representation via Redundancy Reduction (R2R2), a regularization method within SPL. We theoretically identify that standard zero-centering conflicts with SPL's spectral properties and design a non-centered objective accordingly. We verify R2R2 on SPL-native algorithms like TD7. Furthermore, to demonstrate its orthogonality to prior advancements, we extend the state-of-the-art SimbaV2, which originally lacks SPL, by integrating a tailored SPL module, termed SimbaV2-SPL. Experiments across 11 continuous control tasks confirm that R2R2 effectively mitigates overfitting; specifically, at a UTD ratio of 20, it improves TD7 by ~22% and provides additional gains on top of SimbaV2-SPL, which itself establishes a new state-of-the-art. The code can be found at: https://github.com/songsang7/R2R2

</details>


### [222] [Reinforcement Learning for Tool-Calling Agents in Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14126)
*Marius S. Knorr,Robert Müller,Jan P. Bremer,Nils Schweingruber*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出使用强化学习（RL）后训练多轮CodeAct智能体，在FHIR标准下的医疗数据图上进行结构化问答。通过LLM Judge提供执行奖励，在FHIR-AgentBench上将Qwen3-8B模型的准确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%，并保证了数据完整性约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FHIR是医疗互操作性标准，其上回答临床问题需要多步推理、过滤和聚合。现有工具增强的LLM智能体常选错资源或违反遍历约束，性能不足。

Method: 将FHIR推理建模为可查询结构化图上的序列决策问题；实现多轮CodeAct智能体；使用强化学习（RL）进行后训练，通过LLM Judge提供基于执行结果的奖励信号。

Result: 在FHIR-AgentBench上，RL后训练使Qwen3-8B模型答案正确率从50%（o4-mini）提升至77%，且使用了更小的模型。

Conclusion: RL后训练能有效提升多轮结构化临床图推理能力，本文提供了一个完整的端到端后训练流程（环境构建、工具封装、模型训练与评估）。

Abstract: Fast Healthcare Interoperability Resources (FHIR) is the dominant standard for interoperable exchange of healthcare data. In FHIR, electronic health records form a directed graph of resources. Answering clinically meaningful questions over FHIR requires agents to perform multi-step reasoning, filtering, and aggregation across multiple resource types. Prior work shows that even tool-augmented LLM agents (retrieval, code execution, multi-turn planning) often select the wrong resources or violate traversal constraints. We study this problem in the context of FHIR-AgentBench, a benchmark for realistic question answering over real-world hospital data, and frame reasoning on FHIR as a sequential decision-making problem over a queryable structured graph. We implement a multi-turn CodeAct agent and post-train it with reinforcement learning using a custom harness and tools. A LLM Judge provides execution-grounded rewards. Compared to prompt-based, closed-model baselines, RL post-training improves performance while enforcing data-integrity constraints. Empirically, our approach improves answer correctness from 50% (o4-mini) to 77% on FHIR-AgentBench using a smaller and cheaper Qwen3-8B model. We present an end-to-end post-training pipeline (environment building, harness construction, model training and custom evaluation) that reliably improves multi-turn reasoning over structured clinical graphs.

</details>


### [223] [Towards Fine-Grained and Verifiable Concept Bottleneck Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14210)
*Yingying Fang,Haijie Xu,Shuang Wu,Mariathasan Anish,Guang Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种细粒度概念瓶颈模型框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化视觉证据上，使概念表示既可解释又可验证，在医学影像基准上保持预测性能的同时显著提升透明度和可信度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概念瓶颈模型难以验证预测的概念是否对应正确的视觉证据，容易学习虚假相关性，限制了其可靠性。

Method: 提出细粒度CBM框架，将每个概念锚定在局部化视觉证据上，使概念表示可直接检查其位置和编码方式。

Result: 学习到的概念空间信息完备，预测性能与标准CBM相当，同时显著提升透明度；验证了概念表示的存在性和正确性。

Conclusion: 该方法弥合了可解释性与可验证性之间的鸿沟，增强了CBM的可信度，为更可靠、临床可操作的概念学习系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) offer interpretable alternatives to black-box predictors by introducing human-relatable concepts before the final output. However, existing CBMs struggle to verify whether predicted concepts correspond to the correct visual evidence, limiting their reliability. We propose a fine-grained CBM framework that grounds each concept in localized visual evidence, enabling direct inspection of where and how concepts are encoded. This design allows users to interpret predictions and verify that the model learns intended concepts rather than spurious correlations. Experiments on medical imaging benchmarks show that our learned concept space is information-complete and achieves predictive performance comparable to standard CBMs, while substantially improving transparency. Unlike post-hoc attribution methods, our framework validates both the presence and correctness of concept representations, bridging interpretability with verifiability. Our approach enhances the trustworthiness of CBMs and establishes a principled mechanism for human-model interaction at the concept level, paving the way toward more reliable and clinically actionable concept-based learning systems.

</details>


### [224] [PreFT: Prefill-only finetuning for efficient inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14217)
*Andrew Lanpouthakoun,Aryaman Arora,Zhengxuan Wu,Dhruv Pai,Ben Keigwin,Dan Jurafsky,Christopher Potts*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PreFT（仅预填充微调）方法，在LLM推理时仅对预填充阶段应用适配器，丢弃解码阶段适配器，从而显著提升多适配器服务吞吐量（在Llama 3.1 70B上512个适配器时吞吐量提升1.9倍），且性能损失可通过增加秩来补偿，在强化学习任务中与标准PEFT性能相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法在服务多用户个性化适配器时，预填充与解码阶段存在吞吐量不匹配问题，解码阶段因需要为每个适配器单独处理而导致吞吐量远低于预填充阶段，单纯优化参数量无法解决服务效率瓶颈。

Method: 提出PreFT，仅在预填充阶段对token应用适配器（LoRA/ReFT），解码阶段丢弃适配器回到基础模型，基于vLLM推理引擎实现高效服务。

Result: 1) 多用户服务吞吐量：512个适配器时相比传统PEFT提升1.9倍；2) SFT任务中PreFT评估损失略高，但可通过增加秩补偿且几乎不降低吞吐量；3) RL任务中PreFT与标准PEFT性能接近。

Conclusion: PreFT为个性化LLM服务提供了比现有PEFT更优的精度-吞吐量权衡，验证了仅预填充适配的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can now be personalised efficiently at scale using parameter efficient finetuning methods (PEFTs), but serving user-specific PEFTs harms throughput, even with specialised kernels and memory management techniques. This is because, theoretically and empirically, a mismatch exists between prefill (processing a large number of tokens at once) and decode (generating a single token autoregressively): the latter has far lower throughput when serving multiple adapters. Rather than optimising performance relative to parameter count, for efficient multi-adapter serving, we instead ought to optimise performance relative to serving throughput. We therefore propose PreFT (Prefill-only Finetuning), wherein we only apply the adapter to prefill tokens and discard it afterwards. PreFT significantly increases throughput with minimal effect on performance. We develop and release an efficient implementation of two prefill-only PEFTs, LoRA and ReFT, on the vLLM inference engine. We first show that serving multi-user PreFTs is more efficient than traditional PEFTs ($1.9\times$ the throughput when serving $512$ adapters on Llama 3.1 70B). Then, we compare the performance of prefill-only vs. all-token adapters on a variety of supervised finetuning and reinforcement learning tasks with LMs at varying scales. On SFT, we observe that the evaluation loss of PreFTs is higher than PEFTs, but can be compensated by increasing rank with nearly no reduction in throughput. On RL, we consistently find that PreFTs approach parity with standard PEFTs. Together, this work validates prefill-only adaptation of LLMs as a more favourable accuracy-throughput tradeoff than existing PEFTs for personalised serving.

</details>


### [225] [Diagnosing Training Inference Mismatch in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14220)
*Tianle Zhong,Neiwen Ling,Yifan Pi,Zijun Wei,Tianshu Yu,Geoffrey Fox,Peng Wu,Xiao Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM强化学习系统中， rollout生成与策略优化两阶段存在实现差异导致的训练-推理不匹配（TIM），这种微小的token级数值不一致会独立引发训练崩溃，并改变有效优化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM RL系统中rollout生成与策略优化两阶段本应产生完全匹配的token概率，但实现差异导致两者对相同序列赋予不同值，造成难以检查的TIM问题，且TIM与off-policy漂移和稳定机制纠缠在一起，需要被隔离研究。

Method: 提出零不匹配诊断设置（VeXact）来隔离TIM，通过控制变量实验展示token级数值不一致对训练的影响，分析TIM对优化问题的改变，并识别缓解措施。

Result: 小规模token级数值不一致可以独立引发训练崩溃；TIM改变了有效优化问题；识别了一组可缓解TIM的方法。

Conclusion: TIM不是良性的数值噪声，而是系统层面的扰动，应作为分析LLM RL稳定性的首要因素来对待。

Abstract: Modern LLM RL systems separate rollout generation from policy optimization. These two stages are expected to produce token probabilities that match exactly. However, implementation differences can make them assign different values to the same sequence under the same model weights, inducing Training-Inference Mismatch (TIM). TIM is difficult to inspect because it is entangled with off-policy drift and common stabilization mechanisms. In this work, we isolate TIM in a zero-mismatch diagnostic setting (VeXact), and show that small token-level numerical disagreements can independently cause training collapse. We further show that TIM changes the effective optimization problem, and identify a set of remedies that could mitigate TIM. Our results suggest that TIM is not benign numerical noise, but a systems-level perturbation that should be treated as a first-order factor in analyzing LLM RL stability.

</details>


### [226] [AudioMosaic: Contrastive Masked Audio Representation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14231)
*Hanxun Huang,Qizhou Wang,Xingjun Ma,Cihang Xie,Christopher Leckie,Sarah Erfani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: AudioMosaic是一种基于对比学习的音频自监督编码器，通过在频谱图块上进行结构化时频掩码构建正样本对，实现了高效的大批量训练，学习到更具判别性的句子级表示，在线性探测和微调设置下均达到SOTA性能，并能提升音频-语言模型在下游任务上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习方法在音频自监督学习中探索不足，原因在于：1）有效的音频数据增强策略设计困难；2）对比预训练需要大批量数据，内存消耗大。为此，作者提出一种高效的对比学习框架以学习通用的音频表示。

Method: 在预训练阶段，对频谱图块应用结构化的时频掩码来构建正样本对，这一策略降低了内存使用，使得大批量训练成为可能。编码器学习更具判别性的句子级表示。

Result: 在多个标准音频基准测试中，线性探测和微调设置下均达到最先进的性能。将预训练的AudioMosaic编码器集成到音频-语言模型中，能够提升音频-语言任务的性能。

Conclusion: 对比学习结合结构化时频掩码是一种有效的音频自监督学习方法，相比生成式方法能学习到更强迁移性和判别性的表示，且能作为通用音频编码器提升多模态任务性能。

Abstract: Audio self-supervised learning (SSL) aims to learn general-purpose representations from large-scale unlabeled audio data. While recent advances have been driven mainly by generative reconstruction objectives, contrastive approaches remain less explored, partly due to the difficulty of designing effective audio augmentations and the large batch sizes required for contrastive pre-training. We introduce \textbf{AudioMosaic}, a contrastive learning-based audio encoder for general audio understanding. During pre-training, AudioMosaic constructs positive pairs by applying structured time-frequency masking to spectrogram patches, which reduces memory usage and enables efficient large-batch training. Compared with generative approaches, the AudioMosaic encoder learns more discriminative utterance-level representations that demonstrate strong transferability across datasets, domains, and acoustic conditions. Extensive experiments show that AudioMosaic achieves state-of-the-art performance on several standard audio benchmarks under both linear probing and fine-tuning. We further show that integrating the pretrained AudioMosaic encoder into audio-language models improves performance on audio-language tasks. The code is publicly available in our \href{https://github.com/HanxunH/AudioMosaic}{GitHub repository}.

</details>


### [227] [Active Learners as Efficient PRP Rerankers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14236)
*Jeremías Figueiredo Paschmann,Juan Kaplan,Francisco Nattero Santiago Mauricio Barron Bucolo,Juan Wisznia,Luciano del Corro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将PRP重排序重新定义为基于噪声成对比较的主动学习，提出使用主动排序器作为即插即用替代方案，在调用受限条件下显著提升NDCG@10效率。同时引入随机方向预言机，将系统位置偏差转化为零均值噪声，实现无偏聚合排序，无需双向调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统PRP方法依赖排序算法对LLM的成对偏好判断进行聚合，但LLM判断存在噪声、顺序敏感性和非传递性，导致排序假设不成立；且排序追求全排列，在调用预算受限时无法可靠产生top-K结果。

Method: 1) 将PRP重排序重新定义为基于噪声成对比较的主动学习问题；2) 提出主动排序器作为即插即用替代方案；3) 引入随机方向预言机，单次LLM调用即可获得成对判断，将系统位置偏差转化为零均值噪声。

Result: 主动排序器在调用受限条件下显著提升NDCG@10性能；随机方向预言机实现无偏聚合排序，且无需双向调用成本。

Conclusion: 主动学习框架能更有效地利用有限LLM调用预算进行重排序，随机方向预言机为解决LLM位置偏差提供了高效且无偏的新思路。

Abstract: Pairwise Ranking Prompting (PRP) elicits pairwise preference judgments from an LLM, which are then aggregated into a ranking, usually via classical sorting algorithms. However, judgments are noisy, order-sensitive, and sometimes intransitive, so sorting assumptions do not match the setting. Because sorting aims to recover a full permutation, truncating it to meet a call budget does not produce a dependable top-K. We thus reframe PRP reranking as active learning from noisy pairwise comparisons and show that active rankers are drop-in replacements that improve NDCG@10 per call in the call-constrained regime. Our noise-robust framework also introduces a randomized-direction oracle that uses a single LLM call per pair. This approach converts systematic position bias into zero-mean noise, enabling unbiased aggregate ranking without the cost of bidirectional calls.

</details>


### [228] [Artificial Intelligence-Assistant Cardiotocography: Unified Model for Signal Reconstruction, Fetal Heart Rate Analysis, and Variability Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14242)
*Xiaohua Wang,Kai Yu,XuXiao Liang,Liang Wang,Chao Han*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个基于AI的FHrCTG模型用于胎心率监测，通过大规模预训练（558,412条未标注数据）和专家标注微调（7,266条），引入IOL方法将速率分析转化为分类判断，在检测胎心减速和加速方面表现出高灵敏度和特异性，并在Fischer标准临床应用中取得良好AUC分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统胎心率监测方法受限于设备性能、数据传输和医生主观评估的局限性，亟需一种客观、准确的AI解决方案来预防胎儿窘迫和不良妊娠结局。

Method: 开发AI-based FHrCTG模型，先在558,412条未标注数据上预训练，再用7,266条专家审核数据微调；引入Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL)方法将速率分析转化为分类判断。

Result: 关键胎心减速检测灵敏度89.13%、特异性87.78%；加速检测灵敏度62.5%、特异性92.04%；基于Fischer标准的周期验证AUC=0.7214，振幅变化验证AUC=0.9643。

Conclusion: 该AI模型能有效抑制噪声干扰、精准重建信号，在胎心率监测中具有高临床价值，可辅助医生进行客观评估。

Abstract: The monitoring of fetal heart rate (FHR) and the assessment of its variability are crucial for preventing fetal compromise and adverse outcomes. However, traditional methods encounter limitations arising from equipment performance, data transmission, and subjective assessments by doctors. We have developed a tailored AI-based FHrCTG model specifically for FHR monitoring, which effectively mitigates noise interference and precisely reconstructs signals. Our model was pre-trained on a massive dataset consisting of 558,412 unlabeled data points and further refined using 7,266 expert-reviewed entries. To validate FHR, we introduced the Intersection Overlapping Labels (IOL) approach, which transforms rate analysis into categorical judgments. Testing revealed that our model demonstrates high sensitivity and specificity in detecting critical FHR decelerations (89.13% and 87.78%, respectively) and accelerations (62.5% and 92.04%, respectively). Furthermore, based on Fischer's criteria for clinical application, our model achieved impressive AUC scores of 0.7214 and 0.9643 for verifying FHR periodicity and amplitude variation, respectively.

</details>


### [229] [Not All Timesteps Matter Equally: Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation for Spiking Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14252)
*Kai Sun,Peibo Duan,Yongsheng Huang,Guowei Zhang,Benjamin Smith,Nanxu Gong,Levin Kuhlmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种选择性对齐知识蒸馏方法(SeAl-KD)，通过仅对错误时间步进行校正性指导，同时保留有用的时间动态，解决了SNN中逐时间步统一对齐的问题，在静态图像和神经形态事件数据集上均取得了一致改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SNN知识蒸馏方法对所有时间步进行统一对齐，但SNN预测随时间演变，中间时间步无需各自正确，强制对齐会破坏有用的时间动态，因此需要一种能选择性纠正错误时间步而保留正确时间步动态的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出SeAl-KD，包括：(1) 选择性类别级对齐：对错误时间步均衡竞争logits；(2) 选择性时间级对齐：基于置信度和时间步间相似度重加权时间对齐权重。

Result: 在静态图像和神经形态事件数据集上，SeAl-KD consistently优于现有蒸馏方法。

Conclusion: 选择性对齐策略比统一对齐更有效，能提升SNN性能同时保留其时间动态特性，代码已开源。

Abstract: Spiking neural networks (SNNs), which are brain-inspired and spike-driven, achieve high energy efficiency. However, a performance gap between SNNs and artificial neural networks (ANNs) still remains. Knowledge distillation (KD) is commonly adopted to improve SNN performance, but existing methods typically enforce uniform alignment across all timesteps, either from a teacher network or through inter-temporal self-distillation, implicitly assuming that per-timestep predictions should be treated equally. In practice, SNN predictions vary and evolve over time, and intermediate timesteps need not all be individually correct even when the final aggregated output is correct. Under such conditions, effective distillation should not force every timestep toward the same supervision target, but instead provide corrective guidance to erroneous timesteps while preserving useful temporal dynamics. To address this issue, we propose Selective Alignment Knowledge Distillation (SeAl-KD), which selectively aligns class-level and temporal knowledge by equalizing competing logits at erroneous timesteps and reweighting temporal alignment based on confidence and inter-timestep similarity. Extensive experiments on static image and neuromorphic event-based datasets demonstrate consistent improvements over existing distillation methods. The code is available at https://github.com/KaiSUN1/SeAl

</details>


### [230] [Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning for Reusing Local Transition Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14304)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Carlee Joe-Wong,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出矩阵空间强化学习（MSRL），通过正半定矩阵描述子聚合轨迹段的局部转移几何信息，支持代数组合与迁移学习，在组合泛化任务中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在序列决策的组合泛化中忽视了轨迹段的丰富局部转移几何与动力学结构，缺乏可代数组合的抽象表示来支持跨任务迁移。

Method: MSRL使用正半定矩阵描述子聚合单步转移的一阶和二阶统计量，构建抽象矩阵空间支持代数组合；证明描述子在坐标规范下良定义、对低阶可加信号类完备、在有效段组合下可加、且是极小充分的可加描述子；通过将值函数条件化于轨迹段矩阵实现一阶平滑的动作值近似。

Result: 在有限预算下MSRL达到平均目标AUC 0.73，优于MSRL from scratch (0.65)、TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63)和TD-MPC (0.57)。

Conclusion: MSRL通过几何抽象的矩阵描述子有效捕获局部转移结构，支持代数组合与迁移，可作为插件兼容标准强化学习方法，提升组合泛化性能。

Abstract: Compositional generalization in sequential decision-making requires identifying which parts of prior rollouts remain useful for new tasks. Existing methods reuse skills or predictive models, but often overlook rich local transition geometry and dynamics. We propose Matrix-Space Reinforcement Learning (MSRL), a geometric abstraction that represents trajectory segments through positive semidefinite matrix descriptors aggregating first- and second-order statistics of lifted one-step transitions. These descriptors expose shared hidden structure, support algebraic composition in an abstract matrix space, and reveal opportunities for transfer. We prove that the descriptor is well defined up to coordinate gauge, complete for the induced low-order additive signal class, additive under valid segment composition, and minimally sufficient among admissible additive descriptors. We further show that conditioning value functions on the trajectory-segment matrix yields a first-order smooth approximation of action values, enabling source-learned matrix-to-value mappings to bootstrap learning in new tasks. MSRL is plug-in compatible with standard model-free and model-based methods, while obstruction filtering rejects implausible compositions. Empirically, MSRL achieves the best average finite-budget target AUC of 0.73, outperforming MSRL from scratch (0.65), TD-MPC-PT+FT (0.63), and TD-MPC (0.57).

</details>


### [231] [Beyond Binary: Reframing GUI Critique as Continuous Semantic Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14311)
*Yuchen Sun,Pei Fu,Shaojie Zhang,Anan Du,Xiuwen Xi,Ruoceng Zhang,Zhenbo Luo,Jian Luan,Chongyang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出BBCritic，将GUI智能体批评模型从二元分类范式转向基于功能等价性假设的对比学习范式，通过恢复行为空间的层次结构实现细粒度排序，并构建了首个四层等级基准BBBench。3B参数的BBCritic无需额外标注即超越7B最优二元模型，展现出强零样本迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI批评模型统一采用二元分类，导致有效动作与看似合理但无效的干扰项得分高度纠缠不可区分。根源在于两个结构性缺陷：行为空间崩塌（层级结构被压缩为0/1标签）和噪声敏感性（二元目标过拟合到噪声决策边界）。

Method: 提出功能等价性假设，通过两阶段对比学习将指令和动作对齐到共享的行为空间，恢复二元监督所抹平的层次结构。同时构建BBBench基准，配备密集动作空间和四层等级分类体系以支持细粒度排序评估。

Result: BBCritic-3B无需额外标注，在性能上超越7B参数的最优二元模型；在跨平台和跨任务场景下展现出强零样本迁移能力。

Conclusion: GUI智能体的批评问题本质上是度量学习问题而非分类问题。从二元分类转向对比学习范式的范式转变能有效提升批评模型的细粒度排序能力。

Abstract: Test-Time Scaling (TTS), which samples multiple candidate actions and ranks them via a Critic Model, has emerged as a promising paradigm for generalist GUI agents. Its efficacy thus hinges on the critic's fine-grained ranking ability. However, existing GUI critic models uniformly adopt binary classification. Our motivational analysis of these models exposes a severe entanglement: scores for valid actions and plausible-but-invalid distractors become indistinguishable. We attribute this failure to two structural defects: Affordance Collapse--the hierarchical affordance space is compressed into 0/1 labels; and Noise Sensitivity--binary objectives overfit to noisy decision boundaries. To resolve this, we introduce BBCritic (Beyond-Binary Critic), a paradigm shift grounded in the Functional Equivalence Hypothesis. Through two-stage contrastive learning, BBCritic aligns instructions and actions in a shared Affordance Space, recovering the hierarchical structure that binary supervision flattens. We also present BBBench (Beyond-Binary Bench), the first GUI critic benchmark that pairs a dense action space with a hierarchical four-level taxonomy, enabling fine-grained ranking evaluation. Experimental results show that BBCritic-3B, trained without any extra annotation, outperforms 7B-parameter SOTA binary models. It demonstrates strong zero-shot transferability across platforms and tasks, supporting our methodological view: GUI critique is fundamentally a metric-learning problem, not a classification one.

</details>


### [232] [Dynamic Latent Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14323)
*Fangyuan Yu,Xin Su,Amir Abdullah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通用Dijkstra搜索（GDS）证明最优子策略时间组合可恢复全局最优策略，并设计动态潜在路由（DLR）作为语言模型后训练方法，在低数据微调中平均超越监督微调6.6个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究时变奖励MDP中通过子策略时间串联恢复全局最优策略的理论基础，并解决离散潜在变量方法在语言模型微调中表现不如监督微调的问题。

Method: 提出General Dijkstra Search (GDS)理论框架，证明最优子策略时间组合的全局最优性；基于"搜索-选择-更新"原则设计Dynamic Latent Routing (DLR)，在单训练阶段联合学习离散潜在编码、路由策略和模型参数。

Result: 在4个数据集、6个模型上，DLR在低数据微调中匹配或超越监督微调，平均提升+6.6个百分点，而先前的离散潜在基线持续低于SFT。

Conclusion: DLR通过结构化路由行为学习具有不同因果角色的离散潜在编码，验证了GDS理论在语言模型后训练中的有效性。

Abstract: We investigate the temporal concatenation of sub-policies in Markov Decision Processes (MDP) with time-varying reward functions. We introduce General Dijkstra Search (GDS), and prove that globally optimal goal-reaching policies can be recovered through temporal composition of intermediate optimal sub-policies. Motivated by the "search, select, update" principle underlying GDS, we propose Dynamic Latent Routing (DLR), a language-model post-training method that jointly learns discrete latent codes, routing policies, and model parameters through dynamic search in a single training stage. In low-data fine-tuning settings, DLR matches or outperforms supervised fine-tuning across four datasets and six models, achieving a mean gain of +6.6 percentage points, while prior discrete-latent baselines consistently underperform SFT. Mechanistic analyses and targeted code ablations show that DLR learns structured routing behaviors with distinct causal roles.

</details>


### [233] [AIM-DDI: A Model-Agnostic Multimodal Integration Module for Drug-Drug Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14327)
*Yerin Park,Sangseon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出AIM-DDI，一种架构无关的多模态融合模块，将异构药物信息表示为共享潜在空间中的token，通过统一融合模块建模跨模态依赖，实现与多种DDI预测架构的兼容，在DrugBank数据集上显著提升性能，尤其在最难的双方未见设置下效果最强。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DDI多模态模型的融合机制通常与特定预测架构绑定，难以跨模型复用，限制了通用性。

Method: 将结构、化学、语义等异构模态信息表示为共享潜在空间中的token，通过统一的融合模块建模模态间的依赖关系，实现与不同DDI预测架构的兼容集成。

Result: 在多个DDI模型和DrugBank设置上的广泛评估表明，AIM-DDI一致性地提升了预测性能，在双方未见药物（test pair中两种药物均未在训练中出现）的最具挑战性场景下增益最强。

Conclusion: 将多模态融合视为可复用模块而非模型特定的融合组件，是稳健的未见药物DDI预测的有效策略。

Abstract: Drug-drug interaction (DDI) prediction is a critical task in computational biomedicine, as adverse interactions between co-administered drugs can cause severe side effects and clinical risks. A key challenge is unseen-drug generalization, where interactions must be predicted for drugs not observed during training. Although multimodal DDI models exploit diverse drug-related information, their fusion mechanisms are often tied to specific prediction architectures, limiting their reuse across models. To address this, we propose AIM-DDI, an architecture-independent multimodal integration module that represents heterogeneous modality information as tokens in a shared latent space. By modeling dependencies across modality tokens through a unified fusion module, AIM-DDI enables model-agnostic integration of structural, chemical, and semantic drug signals across different DDI prediction architectures. Extensive evaluations across diverse DDI models and DrugBank-based settings show that AIM-DDI consistently improves prediction performance, with the strongest gains under the most challenging both-unseen setting where neither drug in a test pair is observed during training. These results suggest that treating multimodal integration as a reusable module, rather than a model-specific fusion component, is an effective strategy for robust unseen-drug DDI prediction.

</details>


### [234] [RQ-MoE: Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts for Efficient Input-Dependent Vector Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14359)
*Zhengjia Zhong,Shuyan Ke,Zaizhou Lin,Jiaqi Song,Hongyi Lan,Hui Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RQ-MoE框架，通过混合专家机制实现输入依赖的动态码本构建，在保持重构和检索性能的同时实现6-14倍解码加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量量化方法中，多码本方法使用静态码本难以处理异构数据几何分布；而动态量化器如QINCo虽提升表达能力但存在严格顺序依赖导致解码瓶颈。

Method: 提出RQ-MoE框架，采用两级混合专家（MoE）与双流量化相结合，实现输入依赖的码本自适应。通过解耦指令与量化过程，支持并行解码。

Result: 在重构和检索任务上达到最先进或相当性能，解码速度比现有向量量化方法快6-14倍。

Conclusion: RQ-MoE能够恢复标准残差量化和QINCo作为其约束特例，并推导了专家维度的设置准则，验证了动态码本构建在高效向量量化中的有效性。

Abstract: Vector quantization is a fundamental tool for compressing high-dimensional embeddings, yet existing multi-codebook methods rely on static codebooks that limit expressiveness under heterogeneous data geometry. While recent dynamic quantizers like QINCo adapt codebooks to individual inputs and improve expressiveness, their strict sequential dependencies create decoding bottlenecks. We propose Residual Quantization via Mixture of Experts (RQ-MoE), a framework combining a two-level MoE with dual-stream quantization to enable input-dependent codebook adaptation for efficient vector quantization. RQ-MoE enables dynamic codebook construction and decouples instruction from quantization, facilitating parallel decoding. Theoretically, we show that standard Residual Quantization and QINCo can be recovered as constrained special cases of RQ-MoE, and derive a guideline for setting expert dimensionality in RQ-MoE. Extensive experiments show that RQ-MoE achieves state-of-the-art or on-par performance in reconstruction and retrieval, while providing 6x-14x faster decoding than prior vector quantization methods. The implementation is available at https://github.com/KDEGroup/RQ-MoE.

</details>


### [235] [LoMETab: Beyond Rank-1 Ensembles for Tabular Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14365)
*Changryeol Choi,Hyewon Park,Yujin Kwon,Gowun Jeong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LoMETab通过将低秩乘法隐式集成的秩从1扩展到r，并引入初始化尺度σ_init作为多样性控制轴，在保持共享权重结构的同时实现了比传统BatchEnsemble/TabM更大的假设空间和可控的预测多样性，实验证明其性能随数据集在(r, σ_init)网格上变化，是一个可控制的隐式集成家族而非固定秩-1构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前表格学习基准中各类方法性能聚类紧密，难以形成清晰的层次结构，因此需要理解和控制简单神经表格模型竞争力的内在机制，特别是隐式集成方法中多样性的来源和控制方式。

Method: 提出LoMETab，将秩-1的BatchEnsemble/TabM调制推广为秩-r的恒等残差Hadamard族，每个成员权重参数化为W_k = W ⊙ (1 + A_kB_k^⊤)，其中W共享，(A_k,B_k)为成员特异低秩因子。通过适配器秩r和初始化尺度σ_init两个轴控制多样性。

Result: 理论证明r≥2时严格扩大BatchEnsemble假设空间；实验上在分类数据集上LoMETab比加性低秩消融实验维持更高的成对KL散度，(r,σ_init)可在多个数量级范围内控制成对KL；多样性体现在argmax不一致性和回归模糊度等任务适配的输出级度量上；性能在(r,σ_init)网格上呈数据集依赖。

Conclusion: LoMETab作为一个可控制的隐式集成家族，通过秩和初始化尺度两个维度有效调控预测多样性，其性能灵活性优于固定秩-1构造，为理解神经表格模型的竞争力提供了新的可控机制。

Abstract: Recent tabular learning benchmarks increasingly show a tight performance cluster rather than a clear hierarchy among leading methods, spanning gradient boosted decision trees, attention-based architectures, and implicit ensembles such as TabM. As benchmark gains plateau, a complementary goal is to understand and control the mechanisms that make simple neural tabular models competitive. We propose LoMETab, a rank-$r$ generalization of multiplicative implicit ensembles. LoMETab lifts the rank-1 BatchEnsemble/TabM modulation to a rank-$r$ identity-residual Hadamard family by parameterizing each member weight as $W_k = W \odot (1 + A_kB_k^\top)$, where $W$ is shared and $(A_k, B_k)$ are member-specific low-rank factors. This exposes two practical diversity-control axes: the adapter rank $r$ and the initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$, and we prove that for $r \ge 2$ this generalization strictly enlarges BatchEnsemble's hypothesis class. Empirically, we show that this added capacity manifests as measurable predictive diversity after training: on representative classification datasets, LoMETab sustains higher pairwise KL than an additive low-rank ablation, and $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ provides broad control over pairwise KL, varying by up to several orders of magnitude across configurations. The induced diversity is reflected in task-appropriate output-level measures: argmax disagreement for classification and ambiguity for regression, indicating that the control extends beyond pairwise KL to decision- and output-level member variation. Finally, experiments sweeping over adapter rank $r$ and initialization scale $σ_{\mathrm{init}}$ reveal that predictive performance is dataset-dependent over the $(r, σ_{\mathrm{init}})$ grid, supporting LoMETab as a controllable family of implicit ensembles rather than a fixed rank-1 construction.

</details>


### [236] [Optimal Pattern Detection Tree for Symbolic Rule-Based Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14374)
*Young-Chae Hong,Yangho Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出最优模式检测树（OPDT），一种基于混合整数规划的规则学习模型，用于通过二元分类发现数据中的单个最优模式，并引入分支结构约束（BSC）框架以融入领域知识，在适度规模数据集上实现了有最优性保证的模式发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据中的模式发现对医疗、风险评估、机械维护等领域至关重要，而黑盒深度学习模型缺乏可解释性，符号规则发现能生成人类可理解的透明规则，因此需要一种既能保证可解释性又能提供最优性保证的规则发现方法。

Method: 提出OPDT模型，基于新型混合整数规划（MIP）实现二元分类以发现单个最优模式；引入分支结构约束（BSC）框架，允许决策者将领域知识和约束直接编码到模型中；通过最大化覆盖率和最小化误分类导致的假阳性率来优化规则发现。

Result: 计算实验表明，OPDT能在适度规模数据集上、在合理运行时间内，以最优性保证发现模式。

Conclusion: OPDT作为一种基于优化的规则发现方法，结合BSC框架，能够在保证可解释性和透明性的同时，提供有理论保证的最优模式发现，适用于需要领域知识融入的各类应用场景。

Abstract: Pattern discovery in data plays a crucial role across diverse domains, including healthcare, risk assessment, and machinery maintenance. In contrast to black-box deep learning models, symbolic rule discovery emerges as a key data mining task, generating human-interpretable rules that offer both transparency and intuitive explainability. This paper introduces the Optimal Pattern Detection Tree (OPDT), a rule-based machine learning model based on novel mixed-integer programming to discover a single optimal pattern in data through binary classification. To incorporate prior knowledge and compliance requirements, we further introduce the Branching Structure Constraints (BSC) framework, which enables decision makers to encode domain knowledge and constraints directly into the model. This optimization-based approach discovers a hidden underlying pattern in datasets, when it exists, by identifying an optimal rule that maximizes coverage while minimizing the false positive rate due to misclassification. Our computational experiments show that OPDT discovers a pattern with optimality guarantees on moderately sized datasets within reasonable runtime.

</details>


### [237] [Data-Augmented Game Starts for Accelerating Self-Play Exploration in Imperfect Information Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14379)
*JB Lanier,Nathan Monette,Pierre Baldi,Roy Fox*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出数据增强游戏起始状态采样策略(DAGS)，通过利用离线人类演示数据初始化强化学习数据收集的中间状态，加速大规模不完全信息博弈中的在线探索，在固定计算预算下实现更低的可利用度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模不完全信息竞争游戏(如星际争霸、Dota、CS)中，稀疏奖励和长时域探索导致计算不可行，现有方法难以高效找到近似均衡。

Method: 提出DAGS方法：1) 从离线人类演示数据中采样中间状态作为起始状态；2) 在正则化策略梯度方法中使用这些起始状态进行在线探索；3) 引入多任务观测标志来缓解起始状态分布增强可能导致的均衡偏差。

Result: 在合成数据集、两人Kuhn扑克、Goofspiel及反例游戏上的实验表明，在固定计算预算下，DAGS使正则化策略梯度方法在更具挑战性的探索游戏中的可利用度显著降低。

Conclusion: DAGS能有效加速不完全信息博弈的均衡求解，但需注意起始状态分布增强可能导致均衡偏差，多任务观测标志是一种有效的缓解措施。同时发布了新的基准环境。

Abstract: Finding approximate equilibria for large-scale imperfect-information competitive games such as StarCraft, Dota, and CounterStrike remains computationally infeasible due to sparse rewards and challenging exploration over long horizons. In this paper, we propose a multi-agent starting-state sampling strategy designed to substantially accelerate online exploration in regularized policy-gradient game methods for two-player zero-sum (2p0s) games. Motivated by an assumption that offline demonstrations from skilled humans can provide good coverage of high-level strategies relevant to equilibrium play, we propose the initialization of reinforcement learning data collection at intermediate states sampled from offline data to facilitate exploration of strategically relevant subgames. Referring to this method as Data-Augmented Game Starts (DAGS), we perform experiments using synthetic datasets and analytically tractable, long-horizon control variants of two-player Kuhn Poker, Goofspiel, and a counterexample game designed to penalize biased beliefs over hidden information. Under fixed computational budgets, DAGS enables regularized policy gradient methods to achieve lower exploitability in games with significantly more challenging exploration. We show that augmenting starting state distributions when solving imperfect information games can lead to biased equilibria, and we provide a straightforward mitigation to this in the form of multi-task observation flags. Finally, we release a new set of benchmark environments that drastically increase exploration challenges and state counts in existing OpenSpiel games while keeping exploitability measurements analytically tractable.

</details>


### [238] [MahaVar: OOD Detection via Class-wise Mahalanobis Distance Variance under Neural Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14413)
*Donghwan Kim,Hyunsoo Yoon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文观察到ID样本的类间马氏距离方差显著高于OOD样本（锐利最小值结构），基于神经坍缩理论提供理论支撑，并提出MahaVar方法——在Mahalanobis距离中引入类间距离方差项作为OOD检测分数。在OpenOOD v1.5基准上，MahaVar在CIFAR-100和ImageNet上达到SOTA，AUROC和FPR@95均优于现有基于Mahalanobis的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OOD检测方法在区分ID和OOD样本时存在不足，作者通过实验发现ID样本的类间马氏距离呈现高方差的锐利最小值结构，而OOD样本方差较低，希望利用这一差异设计更有效的OOD检测方法，并为其提供理论依据。

Method: 提出MahaVar方法：在传统Mahalanobis距离基础上，引入类间距离方差项作为OOD评分。具体而言，计算样本到每个类别的马氏距离，取最近类距离的同时，衡量所有类间距离的方差。ID样本因神经坍缩特性呈现高方差，OOD样本方差较低，以此区分二者。

Result: 在OpenOOD v1.5基准协议下，MahaVar在CIFAR-100和ImageNet上达到SOTA性能，在所有基准测试中，AUROC和FPR@95指标均一致优于现有基于Mahalanobis的方法。

Conclusion: 马氏距离的类间方差是OOD检测的有效信号，结合神经坍缩理论分析，MahaVar作为轻量级后处理方案具有优越性能，验证了理论发现与实用方法的一致性。

Abstract: Out-of-distribution (OOD) detection is a critical component for ensuring the reliability of deep neural networks in safety-critical applications. In this work, we present a key empirical observation: for in-distribution (ID) samples, class-wise Mahalanobis distances exhibit a pronounced sharp minimum structure, where the distance to the nearest class is small while distances to all other classes remain large, resulting in high variance across classes. In contrast, OOD samples tend to exhibit a less pronounced sharp minimum structure, producing comparatively lower variance across classes. We further provide a theoretical analysis grounding this observation in Neural Collapse geometry: under relaxed Neural Collapse assumptions on within-class compactness and inter-class separation, ID samples are shown to structurally exhibit high class-wise distance variance, offering a theoretical basis for its use as an OOD score. Motivated by this observation and its theoretical backing, we propose MahaVar, a simple and effective post-hoc OOD detector that augments the Mahalanobis distance with a class-wise distance variance term. Following the OpenOOD v1.5 benchmark protocol, MahaVar achieves state-of-the-art performance on CIFAR-100 and ImageNet, with consistent improvements in both AUROC and FPR@95 over existing Mahalanobis-based methods across all benchmarks.

</details>


### [239] [Collaborative Yet Personalized Policy Training: Single-Timescale Federated Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14423)
*Leo Muxing Wang,Pengkun Yang,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个去中心化演员-评论家框架，支持异构环境中多智能体共享线性子空间表示并保留个性化策略组件，建立了单时间尺度马尔可夫采样下的有限时间收敛保证，实验在Hopper-v5上显示优于Single PPO和FedAvg PPO的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有演员-评论家方法要么忽视环境异质性，要么完全放弃个性化采用单一共享策略，缺乏能够在异构环境中既保持共享表示学习又保留个性化策略组件的协作训练框架。

Method: 提出联邦演员-评论家框架，智能体共享公共线性子空间表示并维护个性化局部策略组件；采用单时间尺度更新与马尔可夫采样；通过联合线性近似框架进行收敛分析，发展投影子空间更新和QR分解的扰动分析以及异构马尔可夫噪声的条件混合论证。

Result: 评论家误差以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$收敛到零；策略梯度范数以$\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$收敛到零；在Hopper-v5上优于Single PPO和FedAvg PPO。

Conclusion: 所提框架在异构环境中实现线性加速（关于智能体数量K），具备有限时间收敛保证，并展示出从学习到的共享trunk的下游迁移能力。

Abstract: Despite the popularity of the actor-critic method and the practical needs of collaborative policy training, existing works typically either overlook environmental heterogeneity or give up personalization altogether by training a single shared policy across all agents. We consider a federated actor-critic framework in which agents share a common linear subspace representation while maintaining personalized local policy components, and agents iteratively estimate the common subspace, local critic heads, and local policies (i.e., actors). Under canonical single-timescale updates with Markovian sampling, we establish finite-time convergence via a novel joint linear approximation framework. Specifically, we show that the critic error converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^4\sqrt{TK}))$, and the policy gradient norm converges to zero at the rate of $\tilde{\mathcal{O}}(1/((1-γ)^6\sqrt{TK}))$, where $T$ is the number of rounds, $K$ is the number of agents, and $γ\in (0,1)$ is the discount factor. These results demonstrate linear speedup with respect to the number of agents $K$, despite heterogeneous Markovian trajectories under distinct transition kernels and coupled learning dynamics. To address these challenges, we develop a new perturbation analysis for the projected subspace updates and QR decomposition steps, together with conditional mixing arguments for heterogeneous Markovian noise. Furthermore, to handle the additional complications induced by policy updates and temporal dependence, we establish fine-grained characterizations of the discrepancies between function evaluations under Markovian sampling and under temporally frozen policies. Experiments instantiate the framework within PPO on federated \texttt{Hopper-v5} action-map heterogeneity, showing gains over Single PPO and FedAvg PPO and downstream transfer from the learned shared trunk.

</details>


### [240] [When Answers Stray from Questions: Hallucination Detection via Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14449)
*Siyang Yao,Erhu Feng,Yubin Xia*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QAOD（问答正交分解）框架，通过从答案表示中投影去除问题对齐方向，获得与域无关的正交分量，并利用多样性惩罚Fisher评分和Fisher重要性选择层和神经元。设计了联合探针（正交分量+问题上下文）最大化域内判别性，以及纯正交探针实现鲁棒的跨域迁移。联合探针在域内检测达到最佳AUROC，正交探针在OOD迁移上超越最佳白盒基线最高21%，且推理成本降低75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有幻觉检测方法中，黑盒一致性方法准确但需重复推理，白盒探针效率高但将答案表示孤立处理，在领域漂移下性能急剧下降，需要一种兼顾精度、效率和分布偏移鲁棒性的单次前向检测方法。

Method: 提出QAOD框架：(1) 将答案表示投影到问题方向的正交空间，消除域相关变异；(2) 利用多样性惩罚Fisher评分选择信息层，Fisher重要性筛选判别性神经元；(3) 设计联合探针（正交分量+问题上下文）优化域内判别，纯正交探针保持域不可知的事实信号用于跨域迁移。

Result: 联合探针在所有评估的模型-数据集对上实现最佳域内AUROC；纯正交探针在跨域OOD迁移中表现最强，在BioASQ上超越最佳白盒基线21%，且生成成本仅为黑盒方法的25%以下。

Conclusion: QAOD通过正交分解有效分离了问题相关的域变异与事实性信号，单次前向即可达到甚至超越黑盒一致性方法的检测性能，同时显著提升跨域泛化能力，为高效鲁棒的幻觉检测提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language models (LLMs) requires balancing accu racy, efficiency, and robustness to distribution shift. Black-box consistency methods are effective but demand repeated inference; single-pass white-box probes are effi cient yet treat answer representations in isolation, often degrading sharply under domain shift. We propose QAOD (Question-Answer Orthogonal Decomposition), a single-pass framework that projects away the question-aligned direction from the answer representation to obtain a question-orthogonal component that suppresses domain-conditioned variation. To identify informative signals, QAOD further selects layers via diversity-penalized Fisher scoring and discriminative neurons via Fisher importance. To address both in-domain detection and cross-domain generalization, we design two complementary probing strategies: pairing the or thogonal component with question context yields a joint probe that maximizes in-domain discriminability, while using the orthogonal component alone preserves domain-agnostic factuality signals for robust transfer. QAOD's joint probe achieves the best in-domain AUROC across all evaluated model-dataset pairs, while the orthogonal-only probe delivers the strongest OOD transfer, surpassing the best white-box baseline by up to 21% on BioASQ at under 25% of generation cost.

</details>


### [241] [RxEval: A Prescription-Level Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Medication Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14543)
*Shuhao Chen,Weisen Jiang,Changmiao Wang,Xiaoqing Wu,Xuanren Shi,Yu Zhang,James T. Kwok*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RxEval基准，通过多选问答形式评估LLM在住院场景下进行具体药物-剂量-途径三联体处方推荐的能力，包含1,547道题、16个模型评估，F1范围45.18-77.10，最佳精确匹配仅46.10%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有住院用药推荐基准仅停留在入院级别、使用粗粒度药物编码和多热诊断/程序编码输入，无法反映实际处方过程中每个时间点的丰富信息需求。

Method: 构建RxEval基准，采用多选题形式，每道题包含详细患者画像和时间序列临床轨迹，要求从真实处方和基于推理链扰动生成的患者特异性干扰项中选择正确的药物-剂量-途径三联体。共1,547题，涵盖584名患者、18个诊断类别和969种药物。

Result: 16个LLM评估结果显示F1介于45.18-77.10，最佳精确匹配仅46.10%，表明该基准具有挑战性和区分度。

Conclusion: 即使前沿模型也常忽略已提供的患者信息并无法推导出正确的临床结论，揭示当前LLM在细粒度处方推荐任务上的局限性。

Abstract: Inpatient medication recommendation requires clinicians to repeatedly select specific medications, doses, and routes as a patient's condition evolves. Existing benchmarks formulate this task as admission-level prediction over coarse drug codes with multi-hot diagnostic and procedure code inputs, failing to capture the per-timepoint, information-rich nature of real prescribing. We propose RxEval, a prescription-level benchmark that evaluates LLM prescribing capability by multiple-choice questions: each question presents a detailed patient profile and time-ordered clinical trajectory, requiring selection of specific medication-dose-route triples from real prescriptions and patient-specific distractors generated via reasoning-chain perturbation. RxEval comprises 1,547 questions spanning 584 patients, 18 diagnostic categories, and 969 unique medications. Evaluation of 16 LLMs shows that RxEval is both challenging and discriminative: F1 ranges from 45.18 to 77.10 across models, and the best Exact Match is only 46.10%. Error analysis reveals that even frontier models may overlook stated patient information and fail to derive clinical conclusions.

</details>


### [242] [Efficient Multi-objective Prompt Optimization via Pure-exploration Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14553)
*Donghao Li,Chengshuai Shi,Weijuan Ou,Cong Shen,Jing Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多目标提示选择问题，在帕累托提示集恢复和最佳可行提示识别两种实际场景下，将问题建模为纯探索多目标老虎机框架，提出了一种针对结构化老虎机中最佳可行臂识别的新算法（在线性情况下具有理论保证），实验证明该方法在多个大语言模型上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示工程研究多采用单一指标评估提示性能，但提示效果本质上是多方面的，无法被单一指标全面捕捉。本文旨在填补这一空白，研究多目标提示选择问题。

Method: 将问题建模为纯探索多目标老虎机（pure-exploration multi-objective bandits）框架，针对两种设定（帕累托提示集恢复和最佳可行提示识别）适配已有算法，并针对结构化老虎机中的最佳可行臂识别提出新算法，在线性情况下提供错误识别保证。

Result: 在多个大语言模型上的大量实验表明，基于老虎机的方法相比基线方法取得了显著改进。
